# state.md — What's True Right Now

> **Authority level**: Volatile. This is the only doc that changes weekly.  
> **Audience**: You (to know what's live and what's broken) and Claude Code agents (to avoid building on broken foundations or duplicating what exists).  
> **Maintenance rule**: Update this doc before every merge to main. Use `/ship` or update manually. If a section's "last verified" date is more than 7 days old, treat it as suspect.  
> **Related docs**: system.md covers how the architecture works. intent.md covers what we're building and why. rules.md covers what never changes.

---

## Deployed Features
*Last verified: 2026-06-03*

**Live features at a glance** (audit-only catalog of what's working end-to-end across all surfaces; full ship log below):

- Public marketing landing at `/` (editorial Bloom page); the app lives at `/app`. Contact form → waitlist capture; admin-only "new signups" badges (in-app + admin nav); SEO with generated OG image, robots, sitemap. Middleware now active at `src/middleware.ts`.
- Auth: magic link + Google OAuth, middleware redirect (now enforced via `src/middleware.ts`), session refresh
- Onboarding: pre-auth flow (entry → info screens → seed → anonymous auth), dissolve transition into chat, skip for returning users
- Streaming chat with Jove: SSE, batch rendering, retry on error
- Sliding window: first 2 + last 48 when > 50 messages
- Checkpoint pipeline: Haiku classifier → server-side Sonnet composition (`composeManualEntry`). No inline sentinel — composition always runs server-side after classifier flags a checkpoint.
- Checkpoint cards: inline confirm/reject/refine UI
- Manual building: upsert to `manual_entries`, manual tab with markdown rendering
- Bottom nav: 3 tabs (session, manual, settings), hides on keyboard open
- Session summary: Haiku, fire-and-forget on stale sessions (> 30 min)
- Session history: side drawer, browse/switch past sessions, start new session
- Dev simulate: Settings → "Simulate user" → auto-runs conversation until checkpoint. Persona modes (autistic / ADHD / dyslexic / general) and intake mode (situation / guided-intake / upload) are selectable per-run. Persona can emit `[END]` to stop naturally.
- Dev reset: deletes all user data (not profile/auth) + localStorage clear
- Crisis protocol: detects crisis language, provides 988 + Crisis Text Line
- Voice input (Deepgram): auto-scroll during transcription, 3.5-line max height
- Guest-to-real auth conversion after first checkpoint
- Admin panel: user list (sorted by `last_active`), conversation/message viewer with extraction state, access logging. Tab bar: Users / Beta / Feedback. The Beta tab is one filterable/sortable signup list — status `invited` IS beta access.
- Exploration mode: "Explore with Jove" from manual entries
- Transcript recognition: regex detects pasted transcripts (iMessage, email, journal, timestamped chat), Jove asks for context and cross-references manual.
- PWA: web app manifest, icons, standalone display, service worker (offline fallback, stale-while-revalidate for static assets, network-only for API), update prompt.
- Per-user daily message cap: Postgres-backed, default 200 user-authored messages per UTC day (env-overridable). Server-side gate at `/api/chat`; returns 429 with friendly client message and drops the optimistic user bubble. Skipped on server-triggered openers (message:null). Fails open on Supabase query error.
- Per-row OTP brute-force cap: `phone_numbers.otp_attempts` integer column, capped at `OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5` in `src/lib/phone-otp.ts`. Verify route returns 429 `too_many_attempts` once the cap is hit; increments on every wrong code; resets to 0 on successful verify. Send route resets on every fresh issuance so the legitimate "I typed wrong; send me a new code" recovery path is never locked. Layered with Upstash phone-verify rate limiter (currently failing open in prod) for burst protection if ever provisioned.
- Voice persona picker in Settings: "How Jove talks to you" section in `/settings` lets users change persona modes anytime — same multi-select UX as onboarding (General / Autistic / ADHD / Dyslexic), inline, auto-save on toggle with optimistic update + error revert. Backed by `PATCH /api/user/persona-modes` (auth-gated, validated for shape + exclusivity). Default for new accounts is now `{general}` (flipped from `{autistic}` 2026-05-19 — see ship log entry below). Selecting "Dyslexic" also swaps serif font tokens to sans-serif (DM Sans) app-wide via `[data-persona-dyslexic="true"]` driven by `usePersonaDyslexicFont` — FOUC-safe, cross-tab synced, no new font dependency.

**Ship log** (newest first):

- Extraction-map page — remove dead `next_prompt` field + correct field truth table + naming rebuild (2026-06-03, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): Three changes to the extraction system and its admin map (`/admin/extraction-map`), output of a teaching + audit walkthrough. **(1) Removed the dead `next_prompt` field.** It was write-only: the extraction call produced a 3–6 word placeholder hint, threaded it through the SSE `message_complete` payload (and the upload-opener event) to the client, but the ChatInput placeholder was hardcoded empty — nothing ever rendered it. Distinct from `sage_brief` (the live "BRIEF FOR YOUR NEXT RESPONSE" Jove actually reads), which is preserved. Removed from the `ExtractionState` interface + default, `EXTRACTION_SYSTEM` prompt (JSON example + the 3–6-word rule), the parse + fallback paths, both `call-persona` SSE payloads, the `sse-parser` type, and the extraction-map field card. Test mocks updated. **(2) Corrected the field truth table.** `depth` was tagged auxiliary/prunable but is a **hard checkpoint gate** (`persona-pipeline.ts:567` blocks the checkpoint until depth ≥ `feeling` first / `mechanism` subsequent) — moved into the gate group, marked load-bearing, added the missing pipeline reader. `has_charged_language` was tagged load-bearing but Lock 1 (ADR-043) replaced the boolean gate with a deterministic language-bank read — demoted to auxiliary (its own readers note already said "no longer a gate"). Header counts corrected to **20 fields / 13 load-bearing / 7 auxiliary** (was the self-contradictory "21 / 14 / 7"); dropped the false "charged-language anchor" gate claim and the now-removed "UI placeholder hint" from the card captions. **(3) Naming rebuild.** Dropped the "Layer 0–5" card-title prefixes — they collided with the Manual's five Layers (a reader couldn't tell "Layer 2" the page section from Layer 2 the Manual layer); titles are now plain ("The extraction call", "The brief", "The checkpoint gate", …). Renamed Stage → Step throughout (`Step` type, `STEPS`, `stepIndex`, `StepCaption`, the visible chip, aria-label) to match the prompt-architecture page, which already uses "Step"/"Stepper". Tightened the front-door subtitle into two sentences (what the call writes + the one-turn lag + how to read the cards), removing the same Layer collision; field count stays auto (`FIELDS.length`). **Tests/build.** 1154/1154 pass; typecheck + design-tokens guard + clean `next build` green. **Not browser-verified in-session** (preview worktree-anchored) — eyeball `/admin/extraction-map`. **Commits** `5d8330d` (next_prompt removal), `f1519b0` (truth table), `bd49264` (naming).
- Elevate Strengths to Layer 1 as "My Strengths" (2026-06-03, worktree-misc-bugs-6.3.26 branch): Reordered the five Manual layers so the strengths layer leads as **Layer 1**, retitled **"My Strengths"** with a value-forward description ("Understand the traits that make you exceptional and the environments that bring them out."). The other four shift down one, relative order preserved: Some of My Patterns (2), How I Process Things (3), What Helps (4), How I Show Up with People (5). Implementation renumbers the canonical layer ids in `src/lib/manual/layers.ts` (the single source of truth); every downstream surface — extraction catalog, manual-context compression, UI tiles/eyebrows/Roman numerals/accent colors — re-derives automatically. The strengths layer keeps its stable `where-strong` slug. Two hardcoded references corrected: `extraction.ts`'s per-layer "mechanism" note now resolves What Helps + Strengths **by slug** (reorder-proof), and a `system-prompt.ts` inline example bumped Layer 1→2. **DB migration** (`20260603000000_reorder_strengths_layer1.sql`) atomically remaps the `layer` int on both `manual_entries` and `manual_changelog` (5→1, 1→2, 2→3, 3→4, 4→5) with an inline rollback block — **runs against live entries on deploy**; no-op on a fresh DB. **Tests/build.** 1154/1154 pass; 4 tier-3 prompt snapshots regenerated (diff was only the Layer 1→2 example); clean `next build`; typecheck clean. **Not browser-verified in-session** (preview is worktree-anchored; Manual UI is data-driven from the canonical file and covered by unit tests) — eyeball the Manual tab after deploy to confirm My Strengths renders first. **Commits** `fd6146a`, merge `d22c510`.
- Prompt-architecture page — 3-part "The prompt" overview front door (2026-06-02, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): Added a collapsible overview at the top of the diagram column on `/admin/prompt-architecture` — the plain-language front door the detailed diagram lacked. "The prompt — one call, every turn" with three parts (Instructions / About the user / The conversation), each expanding to its section headers + a one-line description (Tier 1/2/3; the four live-context blocks; the message parts).
