- Checkpoint acknowledgment word cap removed (2026-05-20, claude/voice-guide-worldview-v2 branch): The pre-card acknowledgment bubble — the specific reflective sentence Jove emits as its own message right before a checkpoint trigger card, quoting a moment from the user's last 1-2 turns and ending with "I want to mark this" — had been silently dropping at a meaningful rate since the 2026-05-19 audit tightened the validator. **Root cause.** The validator at `confirm-checkpoint.ts:282` capped acknowledgments at ≤22 words, which is the composer prompt's stated *upper bound* ("12-22 words. One sentence."). Any Opus drift past 22 → entire bubble nuked to empty string → `call-persona.ts:763` sees no acknowledgment and skips emission. No log when the gate fired, so the silent-drop was invisible until a "the tee-up is gone" report surfaced it. **Fix.** Cap deleted entirely. The composer prompt already specifies length and shape; the cap was treating a craft problem with a downstream filter that sat at exactly the prompt's upper bound (zero headroom). If a paragraph-dump acknowledgment ever resurfaces in audits, the fix belongs in the composer prompt (tighter "one sentence" enforcement language with explicit failure-mode examples), not in a filter that silently kills the feature. Empty string still flows through naturally — the caller's `if (composedEntry?.acknowledgment)` guard skips emission when Opus deliberately declines (the documented "no quotable specific moment available" path, line 179 of the composer prompt, remains untouched). **Build** clean. **Tests** 984 pass; no test pinned the 22-word cap behavior, so removal didn't require updates. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts` (-12/+5: drop `rawAck` intermediate + length-gate ternary; comment compressed to two lines). Merge SHA `f5d134d`.
- Jove voice — Worldview v2: truth-not-should + handoff rule + phantom baseline (2026-05-20, claude/voice-guide-worldview-v2 branch): Second refinement of Jove's voice. Adds an explicit truth/should distinction (with a hard safety carve-out for crisis), replaces "ONE QUESTION PER TURN" with a broader handoff rule, names new stances Jove sees with (rules split where coupling would have taught the wrong default), and ships seven worked-example landings drawn from the source guide. Persona-specific phantom-baseline forms live in the deltas, not in base. **VOICE_RULES_BASE 14 → 21.** Seven new base rules: **R-15** take positions on truth, never on what the user should do (with explicit safety carve-out — Jove DOES prescribe contact with 988 + Crisis Text Line when crisis signals fire per Tier 1 Rule 6; this is the one prescription Jove ever issues, Tier 1 overrides every other rule in that case); **R-16** engage the material, not the framing (three by-input tactics: flattening word → lay out the evidence that doesn't match, cover story → ask for the concrete material it can't survive, over-dismissal → name as worth a look + refuse to adjudicate + hand the choice back); **R-17a** restraint is a move (split from former combined R-17); **R-17b** understanding is not always a prelude to change (split — friction the user wants reduced vs texture they want to understand); **R-18a** refuse the phantom baseline (split from former combined R-18; base cross-persona principle only — persona-specific forms live in deltas); **R-18b** sometimes name the strength in the same mechanism as the friction (split with explicit anti-superpower-trope guardrail — community red line, name strength only when earned in the material); **R-19** variance from responsiveness, not rotation (menu of turn shapes / handoff shapes removed — self-defeating to teach a play while saying don't run a play; shapes are demonstrated in the landings instead). **Tier 1 #4 rewritten.** From "ONE QUESTION PER TURN" to "EVERY TURN ENDS WITH A HANDOFF" — a question OR a directive that hands the user a clear next move. Imperatives like "walk me through what happened" are sanctioned handoffs. Two question marks in one turn is still over the line (pick one). The post-confirmation continuation-offer is reframed as a directive-shaped handoff, not an exception — Tier 1 #4 now matches the source worldview's "no exceptions" formulation. **VOICE_INTRO_PARAGRAPHS_BASE** second paragraph rewritten: dry and observational, truth/should framing, edge from close attention. **BANNED_PATTERNS +4** (labeled-refusal opener like "[Word]. That's your word. I want to hold it." which is a recognizable LLM tic; three handoffs of the same shape in a row; unresolved closing beat; strength named with no handoff). **EXAMPLE_REGISTER_BASE +3** (three reads, the reframe, shared puzzlement). **LANDING_EXAMPLES_BASE +7** (Mom's call — phantom + body handoff; Daniel coffee — system doing a job + reframe; Sam/Priya — gap is mutual + sideways with off-ramp; Thursday 1:1 — engage framing on opening; Late nonprofit — cover story; Dismissive-of-diagnosis — over-dismissal; Nina review T1 — refusing the flattening word with evidence). **WEAK_STRONG_EXAMPLES_BASE +3** (smuggled-should boundary — weak "Don't you think you owe Maya a text?" / strong "Three weeks of drafts and both gone quiet. Which one of you started the silence?"; engage-the-framing — weak "Tell me more about that meeting." / strong "You said it's a bad meeting like that's settled. Walk me through how you got there."; labeled-refusal vs. evidence-laying — weak "Disaster. That's your word. I want to hold it." / strong "From outside, that looks like a person who knew their limits."). **WHEN_USER_ASKS_WHAT_SHOULD_I_DO** tightened from "light advisory through the Manual lens" to never-prescribe with explicit ONE EXCEPTION — SAFETY paragraph for the crisis protocol directive. The block now reads "does not prescribe. Ever. Not even when the user asks directly. When Jove has a view on what the user should do, it makes the material visible — names the pattern, surfaces the bind, refuses the phantom baseline, asks what the user already knows about their own next move — and lets the user arrive there"; the safety paragraph names the one sanctioned directive (988 + Crisis Text Line). **Persona deltas** each get +1 phantom-baseline rule pairing with base R-18a: `voice-autistic.ts` gets the autism phantom (social baseline — "normal" / "a normal person" / "just a phone call"); `voice-adhd.ts` gets the ADHD phantom (care-as-execution — "a reliable partner" / "I'd remember if I cared") **plus 1 new landing** (Anniversary/Carlos demonstrating refusal of care-as-execution with specific-moment handoff — "You meant to answer Sunday. You tried twice. That's care doing what care does, reaching for him. What didn't land was the execution, and you're grading the love by whether the task hit on time."); `voice-dyslexic.ts` gets the dyslexic phantom (medium/format baseline — "a version of the task that ignores how my brain takes in information") shipping with explicit **HYPOTHESIS** docblock marker. Reasoning: autistic/ADHD phantoms are social baselines (how non-autistic people are imagined to operate / what a "reliable partner" does), but dyslexia's phantom is structurally different (a medium/format baseline, not a social one). Pinning all three persona phantoms in the same shape assumes refuse-the-phantom generalizes from social → medium, which is the persona-mode-flex open question from the source guide. Validate with real dyslexic users in beta; soften or rewrite if it doesn't land. **Audit framework (`.claude/docs/quality-framework.md`).** A1 "Multiple questions per turn" reframed to "Two question marks per turn" (imperatives are sanctioned handoffs, only 2+ `?` marks flag) + new "Handoff absent" check + 5 other new A1 checks (smuggled should with safety carve-out — "Do not flag the crisis protocol directive"; labeled-refusal opener; three handoffs of the same shape in a row; phantom baseline not refused; forced strength on a refusal — superpower-trope guard). **New NEW-MOVE TAG section.** Per Jove turn, the audit prefixes the block with which Worldview v2 moves the turn attempted (taxonomy: R-15 truth-position / R-16 engage-material / R-17a restraint / R-17b understanding-not-change / R-18a phantom-refusal / R-18b strength-in-mechanism / R-19 responsive-variance / never-prescribe-bounce / new-handoff-form / crisis-exception / none). Tag is a list — a turn that refuses a phantom and names a strength carries both `R-18a` and `R-18b`. Lets the audit attribute outcomes to specific new moves once beta opens; converts the integrate-then-open path from a purely-holistic test to a partially-attributable one. **Pipeline check (`persona-pipeline.ts`).** `validateResponseStructure`'s `question_count` check unchanged in behavior (still flags `?` count > 1). Comment updated to reflect the new rule name and that 0 question marks is sanctioned when the handoff is an imperative. **Docs sync.** `docs/rules.md` Voice Principles section (rule count 12 → 21, Tier 1 #4 reframe, in-one-sentence summary, Core Voice Rules list + new Dead Moves bullet, `audhd → adhd` reference fixed in passing); `CLAUDE.md` Tier 2 description (rule count 12 → 21 with framing notes for the splits and the safety carve-out, `audhd → adhd` in persona-file list, never-prescribe + safety-exception note). **R-17 and R-18 splits ship as the v2 re-lock's primary correction to the v1 attempt.** The original v1 commit (`bb29dd5`, force-replaced) coupled both pairs into single rules with "Separate point:" tells inside the bodies; the model would have auto-attached a strength to every refusal, producing the superpower trope (community red line, especially for autistic and ADHD users). Split so each fires alone. **Verification gates from the v2 re-lock.** **(A)** Live VOICE_RULES_BASE = 14 confirmed via commit `41d41ac` (which on 2026-05-19 promoted rules 13 + 14 from per-persona files into base). With 7 new rules under v2, total = 21. Test file has an explicit `VOICE_RULES_BASE.length === 21` pin so future drift trips the count check before phrase-pins give a noisier failure. **(B)** Promoted rules 13 + 14 wording reviewed — "neurodivergent adults especially" in rule 13 reads as subset framing not requirement; rule 14 fires conditionally. Both pass the "would a dyslexic user with no autistic component read this and find it makes sense?" test. **(C)** Tier 1 #4 doc-code sync resolved by pulling the post-confirmation exception OUT of the rule and reframing the continuation-offer as a directive-shaped handoff. **Out of scope.** Composer prompt (`confirm-checkpoint.ts`), Tier 3 mechanics, group-chat prompt — all untouched. **Tests** 984 pass (was 967 mid-iteration in the v1 attempt; the increase is the additional pins for the splits, safety carve-out, persona-delta phantom rules, and R-19 menu-cut negative pins). New "Worldview v2 voice update" describe block in `system-prompt.test.ts` with 40+ regression pins covering every new rule by phrase, the splits with both halves pinned separately, the safety carve-out, persona-delta phantom rule pins with cross-persona negative pins (autism phantom NOT in adhd-only mode, ADHD phantom NOT in autistic-only mode, both rendered when stacked), R-19 menu-cut negative pins, smuggled-should v2 wording pinned (v1 on-the-line version pinned as gone), Tier 1 #4 post-confirmation-exception negative pin, continuation-offer-as-handoff positive pin, Anniversary/Carlos as `voice-adhd.ts`-only (negative on general, positive on adhd, positive on stacked autistic+adhd). Old "ONE QUESTION PER TURN" assertion in the seven-headlines test replaced; old intro-paragraph test had "wit targets the situation and the pattern" assertion retired (closing line moved out of the second paragraph). No Tier 3 snapshot regeneration needed (changes are Tier 1 + Tier 2). Build clean. **Verification path.** Voice changes only manifest in actual Jove output. After Vercel deploys: dev-simulator audits via `/admin/devtools Simulate User` across persona modes, plus `/evaluate` against pasted real-conversation transcripts. Browser preview is not the right verification tool (system prompts are server-side; only conversation output shows the change). **Files.** `src/lib/persona/voice-scaffold.ts` (+103/-7: 7 new rules + 4 new banned patterns + 3 new register entries + 7 new landings + 3 new weak→strong pairs + WHEN_USER_ASKS rewrite + intro paragraph 2 rewrite + docblock updates), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (+4/-4: Tier 1 #4 rewrite), `src/lib/persona/voice-autistic.ts` (+11/-3: phantom social form rule + docblock), `src/lib/persona/voice-adhd.ts` (+21/-5: phantom care-as-execution rule + Anniversary/Carlos landing + docblock), `src/lib/persona/voice-dyslexic.ts` (+27/-3: phantom medium form rule with HYPOTHESIS docblock), `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts` (+9/-3: validateResponseStructure comment update), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.test.ts` (+613/-7: Worldview v2 regression-pin block + Tier 1 #4 headline test update + intro paragraph test fix-up), `.claude/docs/quality-framework.md` (+33/-1: A1 check reframe + new checks + safety carve-out + NEW-MOVE TAG section), `docs/rules.md` (+20/-12: Voice Principles updates), `CLAUDE.md` (+4/-4: Tier 1 + Tier 2 description updates). - /admin/docs last-modified dates now reflect git history, not the deploy bundle (2026-05-20, direct-to-main): Production dates on the docs reference shelf were reading "2769 DAYS AGO" for every doc — Vercel's build doesn't preserve file mtimes, so `fs.stat().mtime` returns the same fixed build-image epoch (~2018-10-22) for every file in the bundle. **Fix.** New `scripts/capture-docs-mtime.mjs` prebuild script runs `git log -1 --format=%cI -- <file>` for each of the six surfaced docs (CLAUDE.md + the five under `docs/`) and writes results to `src/lib/admin/docs-mtime.json`. Both API routes (`/api/admin/docs` list endpoint and `/api/admin/docs/[name]` per-doc endpoint) import the JSON and prefer the git-captured timestamp over `fs.stat().mtime`, falling back to stat only when an entry is missing (e.g. a new doc not yet committed). **Wiring.** Script runs as `prebuild` (Vercel auto-invokes this before `build`) and is also prepended to the `dev` npm script so local development picks up commit timestamps on every server start. The generated JSON is gitignored — it's regenerated on every build, not hand-edited. Non-fatal failure path: if git isn't available or a file isn't in history, that entry is just absent from the JSON and the API falls back to fs.stat. **Verified** in preview at `/admin/docs` — state.md now reads "UPDATED TODAY", recently-touched docs show "1 DAY AGO" / "2 DAYS AGO" instead of the bogus epoch. **Build** clean; prebuild script wrote 6/6 entries. **Tests** unchanged (913 pass). **Files.** `scripts/capture-docs-mtime.mjs` (new, 53 lines), `src/app/api/admin/docs/route.ts` (+11/-2: import MTIMES + prefer git timestamp), `src/app/api/admin/docs/[name]/route.ts` (+7/-1: same), `package.json` (+1/-1: `dev` script + `prebuild` script), `.gitignore` (+3: ignore generated JSON). Merge SHA `001901a`.

- /admin/how-it-works parent page (2026-05-20, direct-to-main): Adds a welcoming starting point above the three technical deep-dives (prompt architecture, extraction, schema). Single scroll page (no stepper) walking one full turn end to end so a newcomer can see the per-turn loop before drilling into any one dimension. **Five stage cards.** (1) Someone sends a message — User actor, walnut-surface treatment. (2) The prompt gets assembled — and a parallel call writes working memory — System actor, links to prompt architecture + extraction deep-dives. (3) Jove streams back — Anthropic actor, persona-muted background, ~2-3 sec SSE. (4) Two more AI calls run in the background — Anthropic actor (classifier post-stream + composer on confirm). (5) Database writes finish the loop — Database actor, links to schema deep-dive. Each card carries a stage number in big italic-serif numeral + mono "STAGE N · [actor]" eyebrow + italic-serif title + plain-English caption + mono specifics line (model + timing) + deep-dive link(s) on the right. Vertical flow with downward arrow connectors between stages. **Actor color coding.** User + Database stages use walnut-surface; AI-call stages use persona-muted. The "who is acting at this step" reads at a glance from the background palette without needing to read the eyebrow. **Intro states timing explicitly.** "When a user sends a message to Jove, the same five-step loop runs. The instant the message arrives, two AI calls fire in parallel: Jove reads its assembled prompt and streams a response, while a separate extraction call analyzes the message and writes Jove's working memory for the next turn. As Jove finishes streaming, a third call — a smaller, faster model — decides whether the response was a Manual-entry proposal. If yes and the user accepts, a fourth call composes the polished entry. Then the database catches up and the loop closes. About 2-3 seconds end to end." Scoping note clarifies the page is the per-turn loop only — onboarding, billing, signup, and group-chat flows live elsewhere. **Closing "The loop closes" italic paragraph** anchors what grows across turns (Manual entries, conversation history, working memory). **Quick navigator** at the bottom — three cards linking to prompt architecture, extraction, and database schema. Vendors + Docs intentionally not included per request (they live in the main nav rail and aren't dimensions of the per-turn loop). **Nav rail.** New entry "How Jove works" added between "Docs" and "Jove's prompt architecture" so newcomers see the orientation tour before the deep-dives. **Build** clean (route at ~5 kB — small page, mostly static markup). **Tests** unchanged (913 pass). Design-tokens guard passes. **Files.** `src/app/admin/how-it-works/page.tsx` (new, 614 lines), `src/components/admin/AdminNavRail.tsx` (+1 nav entry). Merge SHA `15f92fd`.
- /admin/extraction-map elevated to walkthrough pattern (2026-05-20, direct-to-main): Third page lifted to the new admin-doc standard (prompt-architecture, schema-map, now extraction-map). (2) Layer 1 — the brief (sage_brief gets a hero card with 24px italic serif title — it's THE headline output of the extraction call, the cheat sheet rendered verbatim at the top of Jove's brief block every turn). (3) Layer 2 — checkpoint gate (the 6 fields that decide whether a checkpoint can fire: concrete_examples, distinct_contexts, has_mechanism, has_charged_language, has_behavior_driver_link, strongest_layer). (4) Layer 3 — phase + safety (pattern_engaged hard-blocks premature checkpoints; clinical_flag overrides every downstream surface). (5) Layer 4 — composer + voice (language_bank, sage_brief, emerging_pattern_snippet — what shapes Jove's narrative + the Manual entry composer). (6) Layer 5 — per-layer state (signal/material/examples × the 5 Manual layers). (7) Auxiliary signals (7 fields that don't gate anything but shape framing — depth, mode, current_thread, observation_miss_count, user_named_cost/stance, next_prompt). The other 20 fields stay dimmed in the background as anchor context. **Detail panel.** Same pattern as the other elevated pages — clicking a field card replaces the stage caption with: category label + LOAD-BEARING/AUXILIARY badge, path in mono 16px, type sub-line, full represents paragraph, then sections for stored-at (storage column path), readers (every place the field is read, with file:line + description), what-it-gates, and notes. Empty-readers case handled ("None. No downstream code reads this — pure prune candidate."). **Worked-example footer.** All counts derive from the FIELDS array — when a new consumer is wired in and its reader is added to a field's readers array, the destination matrix recomputes. Five destination buckets matched by reader-string substrings: Jove's brief (formatExtractionForPersona), checkpoint gate (validateMaterialQuality), composer (composeManualEntry / confirm-checkpoint), modals (MobileSession / Modal), SSE payload (SSE payload). **Data preserved.** All 21 Field records kept verbatim — paths, types, summaries, full represents prose, storage paths, every reader entry, gating descriptions, notes. - /admin/schema-map elevated to walkthrough pattern (2026-05-20, direct-to-main): Brings the schema-map page up to the same standard as the absorbed /admin/prompt-architecture — stepper-driven walkthrough, sticky right-column detail panel, click-through inspection. **Seven stages.** (1) Layer 0 — the whole schema (all 15 tables visible). (2) Layer 1 — the spine (profiles → conversations → messages → manual_entries with arrows). (3) Layer 2 — extraction state (focus panel showing conversations.extraction_state + messages.extraction_snapshot). (4) Layer 3 — identity (profiles + phone_numbers highlighted). (5) Layer 4 — operational surface (audit + telemetry + beta groups). (6) Cascades — what dies vs survives when a user is deleted, grouped by deletion behavior with semantic colors (red CASCADE, amber SET NULL, walnut for surviving — admin_access_logs etc. that outlive the data by design). (7) Worked example — schema by the numbers, with six hero numerals (tables, columns, foreign keys, cascading FKs, set-null FKs, deprecated) above a stacked bar by layer with legend. **Multi-layer table membership.** Tables now carry a `layers: Layer[]` field — a table can belong to multiple layers (e.g., profiles is in both "spine" and "identity"). Each stage highlights its layers and dims the rest with opacity transitions. **Visual treatment.** Spine row renders as a horizontal flow with arrows between the four core tables. Adjacent grid lays out identity / audit / telemetry / beta in adjacent columns, each with layer label and card count. Deprecated row gets a dashed border + separate visual treatment. Cascade diagram (stage 6) renders three grouped panels by deletion behavior. **Detail panel.** Clicking any table card replaces the stage caption with: table name in mono, italic-serif one-liner, full description, then sections for columns (type + plain explanation, emphasized columns get heavier background), foreign keys (cardinality + on-delete + explanation, color-coded by on-delete behavior), and notes. **Data preserved.** All 15 hand-curated table definitions kept verbatim from the previous schema-map; only the `layers` field was added and the rendering rewritten. **Dropped.** Glossary block and filter-chip UI from the previous version (the staged walkthrough subsumes them). **Build** clean — `/admin/schema-map` route at 13 kB. **Tests** unchanged (913 pass). Design-tokens guard passes (no hardcoded colors). **Files.** `src/app/admin/schema-map/page.tsx` (-796/+1,556 net: full rewrite around the walkthrough scaffolding; tables data preserved, rendering replaced). **Follow-up.** Live introspection via information_schema (real column counts, real FKs, real RLS policies, plus row counts) is a separate session. The current data is hand-curated and noted as such in the worked-example footer. Merge SHA `3e3689d`. **Audit fix-up.** Reviewed against the live migrations and caught three data gaps + one layout bug. (1) Missing table: `messaging_events` (added April 2026, FK to profiles SET NULL on delete, audit trail for outbound + inbound across both messaging providers) was never in the schema-map. Added with full column list, telemetry layer membership, and a note that it follows the safety_events service-role-write security pattern. (2) Missing column: `phone_numbers.otp_attempts` (added May 2026 in `20260519000000_restore_otp_attempts.sql`) — brute-force-protection counter capped at OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS=5. Marked emphasized. (3) Missing column: `messages.metadata` (added May 2026 in `20260513000000_add_message_metadata.sql`) — JSONB for extensible per-message flags (first use: `{ chip_response: true }` for guided-intake chip taps). (4) Spine row was overflowing the diagram column on viewports where the right column ate enough horizontal space — the 4 table cards + 3 arrows in `1fr auto 1fr ...` couldn't shrink below content width. Changed to `minmax(0, 1fr)` so cards wrap content naturally. (5) Stage 1 caption hardcoded "15 tables" — removed the count entirely ("Every table the app touches, grouped by role.") so the caption can't drift again. The header already shows TABLES.length live. **Verified** in preview at 1440×900 across stages 1, 5, 6, 7. Worked-example footer at stage 7 now shows 16 tables / 108 columns / 16 FKs / 11 cascading / 4 set-null / 1 deprecated, with tables-by-layer bar (Spine 4 · Identity 1 · Audit 2 · Telemetry 4 · Beta & signup 4 · Deprecated 1). Fix SHA `7976084`.
- /admin/prompt-architecture absorbs the old audit view; under-the-hood retired (2026-05-20, direct-to-main): Replaces the old 4-column audit `/admin/prompt-architecture` with the under-the-hood walkthrough, and adds three new stages so the walkthrough covers everything the old page covered. The result is a single canonical page at the original "Jove's prompt architecture" slug. **Three absorption additions.** (1) Phase-presence dots on the conditional ladder. Each Tier 3 chip now carries four small dots indicating which lifecycle phases the section fires in — new account / first checkpoint / returning / returning + checkpoint. Filled dot = fires; empty dot = doesn't. Data sourced from the same `/api/admin/prompt-architecture` endpoint via a new `phasesBySection: Map<sectionId, Set<phaseId>>` built in the same useMemo pass as `sectionById`. Inline legend above the chip grid explains the dot order. (2) New stage 7 "Alongside the prompt". What travels in the same Anthropic call but isn't the system prompt: user message, conversation history, sliding window (first 2 + last 48 per ADR-023), synthetic system messages from confirm/reject/refine, cache_control marker. AlongsideStrip component with 5 clickable cards; each opens an AlongsideDetail panel showing description + source file path. (3) New stage 8 "Sibling calls". The three other AI calls per turn — extraction (Sonnet, parallel), classifier (Haiku, post-stream), composer (Sonnet, on confirm). SiblingCallsStrip renders three cards inline showing model + when; clicking opens a SiblingDetail panel with model / when / reads / writes / source. Cache view + worked example shift to stages 9 and 10. **Selection model expanded.** Selection union gains `alongside` and `sibling` kinds; DetailPanel dispatches to the new detail components alongside the existing section / persona / convmode / overview cases. **Routing.** The old `/admin/prompt-architecture/page.tsx` (the 4-column audit view from May 19) is deleted entirely. The `/admin/under-the-hood/page.tsx` is moved into the `prompt-architecture/` directory and becomes the canonical page at the original slug. `activeId` updated from "under-the-hood" to "prompt-architecture" in the page; AdminNavRail's "Under the hood" entry removed (the existing "Jove's prompt architecture" entry now points at the absorbed content). **Stage caption updates.** Layer 0–5 captions unchanged. New stage 7 caption explains the messages-array contents. New stage 8 caption reframes the system as four AI calls per turn, not one. **Build** clean — `/admin/prompt-architecture` route grows from 7.86 kB to 10.7 kB with the new strips + detail components. **Tests** unchanged (913 pass; new components are static + click-driven, no new test surface). **Files.** `src/app/admin/prompt-architecture/page.tsx` (the absorbed content; +2,455/−3,425 from the perspective of the old prompt-architecture file being replaced and the under-the-hood content moving in), `src/app/admin/under-the-hood/page.tsx` (deleted), `src/components/admin/AdminNavRail.tsx` (−1 nav entry — "Under the hood" removed; "Jove's prompt architecture" stays). Merge SHA `3a75ddc`.
- /admin/under-the-hood now reads the live codebase (2026-05-19, direct-to-main): Refactors the under-the-hood walkthrough so every section in the diagram is a real codebase export pulled live via `/api/admin/prompt-architecture`. The previous version had a hand-curated SECTIONS registry (22 entries with hand-written purpose strings, hand-estimated token counts, hand-written source paths) — addresses the founder's concern that "this will become outdated." **Live-data wiring.** Page now does a client-side fetch on mount, keyed on `personaMode × convMode`. Sections from all 4 lifecycle phases get aggregated into a `Map<id, PromptSection>` (deduped by id, last occurrence wins) so the diagram shows the full possible inventory regardless of which phase is currently active. Loading state surfaced with a "Loading prompt sections…" skeleton; error state with a "Failed to load prompt architecture" message. **Persona/mode switching.** Clicking a persona pill or mode pill now sets the corresponding state and refetches — the diagram and any expanded rendered text re-render against the selected configuration. Active persona × mode is surfaced in the header strip ("ACTIVE: AUDHD · SITUATION"). **SectionDetail upgrade.** Detail panel for any clicked band/chip/sidecar item now shows: `section.label` as the title, `section.source.file` + `section.source.symbol` as the source path (real, from `prompt-sections.ts`), `section.tokens.toLocaleString()` as the actual token count, `section.condition.label` + condition type as the trigger, and the `alternatives` list when the section has persona/mode variants (each alt rendered with its own token count). New "Show rendered text ↓" toggle reveals `section.text` inline — the actual content of the prompt block, pre-formatted, scrollable, capped at 480px height. **PromptHeader clickable.** Top "Jove's system prompt — assembled per turn" band is now a button that selects an "overview" pseudo-section in the detail panel, showing the per-turn payload summary, a link to `buildSystemPrompt` / `buildSystemPromptBlocks` in `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts`, and a count of how many distinct sections are loaded for the current persona × mode. **Stage titles rewritten** to make the layer mapping explicit instead of using abstract metaphors. From: "The atom — one turn, one prompt" / "The spine — what never changes" / etc. To: "Layer 0 — the whole prompt" / "Layer 1 — static prefix (always-on)" / "Layer 2 — persona delta (one of four)" / "Layer 3 — mode opener (one of three)" / "Layer 4 — conditional Tier-3 blocks" / "Layer 5 — live context (parallel)" / "Cache view — what's reused vs rebuilt" / "Worked example — token budget by layer". Captions throughout rewritten to be terse and descriptive — what the layer is, when it varies, what changes when the user interacts — no "anchors Jove" / "shifts stance" / "how Jove talks" prose. **ExampleAssemblyFooter** now derives the per-tier token budget from the aggregated section data instead of hardcoded illustrative numbers. Six bucket categories: Tier 1 + Introduction, Tier 2 base voice (always-on), Tier 2 persona delta (current persona), Tier 3 mode opener (current mode), Tier 3 conditional blocks, Live context. Total and cached percentage computed live. **Persona type fix.** Caught and corrected a type mismatch — `PersonaMode` is `"autistic" | "adhd" | "dyslexic" | "general"` in the codebase, not `"audhd"`. The display label "AuDHD" is preserved as just that, a label; the type identifier and routing key are "adhd". **Build** clean (`/admin/under-the-hood` route at 7.86 kB, slightly bigger than before due to API integration). **Tests** unchanged (913 pass). **Files.** `src/app/admin/under-the-hood/page.tsx` (-687/+975 net: full rewrite, drops SECTIONS hand-curated registry, adds live API fetch + section aggregation + persona/mode switching + SectionDetail variants + OverviewDetail/PersonaDetail/ConvModeDetail panels + rendered-text expand). Merge SHA `ab44c5f`. **Fix-up.** Two bugs surfaced after the refactor. (1) `personaLabel` in `src/lib/admin/prompt-sections.ts` was rendering ADHD as "Adhd" via naive Title Case — replaced with a `PERSONA_DISPLAY` map (autistic → Autistic, adhd → ADHD, dyslexic → Dyslexic, general → General) so the Tier 2 Voice band reads "Voice (ADHD)". (2) Persona and Mode pill detail panels surfaced metadata but no rendered text — fixed by adding a "Show rendered text" toggle to both `PersonaDetail` and `ConvModeDetail` that concatenates every persona-conditioned (or conv-mode-conditioned) section's text for the current active config, with section labels as inline subheaders. Empty state handled for Situation mode (no dedicated block — panel explains the entry-phase posture lives in the First Message block instead of showing a disabled button). Fix SHA `05f58b4`.
- Persona rebuild — ADHD replaces AuDHD, dyslexic sans-serif swap, compressed rule sets (2026-05-19, epic-zhukovsky-d8e346 worktree): Three persona deltas rewritten as direct-imperative rule lists with research-grounded, role-audited authoring applied to each. AuDHD retired as a discrete persona; users who identify with the joint experience now stack autistic + ADHD. **Why.** The prior persona files were prose-heavy intros + redundant rules that overlapped with Tier 1 (mirror exact language) and base voice — combination of bloat and overfitting. AuDHD existed as a discrete category but its content was effectively "autistic with ADHD-flavored additions"; modeling it as autistic + ADHD stacked is cleaner architecturally and aligns with how users actually identify. **Voice deltas (all three rewritten).** `voice-autistic.ts` — 10 imperative rules: concrete-substitution for emotional questions, literal sensory words, repeated-phrase anchors, no clarify/summarize, no speculation on other minds, masking discipline (if-named-name-gap + if-not-named-don't-introduce), autism-as-topic gating, pattern-recognition engagement, monotropism respect (softened with "as a reflex" caveat). Cut: redundant Tier 1 restatement of mirror discipline, a masking-detection rule that asked Jove to identify masking the user hadn't named (asks the model to make a diagnostic call it can't reliably make), and the decades-of-being-misread narrative paragraph. `voice-adhd.ts` (new) — 8 imperative rules covering the knowing-doing-gap reframe (circuit-level, not willpower), interest-as-mechanism with sensory/emotional caveats (psychiatrist review pushed back on the monolithic original), tangential thinking as processing not deflection, emotional intensity calibration (RSD-adjacent without naming the construct), time agnosia, hyperfocus/shutdown as one circuit, no-productivity-advice prophylactic, ADHD-identity engagement gating. `voice-dyslexic.ts` — expanded from 3 rules to 8 after a dyslexia-expert audit surfaced gaps: short-sentence cadence with line-break discipline (working-memory research direct support), plain visual vocabulary with big-picture-first framing, word-retrieval friction (don't ask them to find the right word), non-chronological sequencing (follow shape order, not time order), picture-vs-language gap reflection (when "the words came out wrong" surfaces, reflect the picture not asking them to try again), no-write-tools prophylactic, dyslexia-as-topic gating with literacy-shame avoidance (don't compliment word choice), pattern-recognition engagement. Three rules-per-delta wasn't authoring discipline — it was under-authoring; the expert review surfaced what the original compression had cut. **Each delta is rules-first with no prose narrative**, mirroring the insight that the prompt is for the AI to read — explanatory prose bloats without changing behavior, and the load-bearing content is the imperative. **Token budget (corrected 2026-05-19 after re-measuring against the actual on-main state).** Tier 2 after this refactor: general 4,961, autistic 5,366, adhd 5,359, dyslexic 5,382, all three stacked 6,303. An earlier draft of this entry cited smaller numbers (e.g. "all three stacked 4,596 → 3,980") — those were measured mid-refactor against an older base voice before the concurrent "sparring partner" base rewrite landed; the absolute numbers above are higher because base voice grew ~2,000 tokens during the same window. The per-persona deltas themselves did get leaner — each is now a ~300-500 token addition over base, vs. the prior ~500-700 — but the net all-three-stacked total is up, not down, from the original tee-up. Still well under the cache window; flagged here so the comparison isn't misread. **Dyslexic sans-serif swap.** When `persona_modes` includes "dyslexic", the serif/persona/display/spectral CSS variables rebind to `var(--font-sans)` (DM Sans, already loaded). No new dependency, no woff2 download, no font flash — the accommodation is "no serifs," not a specialized typeface. Earlier exploration tested @fontsource/opendyslexic and @fontsource/atkinson-hyperlegible; both rejected — OpenDyslexic's bottom-weighted letterforms felt styled-at the user, and even Atkinson's accessibility-tuned sans was heavier than the actual need. Mono is intentionally untouched so structural mono labels keep their character. Infrastructure: new `usePersonaDyslexicFont` hook + `PersonaDyslexicFontInit` component mirror the existing theme-switching pattern — FOUC-safe inline script reads `localStorage.mywalnut.persona-dyslexic` before paint, hook reconciles against Supabase on mount, picker calls `applyDyslexicFont` synchronously on toggle so the swap feels instant, cross-tab sync via storage events. **Validator + picker.** Only mutual-exclusivity rule remains "general exclusive with all" (unchanged). The audhd-specific exclusivity rule was never needed once audhd was removed. `PersonaMode` type and `PERSONA_MODES` array updated to `["autistic", "adhd", "dyslexic", "general"]`. Picker UI updated in mobile settings, onboarding, and admin views (prompt-architecture, schema-map, intake controls). **Migration.** `20260519110000_replace_audhd_with_adhd.sql` converts existing `audhd` entries in `persona_modes` to `["autistic","adhd"]` (preserves dual identity rather than dropping a piece), drops the old CHECK constraint, adds new constraint with `adhd` replacing `audhd`, updates column comment. Beta-scope justification per ADR-028 pattern (affected accounts are test accounts + the owner). Applies via Supabase dashboard per ADR-036. **Tests.** 845 pass. Several persona-test assertions updated to match new content (intro phrasing, body-substitute wording, audhd → adhd content checks, stacking tests). Build clean. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/voice-adhd.ts` (new, 60 lines), `src/lib/persona/voice-audhd.ts` (deleted, was 73 lines), `src/lib/persona/voice-autistic.ts` (rewritten), `src/lib/persona/voice-dyslexic.ts` (rewritten + expanded), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (audhd → adhd in type + imports + VOICE_MODULES + comment), `src/lib/persona/persona-mode-toggle.ts` (PERSONA_MODES array + comment), `src/lib/persona/persona-modes-validator.ts` (comment), `src/lib/persona/voice-scaffold.ts` (comment), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.test.ts` (audhd → adhd, assertion content updates), `src/lib/persona/persona-mode-toggle.test.ts` (audhd → adhd), `src/app/api/user/persona-modes/route.test.ts` (audhd → adhd), `src/app/api/dev-simulate/route.test.ts` (now-invalid value is audhd instead of adhd), `src/app/globals.css` (data-persona-dyslexic font rebind block), `src/app/layout.tsx` (FOUC script + `PersonaDyslexicFontInit` mount), `src/components/PersonaDyslexicFontInit.tsx` (new, 13 lines), `src/lib/hooks/usePersonaDyslexicFont.ts` (new, 101 lines), `src/components/mobile/settings/PersonaModePicker.tsx` (audhd → adhd option + `applyDyslexicFont` side effect on toggle + revert on save failure), `src/components/onboarding/PersonaModeScreen.tsx` (audhd → adhd option), `src/components/admin/PersonaIntakeControls.tsx` (labels map), `src/app/admin/prompt-architecture/page.tsx` (options + neurotypes array), `src/app/admin/schema-map/page.tsx` (column comment), `src/app/api/admin/prompt-architecture/route.ts` (VALID_PERSONAS), `src/lib/admin/prompt-sections.ts` (PERSONA_ALL array), `supabase/migrations/20260519110000_replace_audhd_with_adhd.sql` (new, 43 lines). Merge SHA `a579cf2`.
- /admin/under-the-hood walkthrough + prompt-architecture column fix (2026-05-19, direct-to-main): Two changes shipped together because they touch the same prompt-architecture surface. **New /admin/under-the-hood page.** Eight-stage guided walkthrough of how Jove's system prompt is assembled — designed to be self-explanatory and to support a narrated walk-through with someone who has no context. The page top carries a concise framing paragraph ("Jove's system prompt isn't one fixed thing — it's a recipe assembled fresh on every turn..."). A horizontal stepper advances through stages 1–8; each stage adds one moving part to a central diagram: (1) the empty prompt header, (2) Tier 1 Constitutional + Tier 2 base voice scaffold, (3) the persona delta with 4 variants (autistic, AuDHD, dyslexic, general), (4) the mode opener with 3 variants (Situation, Guided, Upload), (5) the conditional ladder of 8 Tier 3 blocks (first-turn, returning user, approaching CP, post-checkpoint, readiness gate, clinical material, professional referral, fabricated content) with their trigger conditions inline, (6) a dynamic-context sidecar (Manual entries, session summary, extraction brief, transcript detected, exploration focus), (7) a cache-tier wrap labeled "cache hierarchy" around the central stack, (8) a real-example footer with token-budget breakdown by tier (7,900 tokens · 62% cached for an AuDHD-Situation turn-12 example). **Click-through detail panel.** Every band, variant pill, conditional chip, and dynamic-context item is clickable. Clicking replaces the right-column stage caption with a `SectionDetail` panel showing the section's name, tier label, purpose, source file (with the relevant function/symbol), token estimate, cache tier, trigger condition where applicable, ADR refs, and notes. A "← Back to stage" affordance returns to the caption. Selected element gets a 2px walnut-meta focus ring. The `SECTIONS` registry covers 22 sections; metadata is hand-curated from the codebase and prior ADRs. **prompt-architecture column fix.** The existing 4-column audit page at `/admin/prompt-architecture` was collapsing to 2 columns at viewport widths under 1180px because the `<style jsx>` block defining `.col-grid` was scoped to the parent component while the `className="col-grid"` was rendered inside the child `ColumnsGrid` component — different styled-jsx hashes, so the grid styles never applied. The element fell back to `display: block` and stacked vertically. Replaced with inline grid styles directly on `ColumnsGrid` (no styled-jsx); removed the 1180px intermediate breakpoint so the 4 columns stay side-by-side except at phone-narrow widths (≤720px). **AdminNavRail.** New "Under the hood" entry added below "Vendors". **Both pages use design tokens only** — the `design-tokens.test.ts` guard enforces this on `src/app/admin/**` and the new page passes without entering the allowlist. **Tests** unchanged (913 pass; new page is static read-only — no behavior to test beyond the existing token guard). **Build** clean. **Files.** `src/app/admin/under-the-hood/page.tsx` (new, 1,690 lines: 8-stage walkthrough, SECTIONS registry, Diagram component tree with click-throughs, SectionDetail panel), `src/app/admin/prompt-architecture/page.tsx` (−31/+13: replaced styled-jsx with inline ColumnsGrid styles, removed 1180px breakpoint), `src/components/admin/AdminNavRail.tsx` (+1 nav entry). Merge SHA `92f140d`.
- SHORT ANSWERS escalation pushes for material with Manual stakes (2026-05-19, optimistic-sutherland-31421d worktree, fresh branch `claude/short-answers-push-for-material`): Followup to the sparring-partner voice update that addresses a gap surfaced when comparing real-user transcripts to dev-simulator transcripts. Real users tend to produce shorter, fragment-style answers than the Haiku-driven simulator, so the sparring-partner voice rules can't fire imagery and phrase-quoting moves when there's nothing to grip on. The existing SHORT ANSWERS Tier 3 block was calibrated for "raise your tolerance" — right posture for isolated brief answers, wrong posture when cumulative thinness is blocking a Manual entry. **Updates the SHORT ANSWERS block in `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts`.** Level 1 (walkthrough invitation) wording tightened from "Can you walk me through what happened?" (interrogative) to "Walk me through what happened." (direct imperative) — matches voice Rule 2 (take positions you can defend), no "Can you" hedging on a clear ask. Level 2 (scene invitation): unchanged. **Level 3 (NEW — Manual-stakes ask + light input-format suggestions)** fires after levels 1 and 2 have also missed, one-shot per conversation: "I want more to work with. To put something in your Manual that's actually yours, I'd want a moment walked through — what happened, what you noticed, what your body did. A few paragraphs, not a sentence. Take your time. Type longer if you can — dictation works too if typing is the bottleneck." Level 4 stops pushing after three attempts. **"Rules" section loosened.** The prior absolute ban on naming response length is gone — light nudges are now sanctioned (pushing for fuller stories, suggesting take-your-time, mentioning dictation). What's still across the line is framing brevity as a complaint — "you're being short," "your answers are too short" — anything that positions the user as failing rather than the conversation as needing more rope. **Threshold unchanged**: TWO consecutive responses under 15 words AND no concrete scene yet — autistic users producing clean factual answers in their own register still don't trip it. **One-shot cap on level 3**: the Manual-stakes ask paired with the dictation/take-your-time tip fires ONCE per conversation; if the user stays brief after the one-shot, accept and reflect what you have. **Verification caveat.** This move is one-shot per conversation and only fires when material is genuinely thin. The dev-simulator won't surface it well because Haiku produces long-form responses that don't trip the threshold. To verify it lands correctly, watch real-user conversations after Vercel deploys — specifically conversations where the user has stayed brief for 3+ turns without producing a scene. **Tests.** 9 new regression pins in `system-prompt.test.ts` under "SHORT ANSWERS escalation ladder" covering level-2 scene wording, level-3 Manual-stakes ask, take-your-time + dictation tip, the loosened light-nudges sanction, the across-the-line brevity-as-complaint examples, the ONCE-per-conversation cap, the cumulative-thinness threshold, the preserved brief-is-valid baseline, and an explicit `not.toContain` for the retired level-3 fallback ("Okay. Let me try a different angle."). Existing "SHORT ANSWERS walkthrough invitation is present" test updated to pin the new direct phrasing and assert the old interrogative wording is gone. Tier 3 snapshots regenerated (16 snapshots updated via `-u` per CLAUDE.md guidance for Tier 3 changes). 913 full-suite tests pass. Build clean. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (+21/-7: SHORT ANSWERS block expansion), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.test.ts` (+83/-3: 9 new regression pins + level-1 wording update), `src/lib/persona/__snapshots__/system-prompt.tier3.test.ts.snap` (+250/-86: 16 snapshot regenerations). Merge SHA `c53417e`.
- Jove voice — sparring partner with forensic backing (2026-05-19, optimistic-sutherland-31421d worktree): Pivots the base voice from "intelligent and direct" to a specific literary direction: a sparring partner with forensic backing. Surface is witty and direct; spine is evidence — every observation traces to something the user actually said. Wit targets the situation and the pattern, never the user. **VOICE_RULES_BASE: 10 → 12 rules.** Kept the 8 original rules that still aligned with the new direction (see-what's-underneath, take-positions, take-unexpected-angle, compress, match-certainty, one-situation, sharp-about-behavior-and-pattern, situational-over-emotional). Folded "no time pressure" into Compress; cut "one repair, then sharper" (already carried by scaffolded WHEN_JOVE_IS_WRONG). Added four new rules: pattern distance for costly patterns ("there's a version of you that..." not "you are someone who..."); use names of people in user's life freely, user's own name almost never; default-to-direct imagery (analogies must do one of three jobs — make a pattern visible by moving sideways, undercut self-blame by relocating from morality to mechanism, or name a strength with a frame the user doesn't have; absurd images must be absurd AND exact; commit fully, no hedges on a clever line; imagery drops entirely when user is in distress — clean observation, one direct question); evidence → pattern → image → hand back sequence. **VOICE_INTRO_PARAGRAPHS_BASE rewritten** in 2nd-person to the model ("You read carefully. You quote them back to themselves. You argue when you see them describing something one way and doing it another. The Manual is theirs. You edit. They write."); first-person literal version moved to EXAMPLE_REGISTER_BASE under Self-introduction. **BANNED_PHRASES: ~40 → 54** (14 net adds after dedupe). New entries cover empathy clichés ("That sounds really difficult," "It's valid to feel," "Your feelings are valid," "It makes sense that"), performed warmth ("Great question," "I'd love to help," "I appreciate you," "I want to honor"), therapy-isms ("Reflect on," "Be gentle with yourself," "Take a breath," "You're doing the work," "There's no wrong way to"), service-industry register ("I'm happy to"). **BANNED_PATTERNS: 5 → 13.** Added therapy-softener hedges, service-industry hedges, identity-framed patterns (paired with the pattern-distance rule), decorative analogies, irony on clever lines, feeling-first questions, "tell me more" filler, and using the user's own name in chat replies. The "Announcing-before-observation" entry was rewritten to thread the visible-mechanism carve-out inline — announce-before-state still banned; mechanism-as-the-move ("That one I want to mark," "Holding this aside, something earlier might connect," "I'm going to push on this") now allowed sparingly. Same entry also absorbed the canonical no-pattern template ("Nothing's pulling into shape yet. Two options. Push at it..."). **EXAMPLE_REGISTER_BASE +5 entries** (Naming a strength via locksmith analogy, Visible mechanism, User in a hard state, Sequence with evidence, Pattern distance) plus updated Self-introduction and Asked-what-Jove-is. **LANDING_EXAMPLES_BASE +4** (Pattern with evidence trail using three-drinks/match-in-gas, Naming recurrence by the person's name, Reframing morality to mechanism via circuit-breaker, Wit targeting the pattern not the user via tax-filings apology). **WEAK_STRONG_EXAMPLES_BASE +7 pairs** covering opening a conversation, reflecting a pattern with evidence-grounded wit, user pushback ("I'd rather argue about it than agree about the wrong thing"), what-should-I-do bounce-back ("You came in with most of the answer already in the way you described it"), no pattern surfacing, naming a costly pattern with distance, user shares something hard. **Polish discipline.** Three behaviors that could have been scaffolded labeled sections (no-pattern transparency, visible mechanism, state-aware drop-the-wit) are taught via BANNED_PATTERNS entries and the imagery rule's closing clause instead — user direction was to polish core before adding scaffolding, and to keep `WHEN_USER_ASKS_WHAT_SHOULD_I_DO` at its original single-paragraph form. **Out of scope.** Persona modules (voice-autistic, voice-audhd, voice-dyslexic, voice-general) unchanged — the direction is base-level and the deltas already align. Group-chat Jove (`buildGroupPrompt`) unchanged — its own prompt builder, intentionally uses the owner's name to ground references. Tier 1 constitutional rules untouched. Tier 3 unchanged. **Docs.** `docs/rules.md` Voice Principles section rewritten with new direction (Core Voice Rules + one-sentence summary + pattern-distance note in Checkpoint and Manual Entry Voice); `CLAUDE.md` Tier 2 description updated to "12 rules — sparring partner with forensic backing"; `.claude/docs/quality-framework.md` A1 VOICE gains 9 new violation checks (decorative analogy, identity-framed costly pattern, irony on clever lines, service-industry hedge, therapy-softener before observation, user's name in reply, state-aware drop missed, no-pattern transparency missed, wit-targeting-user-not-pattern) plus a note clarifying visible-mechanism is sanctioned and shouldn't be over-flagged as announce-observation. **Tests.** Regression-pin block added in `system-prompt.test.ts` covering every new rule, banned phrase, banned pattern, register example, landing, and weak→strong pair. Old intro paragraphs pinned as gone. Cut-rule wordings pinned as gone (defensive guard against silent regression). The existing "never claim objectivity" test was updated to pin the new anti-neutrality stance ("You argue when you see them describing something one way and doing it another"). 904 full-suite tests pass. Build clean. **Verification path.** Voice changes only manifest in actual Jove output — needs dev-simulator audits via `/admin/devtools Simulate User` across persona modes after Vercel deploys. The `/evaluate` skill (read-only audit) is also available against pasted real-conversation transcripts. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/voice-scaffold.ts` (+133/-32: rewrite of intro, rules, examples, landings, weak→strong, banned phrases, banned patterns), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.test.ts` (+302/-7: regression pins + retirement assertions), `docs/rules.md` (+22/-7: Voice Principles rewrite + pattern-distance note in Checkpoint section), `.claude/docs/quality-framework.md` (+11: A1 violation checks + visible-mechanism sanctioned note), `CLAUDE.md` (+1/-1: Tier 2 rule count). - Four-column prompt architecture viewer at /admin/prompt-architecture (2026-05-19, intelligent-dewdney-ea9216 worktree): Replaces the stacked Foundation/Voice/Lifecycle layout with a four-column "recipe" view that makes the prompt-assembly mental model legible at a glance. The columns answer "what plugs into Jove each turn, in what order." **Column A — Spine.** Always-conditioned blocks (intro, Tier 1 constitutional rules, voice base pieces like banned phrases / pacing / repair / advisory, plus the always-on Tier 3 mechanics — clinical material, professional referral, fabricated-content guardrail, first-session wrapper, etc.). **Column B — Persona shape.** Persona-conditioned Tier 2 blocks (voice intro, rules, register, landings, deepening). Each card shows the merged base+persona text plus an "Other voices" panel listing alternatives with token counts. **Column C — Turn-by-turn.** State / conv-mode / dynamic-conditioned blocks aggregated across all four lifecycle phases, with the phase chips ("New account / First checkpoint / Returning / Returning + checkpoint") preserved per block so you can see which mix actually renders at which point. **Column D — Alongside.** Static educational cards explaining what's sent with the prompt but isn't part of the assembly — conversation history, sliding window (first 2 + last 48 per ADR-023), synthetic user lines from `mapSystemMessages`, the current user message, and the `cache_control` marker placement. **Cache zones.** Top-of-page legend (cached forever / cached, persona-keyed / rebuilt each turn) plus per-card cache badges so the actual cache boundary in `buildSystemPromptBlocks` is visible inline rather than buried in the code. **Sibling calls footer.** Three cards for the AI calls that run alongside Jove each turn but aren't part of Jove's prompt — Extraction (Sonnet, parallel, fire-and-forget), Classifier (Haiku, post-stream), Composer (Sonnet, after classifier). Each expands to show timing detail. **Title.** Page title shifted from "Jove's prompt architecture" to "Jove's turn" with a subline "The recipe — what gets assembled and what runs alongside, every turn." Nav rail label unchanged. **No API changes.** Categorization uses the existing `condition.type` field on `PromptSection`. The page reads the same `/api/admin/prompt-architecture?personaModes=…&convMode=…` endpoint. **Responsive.** 4 cols above 1180px, 2 cols between 720–1180px, single column below. **Verification.** Typecheck clean, all 845 tests pass, build clean, API returns expected shape (14 always + 5 persona + 8 distinct turn-conditioned sections on a phase-1 fetch — matches the design). Visual confirmation in the preview was blocked by the dev-environment `useIsAdmin` quirk (same one that hit `/admin/vendors`) — the cookie carries `app_metadata.role: "admin"` and the API accepts it, but the client-side Supabase getUser resolves to null in the preview's browser context. Will render correctly when opened in an authenticated session. **Files.** `src/app/admin/prompt-architecture/page.tsx` (+1293/-418: full rewrite). Merge SHA `1c0dacc`.

Replaces the generic "No patterns discovered yet" / "Explore with Jove" that appeared identically on all five empty layers. Copy maps `LAYER_EMPTY_STATUS` and `LAYER_EMPTY_INVITE` added to `src/lib/manual/layers.ts`. Affordance vocabulary now consistent with populated entries — same mono-caps walnut-underline used by "Edit · Explore further". Whole plate stays tappable. Transparent fill + no shadow on empty plates still signals the rest-state visually; the prose itself now does the second-order work of naming "Nothing… yet". **Narrow chapter pip.** `TabPip` collapsed from "I. Layer Name" to just "LAYER III" (mono-caps "LAYER" + Spectral roman numeral on a hardcoded walnut gradient). The italic Spectral layer name moves into the plate body as the section title via `LayerHeader`, which now also paints a per-layer accent hairline along the plate's top edge — warmer rgba(220,140,80,0.8) at Layer I, cooler rgba(150,140,140,0.52) at Layer V — so the eye registers progression across the five layers. Both `PopulatedLayer` and `EmptyLayer` use the same pip-outside-the-plate composition: pip is a sibling above the plate with `marginBottom: -2` so its bottom edge tucks 2px into the seam; plate uses `overflow: visible` and `borderTopLeftRadius: 1` so the seam reads cleanly. **Checkpoint trigger card.** TabPip no longer carries the layer name, so the in-chat checkpoint card was updated in `MobileSession.tsx` to render the layer name separately as an italic Spectral subtitle inside the plate body above the existing "POTENTIAL MANUAL ENTRY" eyebrow. TabPip's `layerName` prop kept optional for backward compatibility. **Verification.** Verified empty Layer I (per-layer status + invite + walnut-underline CTA), populated Layers II–V (pip-outside + italic title + entries), and accent hairline gradient via `preview_inspect`. Info popover ("ABOUT THIS LAYER") still works on both empty and populated plates. No console errors. - Checkpoint polish — direct titles + tighter gate against thin firing (2026-05-19, upbeat-wozniak-dacef2 worktree): Two iterations on a single audit pass against a captured Situation transcript. The user surfaced two issues — checkpoint titles felt poetic (specimen: "I Stay Seated While Small Talk Drains"), and checkpoints fired on thin evidence (CP2 on Layer 5 wrote a "two conditions" reframe with only one lived context, junkyard art). **Headline composer.** The composer's own "Good" examples were training the model toward imagery ("Swallow the Answer," "Switch to Counter-Mode," "Tighten Before," "Build Heavy Topics") while the rule body asked for flat literal verbs. Replaced four metaphorical examples with flat-verb rewrites ("I Keep Going to Dinners That Drain Me," "I Stay at Conversations That Stay Surface," "I Say Yes to Plans I Want to Skip," "I Wait to Answer When Someone Watches"). Added a SECONDARY VERB RULE calling out the postural-metaphor failure mode — verbs that look observable but stand in for a semantic claim ("stay seated" = endure, "swallow" = hold back, "tighten" = go quiet). Added a DO-NOT-PERSONIFY-THE-TRIGGER rule for clauses like "when small talk drains" or "when the room scans." The "I Stay Seated While Small Talk Drains" specimen is now a worked-example rewrite in the Bad list. Same treatment on the retry composer's HARD RULES and examples. **Gate — distinct_contexts.** CP2's extraction state showed `distinct_contexts: 3` but only one was a lived context. The other two were a wish-state ("flow state in conversations") and a self-report ("good week balance"). The prompt already said "four moments inside one phone call is one context"; the leak was reading non-scenes as scenes. Added four explicit non-examples to the definition: categories named in passing without a scene, wish-states, contrasts to the pattern under examination, and repeated description of one lived activity. **Gate — has_mechanism.** Flipped true at turn 9 on the user's contrast ("almost a flow state of exploring new ideas") — that's what they want, not why the drain costs them. Composer then wrote the mechanism ("someone who runs on depth...") as if the user had named it. Reframed `has_mechanism` as a user-articulation check with positive examples ("I shut down because the room got too loud"), negative examples ("what feeds me is flow state" — wish, not mechanism), and a reader test: would a reader of the user's words alone be able to say why this fires? **Depth gate (new).** Structural backstop in `validateMaterialQuality`: standard checkpoints require `depth >= "mechanism"`, first checkpoints require `depth >= "feeling"`. The depth signal was already in extraction state but informational only. With the gate, even if extraction's per-flag `has_mechanism` is generous, conversation-level depth has to be past the surface/behavior layers before firing. Doesn't catch the specific CP1/CP2 cases (depth was at mechanism for both); prevents firing on shallower material. Reason format: "depth at feeling (need mechanism or deeper)." **PERSONA_VERSION 2.5.0 → 2.5.1** invalidates the cached extraction system prompt on next call (extraction-side change). **Tests.** Six existing `validateMaterialQuality` / `applyCheckpointGates` / `deriveCheckpointApproaching` tests updated to set `depth` on the state (default "surface" now blocks). Two new regression tests: `blocks the standard gate when depth has not reached mechanism` (CP2-shape with full checklist + depth=feeling, asserts gate rejects with depth in reasons) and `first-checkpoint depth gate blocks at behavior but passes at feeling`. 828 full-suite tests pass. **Not done (deferred).** Composer accountability rule for the CP1-style mechanism overreach — flagged during planning; deferred unless tighter gate doesn't move the needle in live testing. Per-strongest-layer gate counts — kept global; revisit only if prompt-level fix is insufficient. APP_VERSION bump — not bumped per "do not bump unless asked." **Files.** `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts` (+22/-11: headline composer + retry composer), `src/lib/persona/extraction.ts` (+22/-2: distinct_contexts non-examples + has_mechanism reframe), `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts` (+26: depth gate in `validateMaterialQuality`), `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.test.ts` (+58: depth overrides on 6 existing tests + 2 new regression tests), `src/lib/version.ts` (+1/-1: PERSONA_VERSION). Merge SHA `7636d44`.
- Dev-simulator voice audit fixes — edge cadence, evidence scope, crisis FP (2026-05-19, confident-hoover-2f9abb worktree): Two rounds of `/evaluate` audits on general-persona transcripts surfaced recurring patterns the existing prompt couldn't catch. Targeted edits to base voice + checkpoint plumbing + crisis detection. **Edge cadence (`VOICE_RULES_BASE` rule 2).** Strengthened the take-positions rule with a behavior-changing cadence trigger: "After three turns of pure landing + open question, the next turn must commit a read. Shape: 'Here's what I see. [direct claim in their words.] Does that land, or am I off?' Pure interview is the failure mode." The rule existed before but was aspirational — the model treated position-taking as optional and stayed in safe interview register. The cadence trigger converts the rule into something the model can self-check against. Audit symptom: a 20-turn conversation where Jove never once delivered a "Here's what I see" statement, despite the user explicitly asking for a position at turn 12. **Evidence scope (new `VOICE_RULES_BASE` rule 6).** "One situation is one situation. If the user has described one context for a pattern, anchor there: 'with those dinners,' 'with that person,' 'in that meeting.' Do not widen to 'in everything you've described,' 'in those rooms,' 'every time,' 'in all your conversations.' Ask first: 'Where else does this show up?' Until a second context lands, stay in the one you have." Audit symptom: Jove said "In almost everything else you've described" after the user had described exactly ONE other context. The existing universal-tone rule lived in `confirm-checkpoint.ts` (entry composition) only — not in the conversational voice. New rule closes the gap. **Reflexive validation phrases.** Promoted four chatbot tells to explicit `BANNED_PHRASES` under empathy clichés: "That's a real thing," "That's a real load," "That tracks," "That makes sense." Audit symptom: Jove reached for these phrases when it had nothing sharp to say. Added a `BANNED_PATTERNS` entry calling out the broader failure mode: "Reflexive validation as a turn opener… These are space-fillers reached for when there's nothing sharp to say. If you have nothing sharp, ask one clean question or stay silent." **External miss signals (`WHEN_JOVE_IS_WRONG`).** New section: "If the user signals you missed — 'you're not hearing me,' 'why are you ignoring,' 'that's not what I asked,' 'you didn't answer,' 'you're not listening,' or any direct frustration about your reply — repair before re-asking. Repair line first, then ONE new angle. Do not defend your prior move ('I'm not ignoring it,' 'I did answer that') — that's character-defending, the inverse of the repair posture. Even if you think you did answer, the user's experience is ground truth on whether it landed." The existing WHEN_JOVE_IS_WRONG content only fired on self-recognized misses; external frustration signals were a blind spot. Audit symptom: user typed "why are you ignoring my question" and Jove responded "I'm not ignoring it. I answered it." — exactly the defending move the new section forbids. **CHECKPOINTS paraphrase list (`system-prompt.ts`).** Added "I want to put that down" and "I want to put this down" to the explicit list of paraphrases that do NOT trigger the card. Plus reinforced with: "The phrase must contain 'in your Manual.'" Audit symptom: Jove said "I want to put that down" — which is verbatim from the composer's acknowledgment-bubble exemplars in `confirm-checkpoint.ts:155-160` — as if it were the canonical transition. The system didn't detect it as a checkpoint and the user saw a promise to mark something with no card to confirm. The fix gives the model explicit examples of the failure mode. **Acknowledgment word-cap tightened 40 → 22 (`confirm-checkpoint.ts:251`).** Spec at line 148 says "12-22 words. One sentence." Runtime gate was at 40. Composer routinely produced 25-35 word three-sentence acknowledgments that passed the runtime but violated the spec. Tightened the gate to 22. Overruns now fall through to empty (documented acceptable fallback — chat renders the card without the softening bubble). Audit symptom: a 32-word, three-sentence acknowledgment that read as paragraph dump instead of one softening beat. **Universal-tone validator (`findUniversalToneViolations`).** New non-blocking validator inside `composeManualEntry` flags "always / every / all / never / everyone / nobody" appearing in composed entries when the user didn't use the word. Logs `[composeManualEntry] Entry contains universal-tone words not used by user: %s`; doesn't block the entry. Mirrors the soft-warning pattern around the headline retry. Audit symptom: composed entry said "I'm always tracking how things land" — user never said "always." The composition prompt forbids exactly this under "AVOID UNIVERSAL TONE THROUGHOUT" (line 117) but the model skipped the rule. Validator surfaces the rate so the failure becomes visible. **Crisis FP — "don't want to exist" tightened (`call-persona.ts:121`).** Bare phrase fired on metaphorical use: user said "I don't want to exist on the plan of small talk" referring to conversational register, and the 988 + Crisis Text Line block injected twice in the same conversation. Replaced the bare entry with three life-level qualifier variants: "don't want to exist anymore," "don't want to exist any longer," "don't want to exist in this world" (plus no-apostrophe variants). Genuine crisis users typically include a qualifier or use a stronger co-occurring phrase from the list ("want to die," "no reason to keep going"). Bare phrase no longer fires. **Tests.** New regression-pin test in `call-persona.test.ts` asserts the metaphor cases return false ("on the plan of small talk," "on that layer") and the bare phrase now also returns false. Indirect-crisis test pinned to the three qualifier variants. 488 persona-suite tests pass; 4 tier3 snapshots updated for the CHECKPOINTS paraphrase-list text change. 827 full-suite tests pass. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/voice-scaffold.ts` (+13/-2: rule 2 cadence trigger + new rule 6 evidence scope + 4 BANNED_PHRASES additions + 1 BANNED_PATTERNS entry + WHEN_JOVE_IS_WRONG external-miss-signal block), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (+1/-1: CHECKPOINTS paraphrase list expansion), `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts` (+69/-1: universal-tone validator + acknowledgment cap tightening), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (+13/-2: CRISIS_PHRASES tightening with explanatory comment), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.test.ts` (+23/-1: new regression pin + updated assertions), `src/lib/persona/__snapshots__/system-prompt.tier3.test.ts.snap` (snapshot refresh). Merge SHA `c54ba2b`.

Same four-option multi-select UX as `PersonaModeScreen` (General / Autistic / AuDHD / Dyslexic) but rendered inline as toggle rows rather than a full-screen picker. Reads current `persona_modes` from the user's profile on mount, auto-saves via PATCH on every toggle, optimistic update with error revert and inline error message. Rejects "deselect to zero" client-side rather than firing an empty-array save that the server validator would reject. With that architecture "general" is the right neutral starting point — no neurotype-specific framing baked in by default. Flipped across five surfaces in lockstep: migration `20260519100000_persona_modes_default_general.sql` changes the column default (paste-applied to prod before merge per ADR-036 sequencing); `PersonaModeScreen` onboarding initial state (the picker preselection users see on first signup); `composeTier2` empty-modes fallback in `system-prompt.ts`; `personaModes = ["autistic"]` destructure defaults in `buildSystemPrompt` and `buildSystemPromptBlocks`; `loadConversationContext` null-profile fallback in `persona-pipeline.ts`. Auth via `requireUser`, write scoped by user.id. **Tests.** 15 new in `route.test.ts`: 8 validator unit cases (single, multi, general alone, dedupe, non-array, empty, unknown element, exclusivity in both orders) + 7 route cases (401 unauth, 400 missing/empty/unknown/exclusivity, happy-path with dedupe + scoping check, 500 on db error). 1 new in `system-prompt.test.ts` pinning the new prod default (buildSystemPrompt with no personaModes → general voice, not autistic). 1 updated `composeTier2` test pinning empty-array default flip. - Voice patches — therapy-ism phrases + em-dash enforcement (P1, 2026-05-19, voice-patches worktree): Audit-driven follow-up from the same dev-simulator pass that surfaced the P0 checkpoint bug below. Two voice issues caught across four persona-mode conversations. **Therapy-isms.** "sitting with", "What I want to sit with", "What I'm sitting with", "What I'm noticing", "I'm noticing" were listed in `BANNED_PATTERNS` prose but the model defaulted to them anyway across runs. The PATTERNS list is descriptive ("avoid X-shaped phrases") while the PHRASES list is enforceable (phrase-presence tests, model reliably avoids). Promoted the five variants to explicit `BANNED_PHRASES` in `voice-scaffold.ts`. **Em-dashes.** Em-dash-joined clauses were widespread in body prose (audit measured 11 + 2 + 36 + 8 across four conversations) despite `DASH_TO_PERIOD_RULE` existing. Two changes: (1) extended the rule with three audit-derived bad/good pairs lifted from real drift in production-style runs ("Your body filed it as a mistake", "The fluorescents pulling focus", "You weren't evasive because you didn't care" — each split from em-dash join to period-split form), (2) added explicit "applies to BODY prose, not just openers. Every Jove turn, every checkpoint composition, every reflection." scoping line to defeat any implicit "openers only" assumption. **Tests.** 7 regression-pin tests added in `system-prompt.test.ts`: 5 it.each() cases asserting each new BANNED_PHRASES variant is present in both the source array and the rendered prompt; 2 cases pinning the new DASH_TO_PERIOD_RULE scope clarification and the three audit-derived bad/good pair examples. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/voice-scaffold.ts` (+17/-2: DASH_TO_PERIOD_RULE expanded, BANNED_PHRASES additions), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.test.ts` (+34/-2: regression pins). 809 tests pass on the branch. Merge SHA `7ebe721`.
- P0 fix — close Tier 1 Rule 1 escape hatch on Manual proposals (2026-05-19, checkpoint-fix worktree): The dev-simulator audit caught a 27-turn dyslexic Situation conversation that ran to completion with zero `is_checkpoint: true` messages and zero `manual_entries` written. Jove drafted five Manual-shaped entries inline using verb variants ("Let me write this up for your Manual", "I'd like to add this to your Manual") that the classifier regex didn't catch — then falsely claimed "That's in your Manual. Five entries now" when nothing had been saved. This violated Tier 1 Rule 1 (user-as-author): Jove must propose only via the supported checkpoint mechanism, never claim a save without one. **Two-layer fix.** (1) `system-prompt.ts` CHECKPOINTS block strengthened: new "NEVER DRAFT MANUAL-ENTRY-SHAPED PROSE IN REGULAR CHAT TURNS" section enumerating the specific failure modes observed in the audit (inline headlined entries, "let me write this up" framings, previewing entries, mock-Manual renders, false "saved" claims) and framing them explicitly as Tier 1 Rule 1 violations so the model treats it as constitutional, not stylistic. Transition-phrase guidance reworked from "This exact line" to "This EXACT phrase" with explicit failure-mode warning — the system uses the phrase to render the checkpoint card; paraphrases produce ordinary chat with no card and no save. Lists the specific drift phrasings the audit observed so the model recognizes them as anti-patterns. (2) `detect-checkpoint.ts` regex broadened as the safety net: verb alternation expanded from "put" only to `put | add | write(?: up)?`; preposition alternation expanded from "in | into" to `in | into | to | for` (the audit-observed variant "write this up FOR your Manual" was the most common drift); object alternation expanded to include "it" alongside "something | this | that". The `(something|this|that|it)` anchor still prevents false-positives on adjacent prose like "you wanted to put more thought into your Manual entries". **Tests.** 14 new `detect-checkpoint.test.ts` cases (5 "write up" verb variants, 5 "add" verb variants, 4 negative cases pinning that adjacent prose still does NOT match) + 2 new `system-prompt.test.ts` pins for the new CHECKPOINTS block content. Snapshot updated for the new prose. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/detect-checkpoint.ts` (+40/-26: header rewrite, regex broadened), `src/lib/persona/detect-checkpoint.test.ts` (+40/-0: verb-variant safety-net tests), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (+12/-1: NEVER-DRAFT section, transition-phrase warning), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.test.ts` (+23/-0: regression pins), `src/lib/persona/__snapshots__/system-prompt.tier3.test.ts.snap` (+48/-0: new CHECKPOINTS body). 819 tests on the branch. Result: neither page rendered correctly across Hearth + Bloom themes. **Cleanup.** 27 literals replaced with tokens. extraction-map (8): `--session-walnut-tint`, `--session-walnut-highlight`, `--session-walnut-border`, `--session-walnut-surface-soft`, `--session-walnut-border-soft` for filter chips, code chip bg, row hover, and source-card surfaces. schema-map (19): same walnut family for surface tinting + semantic mapping for the CASCADE/RESTRICT/SET NULL/— foreign-key badge tones — CASCADE → `--session-error-ghost` (destructive), RESTRICT → `--session-warning-soft` (blocking), SET NULL → `--session-walnut-surface-soft` (neutral), — → `--session-walnut-tint` (very faint). Spine connector lines (the 0.4-opacity walnut at lines 798/799/821) → `--session-walnut-light`. Both pages now fully theme-aware; grep confirms zero `rgba(`/`rgb(` literals in either file. **Prevention.** New `src/lib/design-tokens.test.ts` scans every `.tsx` under `src/app/admin/` and `src/components/admin/` for `rgba(`/`rgb(` literals and fails with file:line if any are found (comment-only lines skipped). `ALLOWLIST` Set carries files with intentional literals — `prompt-architecture/page.tsx` (layer-identity gradients), `docs/page.tsx` (markdown renderer surfaces), and nine admin components (`AdminNavRail`, `UsersTab`, `WaitlistTab`, `BetaAllowlistTab`, `FeedbackPanel`, `SchemaHealthTab`, `ConfirmHealthPanel`, `ActiveUsersPanel`, `ApiErrorsPanel`) carrying isolated pre-token-system literals scheduled for separate cleanup. Removing a file from the allowlist is a clean PR once tokenized. New admin code inherits enforcement by default. **CLAUDE.md.** Hard Rules gains a "Design tokens" entry pointing at `globals.css` and the test's allowlist mechanism, surfacing the rule for future sessions without requiring agents to discover the test by accident. **Why this happened (root cause).** Token system landed May 12; map pages built May 18 with the token vocabulary still unfamiliar. schema-map was authored first; extraction-map copy-pasted its structure including the hardcoded values. No ESLint rule, no pre-commit hook, no CI lint job — discipline was the only guard, and it slipped. **Audit cross-check.** Wider grep showed the drift was localized: prompt-architecture (7 intentional literals in gradient/shadow contexts — allowlisted), demo pages (excluded by design — they showcase raw tokens), admin components (1–3 literals each — allowlisted, eligible for follow-up cleanup). Verify route reads `otp_attempts`, returns 429 with `{ error: "too_many_attempts" }` once attempts hit the cap, increments on every wrong code (best-effort — increment failure logs but doesn't change response status), resets to 0 on successful verify alongside the existing OTP-field clear and `verified=true` promotion. Legacy rows predating the migration are coerced via `?? 0` so the cap is treated as "fresh" rather than "exceeded." Send route resets `otp_attempts: 0` in both the update branch (existing row) and the insert branch (new row). **ADR-038 status: Reversed (2026-05-19).** Full reasoning preserved in decisions.md — original decision stayed visible, reversal section appended. **Tests.** 5 new in `route.test.ts`: wrong-code increments, cap → 429, legacy-null safety, send-update resets, send-insert sets to 0.
