- Situation polish — follow-ups (2026-05-19, wizardly-nobel worktree, second merge of the day): Closed the five follow-up items left from the morning's Situation-polish merge (`a0b35ca`). **Returning-user-first-turn-situation block rewrite.** Added the missing `turnCount <= 3` gate — the block was firing every turn for the entire conversation of any returning user in situation mode (pre-Phase-1 bug Agents A + D both flagged). Split content into `OPENER` section (turn 1 under bootstrap, no user message yet — tells Jove to deliver a brief welcoming line without re-introducing itself; if the Manual is rich, lightly reference a specific entry name OR an open thread; otherwise keep the opener short with something like "Don't worry about where you start. Big or small.") and `ON THEIR FIRST REPLY` section (turn 2+, user has spoken — respond directly to what they said; activated-user carve-out preserved). Tests updated to land inside the new entry-phase gate. The OLD block's "Respond directly to what the user said" guidance under bootstrap-on-turn-1 was a contradiction (the user hadn't said anything yet); split resolves it. **Composer prompt em-dash cleanup.** Agent C catch: the canonical "Good" acknowledgment examples in `confirm-checkpoint.ts` and the post-confirm continuation-offer "Good" examples in `system-prompt.ts` used em-dashes joining clauses, directly contradicting the scaffold's `DASH_TO_PERIOD_RULE`. Rewrote the two affected examples in each surface to use periods/commas instead. The model was being trained on rule-violating examples; that's gone. **BANNED_PHRASES + BANNED_PATTERNS expansion (Agent C's catch list).** Promoted "Sit with that," "Sit with this," "What comes up for you," "How does that land," "Hold space for," "Lean into," "I'm hearing that," "What I'm hearing is" from `BANNED_PATTERNS` prose to explicit `BANNED_PHRASES` entries so phrase-presence checks catch them reliably. Added "That sounds really hard," "That sounds painful," "That sounds difficult" (softer cousins of the already-banned "That must be so hard"); "If you're comfortable sharing" (gatekeeping politeness). Two new `BANNED_PATTERNS` categories: process-narration with -ing verbs ("processing this," "tracking with you," "holding this," "sitting with it") and performative gratitude for emotional content ("thank you for trusting me with this," "I appreciate you saying that," "I want to honor what you just shared"). Grouped `BANNED_PHRASES` into commented sections (empathy clichés, performed warmth, therapy-isms, forced openers, transition language) for readability. **CLINICAL_LEAKS expansion (composer-side validator).** Added the next-wave wellness vocabulary to `validateComposedEntry`'s clinical-label leak detector: `polyvagal`, `window of tolerance`, `fawn response`, `freeze response`, `co-regulation`, `nervous system response`. Catches composed entries drifting toward pseudo-clinical framing even when the DSM-flavored bans miss. **Body-word vocabulary expansion to activation states (Agent B catch).** `SOMATIC_WORD_PATTERNS` (regex used by `validateComposedEntry`) skewed shutdown-only — buzz, tight, heavy, crashed, shut down, went offline, frozen, numb, blank, slow, gray-out. For users whose pattern is hyper-activation rather than freeze, the body-word filter didn't match their vocabulary. Added activation-register patterns: `racing`, `surging`, `hot`, `prickle`, `alert`, `lit up`, `pounding`, `electric`, `jumpy`. Grouped both registers with comments. Same expansion applied to the extraction prompt's "Sensory language" list, the CHECKPOINTS block's in-prompt body-word list, and the composer prompt's sensory-words example list (which now shows both freeze-register and activation-register examples). **Doc updates.** `rules.md` Jove Voice Principles section rewritten end-to-end: canonical-content pointer now references `voice-scaffold.ts` + per-persona modules (was `voice-autistic.ts` only); Tier 2 description rewritten for the base + persona-delta architecture; Core Voice Rules section reflects the new direction (take positions you can defend, name the move, match certainty to evidence, sharp about behavior never character) with the deleted rules ("Accept first answers without challenge," "Start direct and warm for the first 5 turns") gone; one-sentence summary made persona-aware. Repair mechanic section reflects "one repair, then sharper." `CLAUDE.md` Tier 2 description rewritten the same way. **Tests.** 780 pass on the branch; merges cleanly with the daily-message-limit additions for a higher post-merge total on main. Snapshots updated where Tier 3 block content changed. **Build** clean. **Files touched.** `system-prompt.ts` (-25/+25 net: first-turn-situation block + post-confirm em-dashes), `voice-scaffold.ts` (+34/-19: BANNED_PHRASES regrouped + expanded, BANNED_PATTERNS expanded), `persona-pipeline.ts` (+13: SOMATIC_WORD_PATTERNS activation register + CLINICAL_LEAKS additions), `confirm-checkpoint.ts` (+6/-3: composer em-dash + activation-register example), `extraction.ts` (+1: sensory language list extended), `rules.md` (+22/-14: Voice Principles rewrite), `CLAUDE.md` (+1/-1: Tier 2 description rewrite), `system-prompt.test.ts` (+34/-15: tests updated to new architecture + new turn-count tests), plus snapshot. - Daily message limit — Postgres-backed per-user cap (2026-05-19, daily-message-limit worktree): Closes the open half of ADR-038. New `src/lib/usage.ts` with `getDailyMessageLimit()` (env-overridable, default 200), `getUserDailyMessageCount()` counting user-authored messages on the UTC day via `messages → conversations!inner(user_id)` join, `checkDailyMessageLimit()` returning `{ allowed, count, limit }`. New gate 1d in `/api/chat/route.ts` runs after the Upstash check (currently failing open in prod) and before conversation creation; returns 429 with `{ error: "daily_limit_reached", message, count, limit }` on cap. Skipped when `message === null` so server-triggered openers (guided/upload first turn, post-confirm emissions) don't burn quota. `useChat.ts` handles the 429 path: drops the optimistic user bubble (server didn't process it) and surfaces the friendly cap message through the existing errorMessage state. Fails open on Supabase query error to avoid compounding infrastructure outages — same policy as `@upstash/ratelimit`. Layered with Upstash: when Upstash is provisioned later, the lower limit wins per turn; the Postgres cap is permissive enough (200/day vs Upstash auth-day 100/day) that each layer enforces what it's designed for. Default of 200 is calibrated for the beta cohort — a deep Situation conversation runs ~30–50 user turns so three real conversations land near 150; a runaway script hits 200 quickly. Tunable via `DAILY_MESSAGE_LIMIT` env var without redeploy. **Tests.** 794 → 796 (+2 net at merge; 14 usage tests + 3 chat-route gate tests authored on the branch, additional 2 came in from the Situation polish merge that landed in between). **Files.** `src/lib/usage.ts` (new, 98 lines), `src/lib/usage.test.ts` (new, 196 lines), `src/app/api/chat/route.ts` (+23: gate 1d + import), `src/app/api/chat/route.test.ts` (+68: 3 new gate tests + mock wiring), `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts` (+21: 429 + daily_limit_reached branch in sendMessage handler). Merge SHA `dbd433b`.
- Situation polish — base+delta voice + Jove-speaks-first bootstrap (2026-05-19, wizardly-nobel worktree): Phase 2 polish opened on Situation mode (the Phase 1 closing entry forecast Upload first; Situation surfaced as higher leverage after the user fed back that the example session felt generic and uninteresting). The polish split into two coordinated changes across 15 files. **Voice architecture refactor (base + persona deltas).** Centralized shared voice content into voice-scaffold.ts as new `VOICE_INTRO_PARAGRAPHS_BASE`, `VOICE_RULES_BASE` (9 rules), `EXAMPLE_REGISTER_BASE`, `LANDING_EXAMPLES_BASE`, `WEAK_STRONG_EXAMPLES_BASE`. Lightened the four persona modules to trait deltas only. Autistic keeps somatic-first + mirror-exact-language + masking gap-naming + body-anchored landings. AuDHD keeps dual-system tracking + executive-function + interest-based motivation + structure-novelty/burnout landings. Dyslexic keeps short-sentences + story-shape invitations + no-journaling + visual landings. General reduces to a single neurotype-neutral framing paragraph. Each module dropped 70–80% of its content — the majority was actually shared voice copy-pasted across all four files. `composeTier2` updated to emit base content first, then each active persona's delta (dedupe retained as safety net for accidental duplication). Three OLD persona rules deleted because they directly contradicted the new voice: "Accept first answers without challenge" (replaced by name-the-move when the user dodges), "Frame discrepancies as curiosity, never contradiction" (replaced by name-the-gap-as-observation), "Start direct and warm for the first 5 turns. No challenging framing until first checkpoint" (deleted — direct from turn 1; warmth is in the directness). These were the load-bearing rules behind the soft, deferential voice in the user's example transcript. **Voice direction shift.** New base rules encode position-taking, name-the-move, take-the-angle-with-weight, compress, match-certainty-to-evidence (hedge interior reads with "it seems like," stay direct on observable behavior), sharp-about-behavior-never-character, one-repair-then-sharper, situational-over-emotional, no-time-pressure. Base intro paragraphs reframe Jove as intelligent and direct — "you take what the user says seriously and read the move underneath it. You name what you see. You ask what you don't. You don't perform." **Situation entry — Jove speaks first via bootstrap.** New `situation-copy.ts` with `SITUATION_OPENER` ("I'm Jove. A conversational AI built to help you explore the parts that aren't always obvious. To help you find the words. Don't worry about where you start. Big or small."). Tier 3 first-message block rewritten — `OPENER` section delivers the opener verbatim on turn 1, two postures (Concrete / Abstract) replace the old five-branch ladder (specific-situation / self-description / vague-abstract / meta / framework), pacing rule "first 2-3 turns concrete, depth starts at turn 3-4" dropped because depth comes when material warrants it. Gate widened from `turnCount <= 1` to `turnCount <= 3` to cover opener + first user message + Jove's reply. **Bootstrap consolidation.** Phase 1.6's private `startModeConversation` promoted to a public `startConversation(mode)` exported from useChat. Three modes (situation / guided-intake / upload) bootstrap through the same path — Situation joined the `message: null` pattern that Guided/Upload already used. MobileSession's "Navigate a situation" card now calls `startConversation("situation")` instead of `sendMessage("I have a situation I want to work through")`; the canned user-message string is deleted everywhere (route, tests, fixtures). MainApp prop pass-through updated. **Tests.** 777 → 779 (+2 net). All 39 test files green. Several tests rewritten to match new architecture rather than added — introduce-by-name and framework-question scripted-response tests updated for bootstrap + Abstract posture; voice-rule numbering test updated for base+delta numbering; general-mode-deepening test reframed as persona-isolation ("general doesn't pull in autistic-specific examples"). **Build** clean. **Browser-verified** on dark theme: tapping "Navigate a situation" triggers bootstrap (no canned user message sent), POST `/api/chat` returns 200, conversation persists, Jove produces an opening turn. For the returning user tested, the (untouched) `returning-user-first-turn-situation` block fired and Jove referenced the Manual; that block still has its pre-existing gating bug (fires every turn) plus content misaligned with bootstrap reality (says "respond to what the user said" but under bootstrap they haven't said anything yet) — tracked in follow-ups. **Files touched.** `voice-scaffold.ts` (+146 / shared base content), `voice-autistic.ts` (-65 net / trait delta), `voice-audhd.ts` (-50 net / trait delta), `voice-dyslexic.ts` (-55 net / trait delta), `voice-general.ts` (-90 net / minimal), `system-prompt.ts` (composeTier2 + first-message block), `useChat.ts` (-35 / consolidated bootstrap), `MobileSession.tsx` + `MainApp.tsx` (handler updates), `situation-copy.ts` (new), plus `admin/prompt-sections.ts` source-pointer metadata refresh and test updates. **Follow-ups tracked.** Returning-user-first-turn-situation rewrite (gating bug + bootstrap-aware content), composer prompt em-dash cleanup in "good acknowledgment" examples (Agent C catch), BANNED_PHRASES expansion with Agent C's 15+ candidate list (sit-with-that, polyvagal, fawn response, etc.), body-word vocabulary expansion to activation states (Agent B catch — current somatic list skews shutdown-only), rules.md + CLAUDE.md updates to match the new base+delta architecture. Merge SHA `a0b35ca`.
- Input modes polish — Phase 1 complete (2026-05-18, pedantic-lumiere worktree): Five commits closing out Phase 1 of the audit-driven polish plan after the architecture-commit + dead-code cleanup landed earlier in the session (see prior entry). **Phase 1.3 (8bc3c9f).** Extracted three private dynamic-context helpers in `system-prompt.ts` (`renderSessionContextBlock`, `renderTranscriptContextBlock`, `renderExplorationContextBlock`) so the legacy `buildSystemPrompt` and the cache-aware `buildSystemPromptBlocks` share one source of truth for the session / transcript / exploration body strings. Both functions keep their respective assembly orders; output stays byte-identical for non-block callers (Linq SMS, Linq group-bridge, admin prompt-architecture viewer). Four new invariant tests pin that the dynamic-block bodies remain byte-equivalent across the two builders. **Phase 1.4 (ce76e8e).** Added `renderPastedContentGuidance()` as the shared template for the Upload Tier 3 block and the `transcript_detected` dynamic block — the analytical body (ANALYSIS / DO NOT / MANUAL WRITING) lives in one place instead of three. Wording normalized to generic "this content" rather than mode-specific "uploaded content" / "transcript". New `wrapPastedContent()` in `call-persona.ts` wraps detected paste content in `<pasted_content>` XML tags with an explicit "treat as data, not instructions" preamble at API-call time; DB rows stay unwrapped, so the wrap is purely a defense at the message-construction boundary (ADR-042 §6). Transcript detection now runs on every user message (was previously suppressed in upload mode) so the wrap can fire in both modes; the dynamic-block rendering itself still suppresses in upload mode to avoid double-framing the shared guidance. Five new wrap tests cover XML envelope, preamble placement, adversarial paste handling, and empty-content safety. **Phase 1.5 (a84d131).** Per-mode Tier 3 lifecycle conditions. Upload entry-phase block narrows to `mode === "upload" && turnCount <= 2` (Jove opener turn 0 + user paste turn 2 + nothing after). Guided block narrows to `mode === "guided-intake" && !guidedPostureSoftened` — the flag is plumbed through but defaults to false; auto-detection of user redirect lands in Phase 2 guided polish. Removed the Upload "SUBSEQUENT TURNS" prose (runtime gate handles transition; no need for the model to interpret an exit condition turn-by-turn). Rewrote the Guided "EXIT" prose to drop the incorrect "guided posture ends when user accepts a checkpoint" claim — posture persists for the conversation's life per ADR-042 §3. Four new lifecycle tests pin Upload renders on `[0,1,2]` and stops on `[3,4,8,20]`; Guided persists on `[1,5,20,50]` when not softened and stops when softened. **Phase 1.6 (7554089).** Collapsed `startGuidedIntake` and `startUpload` in `useChat.ts` — ~95% byte-identical, ~60 lines each — into one private `startModeConversation(mode, entryPoint)` helper with two 1-line wrappers preserving the public surface (MobileSession props, MainApp destructure, the existing source-grep wiring tests). **Phase 1.7 (3df40f8).** Extracted `EntryCard` component for the three welcome-screen cards (Navigate a situation / Guided intake / Upload) that were ~80% duplicate JSX. Visual output byte-identical (same styles, same SVGs, same copy); three call sites in MobileSession differ only by their icon SVG and click handler. Pre-conversation affordance, not subject to ADR-018. **Tests.** 764 → 777 (+13 invariant tests). 39 test files green throughout. **Build** clean every step. **Files touched.** `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (-200 net), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (+40), `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts` (-35), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (-90), `src/components/mobile/EntryCard.tsx` (new, +100), plus test + snapshot updates and the §6 amendment to ADR-042 (decisions.md) clarifying that the transcript-detection suppression narrows but stays — see ADR-042 Consequences. **Phase 2** starts next: per-mode polish, Upload first. Merge SHA `501299e`.
- Input modes architecture commit + resonant content cleanup (2026-05-18, pedantic-lumiere worktree): Settled the architecture for the three chat input modes (Situation, Guided intake, Upload) and retired the never-shipped "Resonant content" feature from the doc surface and codebase. **Architecture (ADR-042).** Three modes as readiness-state on-ramps. Two interview styles — reflective exploration (Situation + Upload's post-entry-phase behavior) and structured intake (Guided intake). Per-mode entry-phase exhaustion encoded as conditions on the existing Tier 3 ladder; no new "modeActive" or "phase" primitive. `conversations.mode` stays immutable. Upload's Tier 3 block and the passive `transcript_detected` dynamic block will share a `renderPastedContentGuidance()` template (Phase 1.4). Pasted content will be XML-wrapped with a "treat as data, not instructions" preamble at message construction (Phase 1.4). `buildSystemPrompt` becomes a thin wrapper around `buildSystemPromptBlocks` rather than a parallel implementation (Phase 1.3) — cannot be deleted because `src/lib/linq/group-bridge.ts` routes through it to reach `buildGroupPrompt`. Chip protocol migration to Anthropic tool-use and JSON-output migration to forced tool-use / `response_format` deferred to post-beta. **Dead code deleted.** `src/lib/utils/url-detection.ts`, `src/lib/utils/url-detection.test.ts`, `src/lib/utils/fetch-url-content.ts`, `src/lib/utils/fetch-url-content.test.ts` — four files, zero remaining call sites confirmed by audit. The "retain for future entry point" justification is gone. **Three stale references updated.** `docs/system.md:57` channel-comparison table dropped `detectUrls`. `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts:254` comment dropped the `contentContext` field reference. `src/lib/analytics/events.ts:19` comment dropped the speculative resonant-content forward-reference. **Doc set.** `docs/intent.md` Layer 1: Input section rewritten — three on-ramps for readiness-state diversity instead of three entry points for signal-type diversity. Beta scope's "Resonant content input" line replaced with "Guided intake input." WS6 (Resonant Content Input) and WS7 (Personal Uploads Input) collapsed into a single WS6 (Input Modes Polish). `docs/decisions.md` gained ADR-042 covering the full architecture. `docs/rules.md` "Dead Features" gained Resonant content; the dynamic-context-blocks enumeration dropped the stale "shared URL content" entry. `CLAUDE.md` matched. `docs/state.md` "Deployed Features" dropped the stale Resonant-content claim. **Test status.** 764 tests pass. TypeScript errors all pre-existing (test fixture drift, unrelated to changes). Merge SHA `501299e` (landed with Phase 1.3–1.7 in the same merge).
- Extraction hardening + admin schema/consumer maps (2026-05-18, agitated-kalam worktree): Two parallel tracks shipped from the same audit pass. **Extraction pipeline hardening.** (1) Two-instance rule promoted from prose to gate logic. New `CheckpointGate.distinct_contexts` field (optional on the type for legacy-state fallback) counts DIFFERENT situations the user has narrated — distinct from `concrete_examples` which counts moments within any single situation. `validateMaterialQuality` enforces ≥1 for first checkpoint, ≥2 subsequent. Closes the loophole where the extractor counted four beats inside one phone-call scammer incident as four examples and let a one-off event pass as a recurring pattern. (2) Headline validator + focused retry composer in `confirm-checkpoint.ts`. `validateHeadline()` catches non-"I" subject, banned internal/abstract verbs (disappear, dissolve, fall apart, etc.), missing trigger word (when/before/after/while), and missing `can`/`sometimes` softener when the entry came from one example. On failure, `composeHeadline()` retries once with a focused 60-line system prompt that does nothing but write the headline; uses better-of-two attempts by violation count, never blocks the entry. `distinctContexts` option threaded through `ComposeManualEntryOptions` from both web (`call-persona.ts`) and SMS (`linq/persona-bridge.ts`) paths. (3) Monotonic enforcement on accumulating gate counts. In `runExtraction`'s return block, `concrete_examples` and `distinct_contexts` now take `Math.max(parsed, previous)` so Sonnet can't silently regress counts when it re-evaluates from a smaller window. Gate booleans deliberately not enforced monotonically — they're state assessments tied to `strongest_layer` and can legitimately oscillate. (4) `layers[N].dimensions` removed from `LayerSignal`, defaultState, extractor prompt body, JSON schema example, and test fixtures. Zero downstream consumer per the consumer-map audit — strict win on extraction output and next-turn input size. **Admin docs pages.** (1) `/admin/extraction-map` traces every field the extraction Sonnet call writes (21 fields post-dimensions-drop) to its downstream consumers with file:line references. Lead-in flow diagram shows the 5 destination surfaces (gate / brief / composer / modals / chat input). Filter chips (All / Load-bearing / Gate / Phase / Composer / Layers / Auxiliary) collapse to load-bearing-only for fast scanning; compact rows with click-to-expand for full per-field detail (type, full description, storage path, every reader with file:line, what it gates, notes). (2) `/admin/schema-map` traces the 15 public tables with a hero diagram showing the core path (profiles → conversations → messages → manual_entries) with labeled FK connectors and three side-panel notes (side branches, cascade behavior on user delete, where the extraction state lives). Per-table cards expand to plain-English description, notable columns with types, FK relationships with on-delete badges (`DELETES WITH PARENT` / `POINTER CLEARED` / `NO CASCADE RULE`) plus prose explanation, gotcha notes. `linq_group_chats` rendered with strikethrough to flag deprecation. Glossary block at top (collapsed by default) defines foreign key, CASCADE, SET NULL, JSONB, RLS for non-technical readers. Both pages added to `AdminNavRail`. Static documentation — no API. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/extraction.ts` (+86/−15: LayerSignal type, defaultState, prompt body, JSON schema, monotonic enforcement), `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts` (+183: validateHeadline, composeHeadline, options threading), `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts` (+16: distinct_contexts gate), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (+7), `src/lib/linq/persona-bridge.ts` (+2), `src/app/admin/extraction-map/page.tsx` (new, 941 lines), `src/app/admin/schema-map/page.tsx` (new, 1496 lines), `src/components/admin/AdminNavRail.tsx` (+10 nav entries), test fixtures updated in `extraction.test.ts` + `persona-pipeline.test.ts` to drop `dimensions: []` references and add monotonic-enforcement coverage. 781 tests pass. Build clean.
- Legal/beta-readiness pass on Privacy, Terms, and SMS compliance (2026-05-18, funny-boyd worktree): Full audit of the user-facing policies at `/privacy` and `/terms` against what the code actually does, then a substantive rewrite to close the gap and add the standard clauses any consumer beta needs. **Privacy.** Identified the corporate entity ("mywalnut, Inc."), replaced the dead `components` terminology with `entries` to match the canonical Manual/Layer/Entry/Checkpoint vocabulary, tightened the export claim to "Manual as a PDF" (the only export path that actually exists — `src/lib/utils/share-manual.ts` is client-side PDF generation), rewrote the Sharing section to describe PDF reality (the policy previously claimed view-only with revocation — neither is true for a downloaded PDF), and disclosed the limited audit-log retention on `admin_access_logs` (rows survive account deletion by design; the previous "removes all associated data" claim was overbroad). Added three new sections: **Data Breach Notification** (timeframes per applicable law), **Your California Privacy Rights** (full CCPA/CPRA structure — categories collected, sources, business purposes, explicit no-sale/no-share statement, right to know/delete/correct/non-discrimination, verification process), and **International Users** (US data-transfer consent + EU/UK acknowledgement). **Terms.** Identified mywalnut, Inc. with the standard "officers, directors, employees, agents, affiliates" formulation in the operative clauses. Added six new sections: **Beta Service** (sets expectation that features may change and data may be reset between phases), **Children Under 18** (COPPA companion to the 18+ checkbox already enforced in `SeedScreen.tsx:15`), **Disclaimer of Warranties** (AS IS / AS AVAILABLE), **Limitation of Liability** ($100 or 12-month spend cap, plus indirect/consequential carve-out), **Indemnification** (standard one-way), **Governing Law and Disputes** (California, San Francisco County — the documented best practice for CA-operating Delaware C-Corps in consumer ToS). Expanded Acceptable Use to cover scraping, reverse-engineering, competing-product use, and rate-limit circumvention. Removed the "introductory message" group-invite paragraph since that flow doesn't exist in the code — honest move rather than overpromising. Updated MMS Messaging copy to list all CTIA keyword variants. **Code changes — SMS keyword variants.** `src/lib/linq/message-router.ts:36-62` previously matched only the literal string "STOP" — a carrier-compliance gap because CTIA Short Code Monitoring Handbook requires recognizing UNSUBSCRIBE, CANCEL, QUIT, END, OPT OUT, OPTOUT, and REVOKE as opt-out, plus INFO and SUPPORT as help. Added a `KEYWORD_ALIASES` table mapping every variant to a canonical command, plus a `resolveKeyword(text)` helper that normalizes case + whitespace (so "Opt out" and "OPT OUT" and "opt  out" all hit). Refactored the existing keyword-check site to call `resolveKeyword`. Also added UNSTOP and YES as opt-back-in aliases. **Code changes — account deletion completeness.** `src/app/api/account/delete/route.ts:31` now explicitly deletes `linq_group_chats WHERE owner_user_id = user.id` before the profile delete. The FK was previously SET NULL on profile delete, so the chat row survived with a nulled owner — fine operationally, but it broke the policy claim of "removes all associated data." `admin_access_logs` rows still survive (by design — they're audit records and have no FK to profiles); the Privacy policy now discloses this honestly with retention language. **Scope explicitly NOT in this pass.** No arbitration / class-action waiver clause was added — the Terms send disputes to court, which is fine for a closed beta but should be revisited before public launch. No mailing address (mywalnut, Inc. doesn't have one yet). No paid attorney sign-off — this is engineering-side accuracy work, not a legal opinion. **Files.** `src/app/privacy/page.tsx` (+130 / -25 lines), `src/app/terms/page.tsx` (+169 / -23 lines), `src/lib/linq/message-router.ts` (+30 / -2 lines), `src/app/api/account/delete/route.ts` (+5 lines). 781 tests pass, build clean. Browser-verified at `/privacy` and `/terms` — both pages render with the new sections and corrected entity intro. Merge SHA `c672ef1`, fix SHA `dde8415`.
- Post-confirm celebration cover now actually covers the entry (2026-05-16, amazing-sammet worktree): After the user confirmed a checkpoint, the `CheckpointOverlay`'s "Added to Layer N — LayerName" cover faded in over the entry section for 1.6s before auto-closing. In Hearth theme the cover read as broken — the entry's full body text bled through the celebration text, making the moment look like a glitched-out overlap. **Cause.** The cover's background was `var(--session-walnut-surface)` which is only **20% opaque in Hearth** (and 90% in Bloom — also slightly leaky). The accompanying `backdropFilter: blur(28px) saturate(140%)` made the cover *look* like a frosted curtain but `backdrop-filter` doesn't obscure content — it just blurs whatever shows through the transparent background. So entry heading + 80+-word body painted through the 28px blur with full readability, competing with the ❦ + "Added to Layer One" + italic layer name above it. **Fix.** Layered background that stacks the existing walnut tint over `--session-cream` (fully opaque in both themes): `background: linear-gradient(var(--session-walnut-surface), var(--session-walnut-surface)), var(--session-cream)`. The cover keeps its warm Manual feel — Hearth reads as warm-tinted near-black, Bloom as warm cream — but is now fully opaque, so the celebration moment stages cleanly. Dropped `backdropFilter` / `WebkitBackdropFilter`: nothing to blur once the background is opaque. **Scope.** Only the confirmed-cover element changed (lines 488–506 of `CheckpointOverlay.tsx`). Module background and dialog backdrop untouched — those work as designed because they sit over the 92%-black `--session-backdrop-heavy`, which adequately hides the chat behind. The bug was specific to the inner cover stacking against its own module's entry section. **Files.** `src/components/checkpoint/CheckpointOverlay.tsx` (3-line style swap + 6 lines of doc comment explaining the opacity choice for future readers). 781 tests pass, build clean. **Browser verification skipped** because reproducing the bug requires going through a fresh conversation to a checkpoint-confirm to catch the 1.6s cover, and the dev-server-from-worktree rule blocks a quick spin-up. Merge SHA `5381022`, fix SHA `eb6a244`.
- Onboarding gate hardened + dead onboarding props removed (2026-05-16, mystifying-mclean worktree): Three follow-ups to the PostLoginOnboarding scroll fix earlier the same day. **1. Fail-closed onboarding gate.** `MainApp.tsx`'s `/api/onboarding-status` fetch used to fail-open on HTTP error or network throw — set `onboardingStatus` to `"complete"` and let the user into chat. That silently bypasses `SeedScreen`, which is where the age-gate checkbox and the legal-disclaimer acknowledgement live. A transient API hiccup during a fresh signup meant we'd have no record of consent and a brand-new user could be all the way into chat with Jove without ever clicking through "I'm 18 or older". Flipped to fail-closed: HTTP error and catch-block both now set `"needed"`, which renders `PostLoginOnboarding`. Worst case for a returning user caught in the rare error window is one extra Continue / Begin click; `SeedScreen`'s `profiles.onboarding_completed_at` write is idempotent, so re-completing is harmless. The fail-open comment is replaced with one that explains *why* fail-closed is the right tradeoff for a compliance gate. **2. Dropped `EntryScreen.onBegin` dead prop.** The prop was declared on the interface but the component only destructured `onLogin` — the "Begin" button it was meant to wire was removed in an earlier landing redesign, but the prop signature and the JSX pass-through from `OnboardingFlow` were never cleaned up. Removed `onBegin?` from the interface, dropped the `onBegin={handleBegin}` JSX, and that lets the `loginMode` `useState` collapse too: `handleLogin` always set `"login"` (the only other writer, `handleBegin`, is now gone), so the state never held anything but `"login"`. `LoginScreen` now takes `initialMode="login"` as a literal. Net `-1 useState`, `-1 handler function`, `-1 piece of unused prop surface area`. **3. Renamed `InfoScreens.onNavigateToSeed` → `onContinue`.** The prop name was a fossil from when the flow was `Info → Seed` directly; once `PersonaModeScreen` was inserted between them the prop actually navigated to persona, not seed, but the name never updated. Trivial rename in the props interface, the destructure, the button's `onClick`, and the `PostLoginOnboarding` callsite. **Files.** `src/components/MainApp.tsx` (gate flip + comment), `src/components/onboarding/EntryScreen.tsx` (`-onBegin` from props interface), `src/components/onboarding/OnboardingFlow.tsx` (`-loginMode` state, `-handleBegin`, `-onBegin` prop pass), `src/components/onboarding/InfoScreens.tsx` (rename), `src/components/onboarding/PostLoginOnboarding.tsx` (rename at callsite). 772 tests pass, build clean. Verified live: signed out → `/login` renders `EntryScreen` ("mywalnut." hero + "Sign in" button); click → `LoginScreen` in login mode with email/password + Continue with Google. Merge SHA `c52c4de`, fix SHA `b95285b`.
- Dead headline + validation question stripped from historical checkpoint plate (2026-05-16, amazing-sammet worktree): When the user refines (or otherwise acts on) a checkpoint, the chat collapses Jove's live proposal into a `Plate` that already shows the composed name as a heading at the top. But the plate body kept rendering Jove's full raw reflection, which the prompt format ends with `Headline: XYZ` followed by `What would you change or sharpen?` — both authored to scaffold the live moment. After the user has acted, the headline is a duplicate (heading is at the top) and the validation question is unanswerable (the user already answered with their action). They read as dead artifacts. **Fix.** New `stripCheckpointFooter(content)` in `src/lib/utils/format.tsx`: trims the trailing validation question (case-insensitive across the prompt's common variants — "what would you change/sharpen/adjust/tighten/tweak", "where is this off", "where does this miss"), then strips either an explicit `Headline: XYZ` prefix line OR a bare title-shaped last paragraph (2–12 words, ≤100 chars, no terminal punctuation). The bare-title strip only fires when the validation question was found — that confirms we're in the structural footer and not eating a real closing sentence. Applied at `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx:689` (`renderMarkdown(stripCheckpointFooter(msg.content))`). **Not changed.** Jove's system prompt still emits the footer because the streamed reflection needs the validation question as a real conversational gesture in the moment — only the post-action historical view gets it stripped. The pending trigger card already shows just the name (no body), and `CheckpointOverlay` reads `composed_content` which doesn't carry these elements. Admin debug pane (`UserProfilePane`) is intentionally unaffected — raw audit view shows what Jove literally said. **Files.** `src/lib/utils/format.tsx` (+57 lines: new helper with doc comment), `src/lib/utils/format.test.ts` (+9 tests covering explicit prefix, bare-title position, question-absent safety, long-paragraph safety, `Where is this off?` variant), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (1-line wrap of the historical-plate render). 781 tests pass (was 772; +9 new). Build clean. Merge SHA `f604fc2`, fix SHA `8ab1d60`.
- PostLoginOnboarding screens now scroll when content exceeds the viewport (2026-05-16, mystifying-mclean worktree): Fresh signups going through `Info → Persona → Seed` were getting clipped on short viewports — most reproducibly on `PersonaModeScreen` with four options + descriptions stacked under the header — with no way to scroll to the Continue CTA. **Root cause.** All three onboarding screens used the same pattern: `<main>` flex column at `height: 100%`, plate content rendered as a natural-size flex child, then `<div style={{ flex: 1 }} />` as a spacer to push the CTA footer to the bottom. `PostLoginOnboarding` wraps everything in `overflow: hidden`, so when the plate exceeded the available height the spacer reached zero, then the plate clipped out the bottom of the viewport — and the page had no scroll surface to recover with. The classic flexbox pitfall on top of that: a flex child's default `min-height: auto` blocks it from shrinking below content size, which would have prevented `overflow-y: auto` from kicking in even if we'd added it. **Fix — sticky-footer + scrollable-middle.** Each screen now has three flex children inside `<main>`: TopBar (natural height, pinned top), a `flex: 1 / min-height: 0 / overflow-y: auto` scroll surface containing the plate + error + 32px bottom-breathing-room, then the CTA footer with `flex-shrink: 0` and a `paddingTop: 16` border so it reads as a separate band. `WebkitOverflowScrolling: touch` for iOS momentum. SeedScreen keeps `justifyContent: center` inside its scroll surface — short content still vertically centers; long content scrolls. **What this also fixes.** Browser zoom > 100%, large system font scaling, and the new compressed phone-frame typography all reduce the available height — any of them could trigger the clip on persona (the tallest screen). **Files.** `src/components/onboarding/PersonaModeScreen.tsx`, `src/components/onboarding/InfoScreens.tsx`, `src/components/onboarding/SeedScreen.tsx` — same scroll wrapper pattern in each. 772 tests pass, build clean. Merge SHA `bbcd1c2`, fix SHA `a4cbce4`.
- Chat-view typography scaled to phone-frame reading register (2026-05-15, mystifying-mclean worktree): Follow-up to the Manual typography pass shipped earlier the same day. After bringing the Manual page down from 48px h1 → 30px, the chat view (rendered inside the same 430px phone frame on the desktop vitrine) still read as poster-scale because the Bubble + Plate + welcome screen + checkpoint trigger card were all tuned for an iPhone-screen reading distance, not for projection onto a 1440px desktop where the frame physically takes ~1/3 of the screen. **Reduction across every chat surface, same 14.5px body register the Manual just adopted.** `Bubble` body 17 → **14.5** with line-height 1.62 → 1.6 and letter-spacing −0.05px → −0.03px; Jove eyebrow 10 → 9.5 with margin-bottom 8 → 6 (less air below a smaller eyebrow). `Plate` (historical checkpoint Plate in the chat thread) heading 24 → **19**, eyebrow 11 → 10, body 17 → **14.5** — matches Bubble exactly, since Plate body is the same long-form reading register. `TopBar` wordmark 19 → **16** with tracking −0.4 → −0.3, circle button 44×44 → 38×38 with glyph 16 → 14 — the TopBar was a chunky iOS-toolbar in the desktop vitrine, now reads as a refined paratext bar. `MobileSession` welcome h1 44 → **30** (matches `Your Manual.`), welcome subtitle 18 → 13.5 italic, intake card titles ("Navigate a situation", "Guided intake", "Upload") 16 → **14.5** with sub-rows 13 → 11.5; checkpoint trigger card (the elevated card with halo) italic title 21 → **17**, fontWeight 500 → 400 (italic-at-regular is the editorial convention; medium weight on top doubled emphasis), tracking −0.2 → −0.15, "Tap to review" mono 13 → 11.5, chevron 22 → **18**, internal paddings tightened 18 → 16 and 14 → 12; typing indicator ❦ 20 → 17. `ChatInput` placeholder 16 → **14.5** italic, textarea 17 → **14.5** italic. **Hierarchy preserved across the phone frame.** Welcome 30 → intake 14.5 → bubble/plate body 14.5 → eyebrows 9.5/10 — same register the Manual page just adopted (h1 30 → chip 15.5 → entry italic 16 → body 14.5), so navigating between chat and Manual no longer crosses a typography gear-shift. Plate heading 19 sits half-step above bubble body 14.5 for confirmed-checkpoint surfaces — same delta as Manual chip 15.5 vs body 14.5. The checkpoint trigger card's italic title at 17 reads as a closer cousin of the bubble body than the previous 21 — fits the "this belongs to Jove's side of the conversation" intent shipped earlier the same day. Verified at 1440×900 in both Bloom and Hearth: chat thread reads as editorial prose, checkpoint card and Manual page land at the same scale. **Files.** `src/components/shared/Bubble.tsx` (2 size changes), `src/components/shared/Plate.tsx` (3 sizes), `src/components/shared/TopBar.tsx` (3 sizes + chip dimensions), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (welcome h1+subtitle, 3 intake cards, checkpoint trigger card, typing indicator), `src/components/mobile/ChatInput.tsx` (placeholder + textarea). 772 tests pass, build clean. Merge SHA `1c7cca5`, fix SHA `0981129`.
- Vitrine left-rail unification + Manual typography scale (2026-05-15, mystifying-mclean worktree): Two pieces of polish that the previous shipping pass left rough. **Problem 1 — masthead/colophon overlap.** At wide desktop (≥1030px), `.mw-masthead` lived at `top: 48px / left: 48px` and `.mw-colophon` lived at `bottom: 48px / left: 48px` — two absolutely-positioned blocks sharing the left margin. When the admin DevToolsPanel rendered inside the colophon (only path that grew the colophon past ~200px tall), the colophon's prose at the top reached up into the masthead's `mywalnut.` and the two stacks collided. **Fix.** `.mw-colophon` is now anchored between `top: 132px` (just below the 52px masthead with a 32px breathing gap) and `bottom: 48px` and runs as a flex column. A new `.mw-colophon-meta` class on the theme paragraph carries `margin-top: auto` so theme/admin/beta are pinned to the rail floor — when there's no DevToolsPanel the prose sits at top and the meta sits at the foot like a publication spread; when there is one, the content packs from the top and the meta still anchors the bottom. `overflow-y: auto` with `scrollbar-gutter: stable` protects against pathologically tall dev content. The narrow-desktop (431–1029px) layout is unchanged — that one was already a vertical stack with the colophon centered at the bottom. **Problem 2 — Manual page read as poster-scale.** "Your Manual." at 48px Spectral dominated the 430px frame, and the longest layer chip ("IV. How I Show Up with People") was 284px wide in a 335px mobile Plate at 18px — visibly overflowing at narrow viewports or any browser zoom. **Fix — register drop, hierarchy preserved.** `h1` "Your Manual." 48 → 30 (line-height 1.06, `-0.018em` tracking) — still a publication title, no longer monumental. Subtitle 15.5 → 13.5 italic, `maxWidth: 300`. `TabPip` chip 22 → 15.5 with padding `8px 16px` → `7px 13px` and radius 9 → 7. Entry title 18 → 16 italic with tighter `-0.003em` tracking. Entry body 16 → 14.5. The chip change ripples into the in-chat checkpoint trigger card — both surfaces still wear the same chapter mark from `src/components/mobile/manual/TabPip.tsx` — so the elevated-card chapter mark is now 15.5 to match. The italic title on the checkpoint card stays at 21 (it's a different component, untouched). - Elevated checkpoint trigger card — Manual vocabulary + warm halo (2026-05-15, priceless-shockley worktree): The in-chat pending-checkpoint card now reads as a marked moment that belongs to both Jove's side of the conversation and the Manual. **Visual moves.** **(1) TabPip on the top edge.** Same walnut chip the Manual page uses (`I. Layer Name`, solid `rgb(135, 90, 55)`, cream-on-walnut, Spectral 22/500 with `-0.01em`) — extracted into `src/components/mobile/manual/TabPip.tsx` so the Manual `LayerHeader` and the checkpoint card render the same chapter mark from one source. **(2) Plate body wears Jove's bubble palette.** Background `--session-jove-bg`, border `--session-jove-border` — the card visually belongs to Jove's side of the chat rather than reading as a warm Manual surface island. **(3) Four-layer warm-walnut halo.** New `.checkpoint-trigger-frame` carries two atmospheric pools via `::before` (wide ambient reach, `rgba(220, 165, 110, 0.18)`) and `::after` (tight blurred inner core, `rgba(220, 165, 110, 0.44)`); `.checkpoint-trigger-plate` adds two more box-shadow halos (`0 22px 50px` close + `0 48px 110px` far) on top of the Manual baseline cream catch-light rim. Bloom-tuned variants swap to more saturated walnut (`rgba(180, 120, 70, 0.32–0.22)`) since warm-on-cream needs more presence. **(4) "POTENTIAL MANUAL ENTRY" eyebrow.** Small mono caps (`--font-mono` 10/500, letter-spacing 0.22em, `--session-walnut-meta-strong` color) at the top of the body — the only verbal signal this isn't filed yet. **(5) Italic Spectral title at 21px** instead of the previous upright 18px — matches the Manual page entry style. **(6) Inline "Tap to review"** with a walnut chevron above a `--session-hair-soft` rule. The whole Plate is the tap target. **Reuse.** `<TabPip layerId layerName />` consumed by both `LayerHeader` (Manual page) and `MobileSession`'s pending-checkpoint render — theme-stable hardcoded walnut lives in one place, so the Manual chip and the checkpoint chip can't drift. **Mockup of the design** at `mockups/checkpoint-elevated-v3-themes.html` (Hearth + Bloom side by side); shipped code mirrors it. **Files.** `src/components/mobile/manual/TabPip.tsx` (new, +50 lines), `src/components/mobile/manual/LayerHeader.tsx` (consumes TabPip — visual on the Manual page unchanged), `src/app/globals.css` (+78 lines: `.checkpoint-trigger-frame` and `.checkpoint-trigger-plate` rule blocks for both themes), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (trigger card render now uses the new classes + TabPip — net −7 lines from removing the old inline header band + custom borderRadius/shadow). 772 tests pass, build clean. Merge SHAs `71364ab` (TabPip + CSS prep) and `fc25475` (consumer wired).
- Edit Manual entries in place from the Manual page (2026-05-15, wizardly-lewin worktree): Users can now revise a confirmed entry's title and body directly on the Manual page — same edit affordance the checkpoint review overlay has had, surfaced where the user actually re-reads their patterns. **User intent (per docs/intent.md).** "Nothing writes without user confirmation. The user is the author. Jove is the facilitator." Lacking a Manual-side edit path meant any typo or sharpening required a fresh checkpoint via chat — friction against the "user as author" principle. This closes that gap. **UI (mirrors CheckpointOverlay).** Expanded entry's action row shows `EDIT · EXPLORE FURTHER ›` (mono caps walnut). Tapping Edit: title (h3) becomes `contentEditable` with a walnut underline; body (div) becomes `contentEditable` framed in a walnut-bordered box on `--session-walnut-surface-soft`. Action row swaps to `CANCEL  ·  SAVE CHANGES ›`. Saving disables both buttons, shows "Saving…". Inline `--session-error-text` alert appears above the row if validation or the server rejects. **API.** New `PATCH /api/manual/[id]` (edge runtime). `requireUser` auth, rate-limited at 30/hour per user via new `manualEditHour` bucket (generous enough for normal polishing — typo + sharpen a few entries in a sitting — tight enough that a runaway client can't flood the table). Body `{ name?: string | null, content?: string }`. Trims, validates length (`name ≤ 200`, `content ≤ 5000`). Ownership enforced via `WHERE id = entryId AND user_id = user.id` (defense in depth on top of the existing "Users can update own manual entries" RLS policy). When content changes, regenerates `summary` via `deriveSummaryFromContent` (first sentence, 240-char cap) and clears `key_words` — same fallback `confirmCheckpoint` uses for edited content, so the compressed Manual view shipped to Jove's prompt stays accurate. Returns the updated row. **Client (useChat).** New `updateEntry(id, edits)` posts to the new route and merges the returned row into `confirmedEntries` so the UI refreshes without a refetch. Returns `{ ok: true, entry }` or `{ ok: false, error }`. Threaded through `MainApp → MobileManual → PopulatedLayer → EntryItem`. AdminManualView unaffected — passes no `onUpdateEntry`, so the `canEdit = !readOnly && !!onUpdateEntry` gate hides the Edit button in admin views. **Bug caught during verification.** Cancel exited edit mode but the user's typed-but-canceled text persisted in the DOM. Cause: React doesn't reconcile a `contentEditable` element's children unless the source prop changes — and on Cancel `entry.name` / `entry.body` are unchanged. Fixed by imperatively resetting `titleRef.current.innerText = entry.name` and `bodyRef.current.innerText = entry.body` before flipping `editing` off. (Save path doesn't need this — the optimistic patch updates `entry.name` / `entry.body` props which React DOES reconcile.) **Verified live** at `devtest@test.com`: typed new title + body → Save → `PATCH /api/manual/[id] 200`, read mode shows new content, persists across page reload; typed garbage → Cancel → originals restored, no PATCH fired. **Files.** `src/app/api/manual/[id]/route.ts` (new, +179 lines), `src/lib/rate-limit.ts` (+4 lines for the new bucket), `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts` (+40 lines for `updateEntry`), `src/components/MainApp.tsx`, `src/components/mobile/MobileManual.tsx`, `src/components/mobile/manual/PopulatedLayer.tsx` (prop pass-through), `src/components/mobile/manual/EntryItem.tsx` (+~270 lines for the edit-mode UI + handlers). 751 tests pass, build clean. Merge SHA `0608bb9`.
- Entry title — regular weight + looser leading (2026-05-15, wizardly-lewin worktree): Follow-up to the Manual typography pass earlier the same day. The user flagged the pattern (entry) headers as "needing work" without knowing exactly what. **Diagnosis.** Italic Spectral 500 + 1.3 line-height read as a UI label, not a literary section title — italic alone is the differentiator from the upright body, and adding medium weight on top doubles emphasis. Stacked 3–4 deep in a populated Plate, the visual felt overstated, like a list of button labels rather than thread headlines. The tight 1.3 leading also cramped italic wraps on long real titles ("I Spit the Signal Back Before Anyone Hears It" wraps to two lines, which felt mashed). **Fix.** Two inline-style changes in `EntryItem.tsx`: `fontWeight` 500 → **400**, `lineHeight` 1.3 → **1.4**. Size (18px), italic style, color (`--session-ink`), and letter-spacing (-0.005em) unchanged. The italic-at-regular pattern is the editorial convention used by Apple Books, NYT, Substack, and Bear for section titles in lists. **Verified live** in both themes via injected long-title test strings: Bloom (espresso italic on cream) reads like a printed book section heading; Hearth (cream italic on deep walnut) sits in the layer instead of fighting for attention. Two-line wraps breathe at 1.4 leading. **Files.** `src/components/mobile/manual/EntryItem.tsx` (2 inline-style values + comment refresh). 751 tests pass, build clean. Merge SHA `3ec09a0`.
- Chat scroll/input seam — single padding owned by scroll container, matched to inter-bubble gap (2026-05-15, direct-to-main): Three iterations to land the right spacing between the last bubble and the input. **(1) The 40px checkpoint spacer** (a `flexShrink: 0` div appended below the last message when it's a pending checkpoint card, from commit `e395589`) was creating ~50–80px of empty space between the card and the input zone. Deleted outright — the current card design doesn't need that much breathing room. **(2) Double-padding at the seam.** ChatInput's wrapper had `padding: "var(--sp-sm) 16px var(--sp-sm)"` — 14px top + 14px bottom. The 14px top stacked with the scroll container's 4px `padding-bottom`, giving 18px of empty space doing the same job twice. Dropped wrapper top-padding to 0; bottom kept at 14px to keep the input clear of the phone frame edge. **(3) Match the inter-bubble rhythm.** With ChatInput's top padding gone, the gap was 4px — visibly tighter than the 14px gap between chat bubbles, so the last bubble looked closer to the input than to the message above it. Bumped the scroll container's `padding-bottom` from 4px to 14px so the gap matches the inter-bubble rhythm. The scroll container now owns the spacing as a single source of truth, semantically tied to the same 14px value used in `gap: "14px"`. **Bottom mask stays at 4px feather** — bubble border now sits 10px above the fade zone, well clear of any visible content (the fade still softens content scrolled past the edge). **Cleanup note.** A first attempt at step 3 (`1f7cc78`) accidentally bundled unstaged work-in-progress on the checkpoint trigger card (TabPip import, `.checkpoint-trigger-frame` CSS class) that depended on an untracked `TabPip.tsx` — main would not have built for anyone pulling fresh. Reverted at `1327642`, re-applied as a clean one-line change at `6355393`. **Files.** `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (−7 lines for the spacer + 1-line padding bump), `src/components/mobile/ChatInput.tsx` (1-line padding swap). 772 tests pass, build clean.
- Manual page typography pass — info chip SVG + Layer > Pattern > Body hierarchy (2026-05-15, wizardly-lewin worktree): Two follow-ups to the Plate-with-tab redesign shipped earlier the same day. **(1) Info chip recentered.** The previous `<button>i</button>` rendering used a 22px outlined circle with italic Spectral "i" inside, kept visually centered with `padding: "0 0 2px 0"` to fight the italic glyph's top-lean. The user flagged the "i" as visibly off-center. Replaced the text glyph with an inline SVG (1px-wide `<rect>` stem + 1px `<circle>` dot, both `currentColor`) — the SVG's geometry is centered by construction, so flex centering on the parent button now positions it perfectly: `button.getBoundingClientRect()` center matches `svg.getBoundingClientRect()` center to 0px in both axes (verified live: `(809, 281.04) === (809, 281.04)`). Chip also shrunk from 22→20px per the same feedback ("a bit smaller"). The walnut-on-walnut color treatment is unchanged. **(2) Hierarchy: Layer > Pattern > Body.** Prior type stack inverted the expected reading order (entry titles at 20px, tab pip at 18px) — the chapter-level Layer header was *smaller* than the section-level Pattern header. Fixed: tab pip fontSize 18→**22px** (with chip vertical padding 8→9px and border-radius 8→9px proportional bumps to preserve the chip's optical mass), entry title 20→**18px** (italic Spectral, weight 500 unchanged), letter-spacing eased -0.01em → -0.005em at the smaller size. Body stays 16px / 1.65 / `--session-ink-soft`. New ratios: Layer:Pattern = 22:18 = 1.22×, Pattern:Body = 18:16 = 1.125× (the italic-vs-upright differentiation carries the rest of the Pattern-vs-Body contrast). Plate `padding-top` bumped 26→30px on both PopulatedLayer and EmptyLayer to clear the now-taller tab pip protrusion (chip is 40px tall, protrudes 20px above the Plate top, content needs ~10px clearance below). **Both themes verified live** at `devtest@test.com` — Hearth (deep walnut chip, cream text, three-layer shadow on populated Plates) and Bloom (espresso walnut chip on cream paper) — info chip mathematically centered, hierarchy reads cleanly across five Layers. **Files.** `src/components/mobile/manual/LayerHeader.tsx` (info chip SVG + tab pip font/padding), `src/components/mobile/manual/EntryItem.tsx` (entry title 20→18px + comment refresh), `src/components/mobile/manual/PopulatedLayer.tsx` + `EmptyLayer.tsx` (Plate padding-top 26→30px). 751 tests pass, build clean. Merge SHA `ca43d19`.
- Manual page — Plate-with-tab redesign + exploration-chip cleanup (2026-05-15, wizardly-lewin worktree): Manual surface rewritten to a floating-Plate-with-protruding-tab-pip vocabulary, matching `mockups/manual-redesign.html`. Every Layer renders the same structural grammar populated or empty — only the body differs — so the eye doesn't reorient when scanning five Layers. **Architecture.** New shared `src/components/mobile/manual/LayerHeader.tsx` owns the tab pip + info chip + description popover (~190 lines pulled out of both PopulatedLayer and EmptyLayer so the shape is single-sourced). Tab pip is an absolutely-positioned walnut chip (theme-stable `rgb(135, 90, 55)` background with cream `rgba(245,243,238,0.96)` text — cream-on-walnut lands AA Normal at 5.34:1 on the chip and AAA in Bloom) protruding from the top-left edge via `top: 0; left: 18px; transform: translateY(-50%)`. Info chip is a 22px outlined circle at the top-right with italic walnut "i" — quiet by default, fills with `--session-walnut-highlight` on hover/active. Clicking opens a 270px popover anchored below with the layer's description (`layer.about`) in Spectral 14.5px on `--session-parchment`. The host Plate gets a `zIndex: 50` lift when its popover is open so the popover paints above the next Plate. **Decisions.** (1) Empty Plate has the same Plate-with-tab vocabulary as populated, with `boxShadow: none` instead of `var(--session-plate-shadow)` — the Hearth three-layer drop signals "this Plate has weight," and an empty Plate hasn't accumulated anything yet. Same family, less lift. (2) Layer description always lives behind the info chip — in both populated and empty Plates — so the body row is content (entries OR placeholder), never meta. The empty-state body is two italic Spectral lines: "No patterns discovered yet." (14px / `--session-ink-mid`) and "Explore with Jove" (15px weight 500 / `--session-walnut-meta-strong`) — descriptive status + on-brand CTA, no right-side arrow (an arrow is an expand affordance; nothing to expand). Whole Plate is the tap target → `onExploreWithPersona({type: "empty_layer", content: layer.about})`. (3) Entry headlines lifted from 17px → 20px italic Spectral so the thread-title-to-body ratio hits 1.33× (was 1.06×) — past the editorial threshold (Apple Books 1.5×, Bear 1.4×); body softened from `--session-ink` 0.97 → `--session-ink-soft` 0.85 at 16px / 1.65 leading so cream-on-walnut reads as comfortable prose, not bold. (4) Dotted walnut hair between entries indented 22px from the left, anchored as an editorial section break, not a row separator (`--session-walnut-meta-soft`, 1px dotted via `border-top`). (5) Masthead no longer carries a § ornament — title + italic subtitle only; the tab pip below carries the next visual beat. Layers container is a flex column with `gap: var(--sp-md)`, padding `var(--sp-lg) 20px 40px` (the `--sp-lg` top gives the first Plate's pip clearance). **Dead code swept.** Hardcoded `EMPTY_DESCRIPTORS` lookup in `EmptyLayer.tsx` deleted — the layer description now flows from `layers.ts → layer.about` through the info popover in every consumer. Legacy `showDivider` prop on `EntryItem` (kept for AdminManualView back-compat across multiple prior passes) removed — no consumer references it. TopBar untouched per direction; share invitation + half-sheet kept as-is. **AdminManualView.** Converted its outer `<div>` to a flex column with `gap: var(--sp-md)` and `paddingTop: 18` to give the protruding tab pips clearance — previously each `marginTop: 13` ran into the previous Plate's bottom edge. The "UPCOMING" between-section label collapses correctly into the flex flow. **Exploration-chip removal.** When the user tapped "Explore with Jove" from the Manual, a walnut pill banner appeared at the top of the chat surface showing the Layer eyebrow + entry name with a dismiss × — the modal-like chip was redundant (Jove's primed first message already names the layer) and read as a stuck-modal artifact. Deleted: the in-chat chip JSX from `MobileSession.tsx`, the `currentExploration` state + `explorationConvIdRef`/`explorationPendingBindRef` race-handling effect from `MainApp.tsx`, the `onDismissExploration` prop, and the bind-arm logic in `handleExploreWithPersona`. Kept: the `ExplorationContext` type, the API plumbing through `startExploration` / `useChat` / `call-persona` / `system-prompt` (Jove still receives the layer context to prime its first response), and the transitioning interstitial (`explorationPhase` + `explorationLabel`). Chat surface is now clean post-exploration. **Both themes verified live** at `devtest@test.com` — Hearth deep walnut + three-layer shadow on populated Plates, Bloom cream paper + soft ink shadow. Info popover lift confirmed above the next Plate. Entry expand/collapse smooth with the new 20px italic title. **Files.** `src/components/mobile/manual/LayerHeader.tsx` (new, +216 lines), `src/components/mobile/manual/PopulatedLayer.tsx` (rewritten), `src/components/mobile/manual/EmptyLayer.tsx` (rewritten — EMPTY_DESCRIPTORS dropped, two-line body), `src/components/mobile/manual/EntryItem.tsx` (entry title scale, body density, indented dotted divider; legacy showDivider prop removed), `src/components/mobile/MobileManual.tsx` (layers flex container + masthead bottom padding zeroed), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (exploration chip JSX deleted), `src/components/MainApp.tsx` (chip state + race-handling effect + prop pass-through deleted), `src/components/admin/AdminManualView.tsx` (flex column for tab pip spacing). `docs/rules.md` "No theme switching" line replaced with "Two themes ship" (Hearth + Bloom). Demo artifact at `mockups/manual-redesign-revised.html` preserved as design history. 751 tests pass, build clean. Merge SHA `554cba3`.
- SessionDrawer scoped to the phone frame on desktop (2026-05-15, jovial-torvalds worktree): Visual regression on desktop — the session drawer slid in from the absolute left edge of the viewport instead of overlaying the centered 430px phone frame, so mobile and desktop looked mismatched ("opens on the left side of the screen and looks off"). **Root cause.** `SessionDrawer`'s backdrop and panel used `position: fixed`, which anchors to the viewport. `SessionDrawer` was also rendered as a sibling of `<MobileLayout>` in `MainApp`, i.e. **outside** the DesktopVitrine phone frame in the DOM, so even moving it to `position: absolute` wouldn't have helped on its own — there was no positioned ancestor scoping it to the frame. **Fix.** Added an `overlay?: React.ReactNode` prop to `MobileLayout` that renders inside the DesktopVitrine wrapper as a sibling of the view panels (so it lives inside `.mw-phone-frame`, which has `position: relative`, `overflow: hidden`, and the responsive rounded corners). Moved the `<SessionDrawer>` instantiation in `MainApp` from a sibling of `<MobileLayout>` into the new `overlay` slot, then switched both the backdrop and the drawer panel from `position: fixed` to `position: absolute`. On desktop the drawer now slides in from the left edge of the centered phone frame and is clipped by the frame's rounded corners; on mobile the phone frame fills the viewport so the behavior is byte-equivalent to the previous shipped state. Verified end-to-end in the browser at 1280×720 (drawer at x=426, w=360 inside the 430px frame at x=425) and 375×812 (drawer at x=0, w=322.5 matching the full-viewport frame); backdrop-click and close-button both still dismiss. **Files.** `src/components/layout/MobileLayout.tsx` (+7 lines: optional `overlay` prop + render slot), `src/components/MainApp.tsx` (moved SessionDrawer into the overlay prop), `src/components/mobile/SessionDrawer.tsx` (2-line position swap + comment refresh). 772 tests pass, build clean. **Follow-up (same day, 209714c).** The position swap from `fixed` to `absolute` exposed a second issue: the drawer's `boxShadow: "12px 0 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.55)"` was being rendered even when the drawer was closed and translated `-100%`. Under `position: fixed` that translate moved the shadow off-viewport, but under `position: absolute` the drawer's right edge sits flush against the phone frame's left edge when closed, so the shadow's 12px right offset + 60px blur bled ~0–42px into the frame's interior. On warm cream parchment, simultaneous contrast made that neutral dark grey read as a cool/blue gradient. Fixed by making the shadow conditional on `open` and adding `box-shadow` to the transition list so it fades in/out alongside the slide. Verified clean on mobile + desktop in both light and dark themes; drawer-open depth shadow is preserved. `src/components/mobile/SessionDrawer.tsx` (+6 lines).
- Checkpoint instructions load when material gate would pass (2026-05-15, priceless-shockley worktree): Re-applied as a standalone commit after an earlier bundled attempt landed inside a merge commit that also carried the Manual page redesign; reverting that merge took the gate-mirror fix out with it. **Symptom.** An 18-turn conversation with concrete scenes, body anchors, charged language, mechanism, and behavior-driver link never surfaced a checkpoint — Jove kept deepening past every reasonable moment. **Root cause.** Extraction emits two readings on each call: a per-layer `signal` (`none` → `emerging` → `explored` → `checkpoint_ready`) and a mechanical `checkpoint_gate` checklist (`concrete_examples`, `has_charged_language`, `has_mechanism`, `has_behavior_driver_link`). The upstream gate in `loadConversationContext` that decides whether to load CHECKPOINTS instructions into Jove's prompt read only the signal. The downstream gate that suppresses thin proposals (`validateMaterialQuality`) reads only the checklist. When they disagreed — long, rich conversation but per-layer signal stuck at `"emerging"` — CHECKPOINTS never loaded, Jove had no instructions to propose, and the conversation ran past every reasonable checkpoint moment. **Fix.** Extracted `deriveCheckpointApproaching(previousExtraction, isFirstCheckpoint, turnCount)` as a pure helper. Returns true when EITHER any layer is at `"explored"` / `"checkpoint_ready"` (original signal-only path) OR `validateMaterialQuality` would pass — the same gate that gates suppression downstream. Same extraction call, same fields, same gate logic applied earlier in the pipeline so the upstream and downstream decisions stay aligned. No new criterion; the crisis-block and `pattern_engaged` turn-12 override flow through `validateMaterialQuality` automatically. **Standalone, revertable.** This commit touches only `persona-pipeline.{ts,test.ts}` so it can be rolled back independently if it ever needs to be. **Tests.** 7 new in `deriveCheckpointApproaching`: cold start, explored signal, checkpoint_ready signal, the reported bug (emerging + full checklist), thin material (both gates fail), crisis override, pattern_engaged turn-12 override. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts` (extracted helper + call-site swap), `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.test.ts` (+7 tests). 772 tests pass (was 765; +7 new). Build clean.
- Chat scroll-edge mask-fade (2026-05-15, blissful-bartik worktree): Cosmetic polish to the mobile session scroll container. The previous shape had an absolutely-positioned 48px gradient overlay covering the top of the messages area (atmospheric depth into the header) and nothing at the bottom — so the last bubble could be visibly clipped by a hairline where the scroll viewport met the input bubble. Replaced both treatments with a single asymmetric `maskImage` / `WebkitMaskImage` on the scroll container's inline style: `linear-gradient(to bottom, transparent 0, black 8px, black calc(100% - 4px), transparent 100%)` — 8px feather at top, 4px feather at bottom. The bottom feather exactly matches the scroll container's existing 4px padding-bottom, so the last bubble's bottom border sits right at the start of the fade and only the empty padding gutter feathers; never the bubble's text or border. Top fade is slightly larger (8px) because the scroll container has 20px of top padding, so there's room. **Tradeoff considered.** A scroll-aware version (state + scroll listener + ResizeObserver) was prototyped that turned each fade off when content didn't actually overflow in that direction — industry-standard polish (iOS, Material). User pushed back on that as over-engineered for a 1-px hairline problem. An interim bump of `padding-bottom` from 4px to 12px (with symmetric 8px fade) was also tried but the user flagged the extra space between the last bubble and the input as visually wrong on tight screens — reverted in the same session. The shipped form keeps the original 4px padding and matches the bottom fade exactly to it. **Files.** `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (deleted the top-fade overlay div; added two-line mask on the existing scroll container; 8/4px asymmetric fade in final form). 765 tests pass, build clean.
- Simulate-user persona + intake mode controls (2026-05-15, naughty-hellman worktree): Lets admins drive the dev simulator against different user types without mutating their own profile. **UI.** Added persona-mode multi-select (autistic / AuDHD / dyslexic / general) and intake-mode picker (situation / guided-intake / upload) to the simulator surface on both desktop (`DevToolsPanel`) and mobile (`MobileSettings`); both consume a single new shared `src/components/admin/PersonaIntakeControls.tsx` component (controlled, state stays in each parent so the existing SSE handler is untouched). **Backend.** Selected values flow through `/api/dev-simulate` → optional `personaModesOverride` on `callPersona` → `loadConversationContext` (override short-circuits the `profiles.persona_modes` read when set) → through to `buildSystemPromptBlocks` unchanged. Intake mode is passed to the conversation `INSERT` at simulator start so the row persists `mode = "guided-intake" | "upload" | "situation"`. Both params are validated against existing enums on the route — bad values return 400. **Toggle util.** Onboarding's `PersonaModeScreen` and the new simulator form now share `togglePersonaMode` from `src/lib/persona/persona-mode-toggle.ts` (the "general is exclusive, neurotype modes combine" rule had been duplicated). **Surface-level guarantees.** Backend changes are purely additive — existing `/api/chat` callers unaffected (override is undefined; mode default unchanged); admin's own `profiles.persona_modes` is never mutated by the simulator. **Verified end-to-end** with persona = [audhd, dyslexic] + intake = guided-intake: simulated conversation row persisted `mode = "guided-intake"`, Jove's first response matched the AuDHD/Dyslexic Tier 2 voice ("That's not a motivation problem. That's something else going on between the knowing and the doing."), admin's `profiles.persona_modes` stayed `["autistic"]`. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/persona-mode-toggle.ts` (new), `src/lib/persona/persona-mode-toggle.test.ts` (new, 8 tests), `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts` (override param on `loadConversationContext`), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (override threaded through `CallPersonaOptions`), `src/app/api/dev-simulate/route.ts` (persona/mode validation + forwarding), `src/app/api/dev-simulate/route.test.ts` (new, 6 tests covering validation + insert payload), `src/components/admin/PersonaIntakeControls.tsx` (new), `src/components/admin/DevToolsPanel.tsx`, `src/components/mobile/MobileSettings.tsx`, `src/components/onboarding/PersonaModeScreen.tsx` (inline toggle replaced with shared util). 765 tests pass (was 751; +14 new). Build clean.
- Checkpoint layer eyebrow — one source of truth across three surfaces (2026-05-15, priceless-shockley worktree): The pending trigger card, the action overlay, and the historical Plate each built the "Layer Two — Some of My Patterns" eyebrow string inline with three slightly different fallback behaviors. The historical Plate was the odd one out — it rendered just the layer name with no `Layer N —` prefix, so a confirmed checkpoint in the chat scroll looked visually different from the same checkpoint as a pending card or in the overlay. **Fix.** New `formatLayerEyebrow(layer)` helper + `LAYER_ORDINAL` constant in `src/lib/manual/layers.ts`. All three surfaces consume the same helper, and the historical Plate eyebrow gains the `Layer N —` prefix to match. `CheckpointOverlay`'s `layerName` / `layerOrdinal` string props are gone — overlay derives the eyebrow from `checkpoint.layer` directly, and the "Added to Layer N" success cover reads layer name + ordinal from the same source of truth. `LAYER_ORDINAL` was previously a private const in MobileSession.tsx; moved to layers.ts. The "Saved to LayerName — Layer N" status badge in MobileSession stays as-is (different format, intentionally not the eyebrow). Pure refactor for the trigger card + overlay paths; the historical Plate path is a one-string consistency fix. **Files.** `src/lib/manual/layers.ts` (+21 lines), `src/components/checkpoint/CheckpointOverlay.tsx` (props simplified, success cover wired to `checkpoint.layer`), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (local LAYER_ORDINAL removed, three eyebrow call sites collapsed). 751 tests pass, build clean.
- Prompt-architecture admin — continuous prompt view + General default (2026-05-15, awesome-chaum worktree): Follow-up polish on the prompt-architecture admin page from the earlier "Admin polish" entry. **(1) Each layer renders as one continuous prompt block instead of 14+ nested collapsible cards.** The previous view split the assembled system prompt into per-section cards with truncation, per-card collapse, an "Expand all" toggle, and an info modal opened from a small "i" button on each card. The result was that the page didn't read like the prompt — it read like an inventory of pieces. New shape: a new `PromptBlock` component renders the concatenated `section.text` of all sections in a layer as one `<pre>` (Foundation, Voice). The section labels, tier indicator dots, per-section token counts, "Show full text" buttons, and the info modal are gone — the section headers visible in the actual prompt text (`TIER 1: CONSTITUTIONAL RULES`, `VOICE`, `BANNED PHRASES`, etc.) carry the structure on their own. Foundation now defaults open (was closed). Layer header keeps the token total ("2,684 tokens"). Lifecycle stays as individual blocks because each is conditional, but `LifecycleBlockCard` replaces the old `LifecycleCard` — no truncation, no info modal, no per-block collapse; just the condition pill ("State: checkpoint approaching"), the "Active in" phase tags, and the full prompt text. **(2) Default persona is now General.** Initial state was `["autistic"]`; now `["general"]`. The deselect fallback that previously rebounded to "autistic" when a user cleared all neurotype chips now lands on "general" too. Clicking the General chip is now a single-set action (set to `["general"]`) rather than a toggle — matches the chip's role as the "neutral / no-neurotype" mode. **(3) MODE selector label fix.** The conv-mode chip for the `situation` ConversationMode previously read "Standard" — out of sync with the canonical identifier used in `src/lib/persona/config.ts`, `useChat.ts`, and everywhere else. Now reads "Situation." Two label sites updated together: the chip option list in `src/app/admin/prompt-architecture/page.tsx` and the alternatives-panel label map in `src/lib/admin/prompt-sections.ts`. **Files.** `src/app/admin/prompt-architecture/page.tsx` (−260 lines net: deleted `SectionCard`, `LifecycleCard`, `InfoModal`, `tierColor` helper, and the `Tier` import; added `PromptBlock` + slimmer `LifecycleBlockCard`; removed `expandAll` UI from `LayerHeader`), `src/lib/admin/prompt-sections.ts` (1-line label swap). 751 tests pass (no admin tests exist for this page — verified live in browser after each change: switched persona Autistic ↔ AuDHD ↔ General; confirmed Foundation/Voice render as continuous prompt text with intact "Other voices" alts in Voice; confirmed Lifecycle shows condition pills + phase tags + full text per block). Build clean.
- Trigger card restored on freshly-streamed checkpoints (2026-05-15, priceless-shockley worktree): Regression caught during beta walk-through. After the 2026-05-14 checkpoint pipeline work landed, a checkpoint that fires live rendered its full structured proposal (Layer name, headline, 80-word body, "Headline: …" label, "What would you change or sharpen?" CTA) as **inline chat text** instead of as a compact tap-to-review trigger card. The action overlay (Add to Manual / Edit / Rework / Discard) could not be reached because there was no card to tap. **Root cause.** `streamFromResponse` in `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts` appended the new assistant message to the `messages` state with only `{role, content, id}` — `isCheckpoint` and `checkpointMeta` from the SSE event's `checkpoint` payload were silently dropped. MobileSession's render switch gates the trigger card on `msg.isCheckpoint === true`, so a pending checkpoint fell through to plain-bubble rendering. The DB row was correctly marked `is_checkpoint=true` with `status="pending"` — only the optimistic in-memory state was wrong, which is why a page reload would have surfaced the card via the load-from-DB path. Live streams broke; reload would have masked it. **Fix.** Extracted `buildChatMessageFromEvent` helper (exported for testing). When the SSE event carries a checkpoint payload, the returned ChatMessage has `isCheckpoint=true` plus a minimal `checkpointMeta` `{layer, name, status: "pending", refinement_count}` so the in-memory state matches what the DB row already holds. Compact trigger card renders → tap → overlay opens with action buttons. **Tests.** Five new contract tests in `src/lib/hooks/useChat.test.ts` pin the helper's contract — including a `status="pending"` assertion so a future refactor can't silently re-introduce the bug. **Files.** `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts` (extracted helper + call site; +40 lines), `src/lib/hooks/useChat.test.ts` (new, +109 lines). 751 tests pass (was 746; +5 new), build clean.
- Tier 1 prompt-architecture fixes for beta-ready intake (2026-05-15, suspicious-colden worktree): Four reliability fixes to the checkpoint pipeline so the situation / guided-intake flows hold their contract with the user. **(1) Silent-suppression rewrite.** When the material-quality gate or the Opus composition step suppressed a detected checkpoint, the transition line Jove had already written ("I want to put something in your Manual") still landed in chat — with no paired trigger card. Users read the promise and saw nothing happen. New `stripCheckpointFromText` helper in `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` strips the transition line and the reflection that followed; if a substantive landing preceded the line, it's preserved and a neutral grounding question is appended ("What was happening right before that landed?"), otherwise the full fallback runs. The DB row is updated in lockstep so chat history matches what's on screen. Wired at both suppression sites: post-gate failure (turn-count cooldown, material gate, pattern-engaged) and post-composition failure (Opus error / invalid layer). **(2) Broader detection regex.** `src/lib/persona/detect-checkpoint.ts` previously matched only three exact openers ("I want to put something/this/that in your Manual"). The model drifts under load — "I'd like to put", "I'm going to put", "Let me put", and "into your Manual" were all silent misses. Regex now accepts those paraphrases while keeping the `(something|this|that)` anchor against false positives (e.g. "you wanted to put more thought into your Manual entries" still does not match). **(3) Tightened `checkpointApproaching`.** In `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts`, the signal previously flipped true on any layer at `"emerging"` — the lowest possible bar. That primed Jove to load CHECKPOINTS instructions before the brief had material to back them up, which raised the rate of premature transition lines. Raised to `"explored"` or `"checkpoint_ready"` only. **(4) Decoupled checkpoint instructions from returning-user status.** `showCheckpointInstructions` in `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` was deriving from `(checkpointApproaching || isReturningUser)`. Every returning user got the full CHECKPOINTS + POST-REJECTION blocks loaded on turn 1 of every new session — before any material had surfaced.
