Now gated on `checkpointApproaching` alone; returning-user context still flows through the existing RETURNING USER block. **Net effect.** Three classes of broken-feeling moments removed from the intake experience: stranded transition lines without cards, missed checkpoint paraphrases, and premature checkpoint priming on turn 1 of returning sessions. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (+61 lines: `stripCheckpointFromText` helper + suppression rewrites at gate-fail and composition-fail), `src/lib/persona/detect-checkpoint.ts` (regex broadened), `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts` (signal threshold raised), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (gate flipped in both `buildSystemPrompt` and `buildSystemPromptBlocks`), plus test + snapshot updates across `call-persona.test.ts`, `detect-checkpoint.test.ts`, `system-prompt.test.ts`, `system-prompt.tier3.test.ts`. 746 tests pass (was 737; +9 new for the suppression helper + regex variants + gate-flip assertions). Build clean.
- Checkpoint hardening — three reliability fixes for beta (2026-05-15, priceless-shockley worktree): Closing the last load-bearing gaps in the checkpoint pipeline after the full beta-readiness audit. **(1) Reject / refine / defer is now idempotent.** Before this change `/api/checkpoint/confirm` for non-confirmed actions did an unguarded `UPDATE` on `messages.checkpoint_meta` + `INSERT` of the action system message. A double-tap (or mobile slow-network re-send before the overlay closes) double-incremented `refinement_count` and inserted two `[User rejected/refined/let it go the checkpoint]` rows, which then both flowed into Jove's next-turn context. The route now reads `checkpoint_meta.status` and no-ops when it is anything other than `"pending"` — returning `{alreadyHandled: true, currentStatus}` with status 200 and an `outcome: "idempotent"` log line. Same idempotency shape as the confirmed path, which had it via the RPC's `FOR UPDATE` lock + partial unique index since Track 2. **(2) Rate limiter hard-fails at module load in production.** `src/lib/rate-limit.ts` previously logged a console warning and silently failed open if `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL` / `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_TOKEN` were unset. Now it `throw`s in `process.env.VERCEL_ENV === "production"` — making the very first request to any route that imports the module 500 immediately, with a clear "FATAL: configure Upstash" message in Vercel logs. Local dev and preview deploys keep the soft fail-open with a warning so devs without Upstash credentials can still run the app. Closes the slow-burn outage path where bot traffic or runaway clients could drain the Anthropic budget unnoticed because the rate limiters were no-ops. **(3) Stream outcome log fires when the SSE stream actually closes.** The confirmed-path was logging `outcome: "success"` synchronously right after `callPersona()` returned the `ReadableStream` object — not after the follow-up stream finished delivering. A mid-stream interruption after a successful DB write would still show as success in the metrics, inflating the apparent success rate. The route now wraps `stream.pipeThrough(TransformStream)` so `flush()` fires the success outcome on natural close, and a second `ReadableStream` observer catches upstream errors and emits a `stream_interrupted` outcome with the error detail. The DB write still happens before either path runs — this is purely an observability accuracy fix, no user-visible behavior change. **Files.** `src/app/api/checkpoint/confirm/route.ts` (idempotency guard + stream wrap; +130 lines), `src/app/api/checkpoint/confirm/route.test.ts` (3 new tests covering rejected/refined/already-confirmed idempotency; +91 lines), `src/lib/rate-limit.ts` (production hard-fail; +14 lines). 737 tests pass (was 734; +3 new), build clean.
- Admin polish — waitlist auto-cleanup, feedback filters, health reorder, prompt-arch redesign (2026-05-15, nice-merkle worktree): Four small but user-asked changes across the admin surface. **(1) Waitlist auto-cleanup on allowlist add.** `POST /api/admin/beta-allowlist` previously updated the matching waitlist row's status to `"invited"` and left it on the list. Now it deletes the waitlist row outright — by id when invoked from a waitlist row's "Add to beta" button, by email when invoked from the manual allowlist add form. `useAdminData.addToBeta` mirrors the deletion in local state so the UI doesn't need to refetch the waitlist. Verified live: `dedupe-test@example.com` clicked from waitlist → row vanished from waitlist, appeared on allowlist within ~1s. **(2) Feedback section filter / sort / delete.** New unified `src/components/admin/FeedbackSection.tsx` replaces the inline beta + in-app rendering in `src/app/admin/page.tsx`. Three chip-row filters across the top — Source (All / Beta / In-app), Status (All / Unread / Read), Sort (Newest / Oldest) — with a `M of N` count on the right. Beta and in-app rows are merged into one list with a small `BETA` / `IN-APP` source pill on each. Per-row two-step Delete (button → "Delete? Confirm / Cancel") wired to new `DELETE` endpoints on `/api/admin/beta-feedback?id=` and `/api/admin/feedback?id=`. New hook actions `deleteBetaFeedback` + `deleteUserFeedback` patch local state without refetch (and decrement the unread badge if a deleted row was unread). The legacy `BetaFeedbackTab` component is no longer used by `page.tsx` but the file is retained because it exports the `BetaFeedbackRow` type consumed by `FeedbackSection` + the hook. **(3) Health page reorder + collapsed Migrations.** Removed the `FeedbackPanel` ("Beta feedback" inside Health) — feedback now lives only on the dedicated Feedback nav section, which already badges the unread count on the rail. New Health order: ConfirmHealthPanel → ApiErrorsPanel → ActiveUsersPanel → SchemaHealthTab. Migrations is now collapsible and **collapsed by default** with a one-line summary in the closed header (`Migrations · In sync · N applied` in green, or `Migrations · Drift · N` in error). The migration-status fetch is gated on the panel being open — closing the panel skips the network call. To support that, `useAsyncFetch<T>(url)` now accepts `string | null` and short-circuits when null (loading=false, no fetch); the four other admin health panels still pass strings and behave identically. **(4) Prompt architecture redesign.** Two changes the user called out: text was too small, and the three layer headers should be color-differentiated like the Manual page's gradient bands. Per-layer `LayerTheme` palette: Foundation (warm walnut gradient `rgba(180,125,75,0.46) → rgba(135,88,52,0.34)` matching the Manual band), Voice (lighter caramel `rgba(220,170,120,0.40) → rgba(180,135,90,0.28)`), Lifecycle (sage `rgba(156,177,138,0.44) → rgba(94,122,79,0.32)`). Each header now uses the manual's idiom — Roman numeral (I., II., III.) in italic Spectral 20px in the layer's accent color, title bumped from 17px to 22px Spectral, subtitle from 12.5px to 14px on `--session-ink-soft` (was `--session-ink-faded`). Section/Lifecycle card preview text bumped from 12.5px to 14px line-height 1.65, label rows from 11px to 12.5px, token meta from 10px to 11.5px, condition pills from 9px to 10.5px. Cards take a faint per-layer surface tint (`--session-walnut-surface-soft` / `--session-walnut-tint` / `--session-persona-tint`) and the fade-out gradient at the bottom of truncated previews now blends to the tint instead of `--session-linen` so the seal reads correctly. "Show full text" links and "Expand all" pill use the layer's accent color. InfoModal grew the source line + body text (13px → 15px line-height 1.75) and the modal header now wears the layer's gradient band so info popups feel cohesive with the layer they came from. **Cross-cutting note.** No DB migrations, no API contract changes for existing consumers (only added DELETE endpoints to two routes that previously had GET + PATCH). 734 tests pass, build clean.
- Bloom-light / Hearth-dark desktop canvas (2026-05-15, nervous-hugle worktree): New background treatment for the DesktopVitrine outer canvas — phone surface, frame, and shadow untouched. **Pairing.** Same gradient grammar in both themes — centered warm radial bloom on a warm-walnut surround, inverted brightness. Light is Bloom (saddle `#b88a55 → #a07644 → #7f5a30` with a cream radial `rgba(255, 240, 210, 0.55)` centered at 50% 50%); dark is Hearth (deep tobacco `#1c130b → #130b06 → #080502` with a warm cream-walnut radial `rgba(220, 165, 110, 0.18)`). Light + dark read as day and night of the same room. **CSS shape.** Background moved from inline `style` on DesktopVitrine's outer `<div>` into a new `.mw-vitrine-canvas` class in `globals.css` with rules for `:root` (dark = Hearth) and `[data-theme="light"]` (= Bloom). **Paratext tokens.** New `--session-paratext`, `--session-paratext-mid`, `--session-paratext-spine`, `--session-paratext-strong` defined in both theme blocks — masthead and colophon copy stay cream-on-warm-dark in both themes (the surround is warm-walnut regardless of theme, so paratext doesn't flip espresso/cream). The phone interior remains the true light-vs-dark surface; the canvas is just the framing context. Measured contrast >6.5:1 light / >14:1 dark on paratext. **Mobile unaffected.** Phone fills viewport below 431px so the canvas is covered, and `.mw-desktop-paratext` stays `display: none` below 431px so the new paratext tokens render nowhere on mobile. **Files.** `src/app/globals.css` (+42 lines: paratext tokens in both theme blocks, new `.mw-vitrine-canvas` rule), `src/components/layout/DesktopVitrine.tsx` (inline background → className; 6 token references swapped). 734 tests pass, build clean.
- Semantic bubble tokens — Jove navy in dark, cream in light (2026-05-14, jove-navy-dark branch): Restores Jove's navy character in dark mode that was lost when the earlier "swap bubble colors + Stone input card" change (2026-05-14, 16a55b0) moved Jove onto `--session-walnut-surface`. **Root cause.** The legacy `--session-bubble-user` token has different character per theme: rgba(72, 80, 98, 0.30) [slate/navy] in dark, rgba(92, 58, 30, 0.10) [walnut wash] in light. Because Bubble.tsx pointed Jove at `--session-walnut-surface` and User at `--session-bubble-user`, the Jove and User assignments inadvertently coupled navy and walnut wash under a single variable — meaning each bubble changed character across themes (Jove went warm in BOTH themes; User went walnut in light but navy in dark). The user's intent was that walnut should be the consistent user-bubble character and navy/cool should be the consistent Jove character. **Fix.** Added four new tokens with consistent semantics per speaker, defined in both dark `:root` and `[data-theme="light"]` blocks: `--session-jove-bg` (slate-navy in dark / cream in light); `--session-jove-border` (cool ink in dark / walnut in light); `--session-user-bg` (walnut in dark / walnut wash in light); `--session-user-border` (walnut in both). `Bubble.tsx` switched to read the new tokens. **What stays.** Existing `--session-bubble-user` (used by ChatInput.tsx and a few other places), `--session-walnut-surface` (used by plates, modals, drawer, etc.), and all their borders are unchanged so non-bubble consumers don't regress. Light-mode bubble values are byte-identical to the previous shipped state — only dark mode changes. **Net effect.** Dark: Jove navy (rgba(72, 80, 98, 0.30)), User walnut (rgba(115, 72, 42, 0.20)) — matches user's pre-swap memory. Light: unchanged (Jove cream, User walnut wash). **Files.** `src/app/globals.css` (+22 lines across the two theme blocks), `src/components/shared/Bubble.tsx` (4 token references swapped). 734 tests pass, build clean.
- Publication-polished Manual page + chip-lifecycle fix (2026-05-14, nice-borg worktree): Full visual + interaction redesign of the Manual page implementing `mockups/manual-empty-state/publication-polished.html`, plus a fix to a pre-existing race condition where the exploration chip silently dismissed itself. **Manual page redesign.** New publication-style header — Spectral 500/48px "Your Manual." with the walnut period accent, italic Spectral 15.5px subtitle "A document about how you operate, in your own voice.", § ornament centered between two `--session-hair-soft` rules. The day-one italic placeholder and the "N entries" eyebrow are gone — the subtitle and the five layer cards carry the structural promise themselves. **Empty Layer** rewrite (`EmptyLayer.tsx`): copper-gradient header (same locked gradient as `PopulatedLayer`) carrying Roman + Layer name only — no info button, no count badge. Body card under the header on `--session-walnut-tint` with the layer's description in Roman Spectral 15.5px in full `--session-ink`, italic example phrases after the em-dash, a dotted `--session-hair-faint` divider, and a right-aligned italic CTA — "*Nothing documented yet — explore with Jove →*" (state portion in `--session-ink-faded`, action portion in full ink, walnut chevron). Whole card is the tap target → routes through `onExploreWithPersona({ type: "empty_layer", content: layer.about })`. `MobileManual` now plumbs the explore callback through to `EmptyLayer` (it previously only reached `PopulatedLayer`). The chat pipeline already handled `type: "empty_layer"` end-to-end — Jove opens by framing what the Layer covers and asking a concrete entry question. **Populated Layer** (`PopulatedLayer.tsx`): same copper gradient header. The count badge in the top-right is replaced by an 18px ⓘ info button bordered with `--session-walnut-meta` carrying an italic Spectral "i". Tapping opens a 270px popover anchored to the button — dark-mode `--session-parchment` background / light-mode `--session-cream`, `--session-walnut-border`, new `--session-popover-shadow` token. Popover holds a DM Mono `LAYER {N}` mono-caps label + the layer description (`layer.about`) in Roman Spectral 14.5px. Rotated-square arrow at top-right points back to the button. Click-outside-to-close via document mousedown/touchstart listeners. `readOnly` mode (used by `AdminManualView`) omits the info button entirely. Outer card border removed — the gradient header carries enough visual weight to separate layers. **Entries** (`EntryItem.tsx`): restyled per spec. Collapsed entries are italic Spectral 15.5px/500 with a small `›` chevron right-aligned. Expanded entries grow the title to italic Spectral 19.5px/500, rotate the chevron 180°, and reveal the body in Roman Spectral 15px line-height 1.65 in full `--session-ink` (was `--session-ink-soft`). Dotted `--session-hair` divider between entries via `isLast` (the legacy `showDivider` prop is preserved as a no-op for compatibility). The per-entry "Explore further" link inside expanded bodies is kept — gives users a direct route from an individual entry into chat about that specific pattern. **Footer** ("Continue with Jove →") was added during build, then removed at user request — the page now ends at the Share invitation card. **3 new --session-* tokens** in both dark + light blocks: `--session-walnut-tint` (faint walnut wash for empty-Layer body), `--session-hair-faint` (sub-hairline inside cards), `--session-popover-shadow` (lift for the info popover). All values pulled from the spec. **Chip duplication fix** (`MobileSession.tsx`): the in-chat exploration chip rendered two rows — eyebrow ("LAYER ONE · SOME OF MY PATTERNS") + label (`name || layerName`). For `empty_layer` exploration `name` is undefined, so the second row duplicated the eyebrow ("Some of My Patterns" under "...SOME OF MY PATTERNS"). The second row is now gated on `currentExploration.name` — empty-Layer chips show one clean line; entry chips still show both rows. **Chip race-condition fix** (`MainApp.tsx`): the chip-lifecycle effect captured `conversationId` on the render where `currentExploration` first became truthy, then dismissed the chip on any subsequent change. But `startExploration` does `setConversationId(null)` before the new exploration-conversation id arrives via the stream's completeEvent — so the chip captured the OLD chat conv id, then saw the null → newExpId transition as a mismatch and dismissed itself before the user ever saw it. Visible whenever the user explored from inside an active chat (the empty-data path masked the bug because `conversationId` started null). Added a `explorationPendingBindRef` flag that `handleExploreWithPersona` arms before `setCurrentExploration`; the effect waits past the null intermediate, then captures the next non-null `conversationId` (the new exploration conv). Other dismissal paths (new session, drawer-pick a different conv, explicit X) still work because they don't go through pending-bind state. **Files.** `src/app/globals.css` (+12 tokens), `src/components/mobile/manual/{EmptyLayer,PopulatedLayer,EntryItem}.tsx` (full rewrites of the first two; restyle of the third), `src/components/mobile/MobileManual.tsx` (new header, layer-list edge bleed, no eyebrow, callback plumbing), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (chip duplication fix), `src/components/MainApp.tsx` (chip race fix). 734 tests pass, build clean.
- Paper-halo dark — chat surface tone-down for dark mode (2026-05-14, paper-halo-dark branch): Dark-mode analog of the light-mode whisper-walnut shipped earlier the same day. Same goal — recede the walnut bath while keeping a warm halo behind the masthead — but tuned for the dark palette (walnut + slate on graphite). **Token changes (dark mode only).** Only `--session-bg-chat` changes — the three contained walnut surfaces (Jove bubble + plate + chat input via `--session-walnut-surface`) intentionally stay at their current alpha because they're the brand-character surfaces, not the bath. Specifically: radial 1 (caramel top-center) tightened from `ellipse 130% 60%` to `ellipse 110% 45%` and alpha dialed from 0.55 → 0.32 so the walnut doesn't bleed past the upper third; radial 2 (slate pool) expanded from `ellipse 90% 55% at 50% 95%` to `ellipse 120% 70% at 50% 90%` and alpha deepened from 0.85 → 0.92 so the slate owns the lower two-thirds; radial 3 (deep walnut top-right) UNCHANGED at 0.40 — this is the masthead halo, intentionally kept. **Rationale.** After shipping whisper-walnut for light mode, the user asked to explore similar tone-down approaches for dark mode. Three options were mocked side-by-side: (A) Paper Halo concentrating walnut at top with slate dominant below, surfaces untouched; (B) Whisper Walnut dialing every walnut alpha down including the surface wash; (C) Smoked Walnut shifting the walnut HUE cooler (115/72/42 → 96/70/50). Paper Halo (A) was chosen — it holds dark mode's brand identity by keeping the walnut surfaces intact while letting the page recede. The other two are preserved as mockups at `public/mocks/dark-walnut-{A,B,C}.html`. **Light mode untouched** — its tokens already settled by the previous whisper-walnut pass. **Files.** `src/app/globals.css` (9 lines, dark-mode block only — comment added explaining the halo logic). 734 tests pass, build clean.
- Specific acknowledgment bubble before trigger card (2026-05-14, happy-buck worktree): User testing flagged that the trigger card appeared cold after vulnerable disclosures — no reflective beat between "user shares specific thing" and "here's a structured artifact." The forming-state indicator ("Something is forming…") filled wait time but didn't demonstrate Jove had heard specifics. The behavioral function: after a vulnerable specific disclosure, the next move should quote a phrase or moment from the user's words before pivoting to the artifact. Reflect before redirect. **(1) Composition output gets a new `acknowledgment` field.** Opus produces one sentence (12-22 words), second-person, quotes a specific phrase or moment from the user's last 1-2 turns, ends with the contractual signal ("I want to mark this," "I want to put this down," or close variant). Length-validated and gracefully empty when Opus declines for lack of usable specifics — better silence than a generic bubble. Example: user shared about checking the monstera with no memory of starting → "That last bit — finding yourself at the monstera with no memory of starting — that's the part I want to mark." **(2) Server emits it as a regular Jove chat bubble** between composition complete and the trigger card's message_complete event. Saved to DB with created_at backdated 1s before the checkpoint message so time-ordered reload reads acknowledgment → card. **(3) Removed (now redundant): the transient `composing` SSE event + "Something is forming…" typing-indicator label.** The acknowledgment bubble replaces that beat with specific content. Also removed: client `composingMessage` state in useChat, prop threading through MainApp → MobileSession, `onComposing` callback in sse-parser. **(4) Removed: client-side "Building a suggested entry…" → "Suggested entry ready." indicator inside the trigger card.** That was a 2.2s `checkpointReady` timer plus a dot + text indicator added back when the card was the only post-detection signal. Now the acknowledgment bubble carries the reflective beat; the card appears immediately when activeCheckpoint lands. Typing indicator simplified back to glyph-only. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts` (acknowledgment prompt + return type + length validation), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (emission + composing event removal), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.test.ts` (4 new contract tests), `src/lib/utils/sse-parser.ts` (onComposing removed), `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts` (composingMessage state removed), `src/components/MainApp.tsx` (prop removed), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (building indicator + checkpointReady timer removed, typing indicator simplified). Resolved one cross-worktree conflict with the whisper-walnut chat surface change (kept main's `${PERSONA_NAME} is typing` aria-label, dropped composingMessage branch). Pre-existing test/source mismatch on `parseStreamUsage(event)` → `onUsage` fixed inline as merge collateral. 734 tests pass (was 730; +4 acknowledgment contract tests). Build clean.
- Whisper-walnut — chat surface tone-down (2026-05-14, whisper-walnut branch): Light-mode-only adjustment to recede the walnut warmth across the chat surface while keeping the warm halo behind the masthead. Same gradient shape, lower weight everywhere else. **Token changes (light mode only).** `--session-bg-chat` radial alphas: caramel top-center 0.58 → 0.40; cream bottom-center 0.85 → 0.92 (the cream pool DEEPENS so the lower viewport feels paler); deep walnut top-right 0.38 → 0.25. `--session-bubble-user`: rgba(92, 58, 30, 0.16) → rgba(92, 58, 30, 0.10) — the user bubble now reads as a warm tint rather than a chocolate panel. The walnut border stays at 0.32 alpha so the ink spine reads on both bubbles and surfaces. **Rationale.** The user feedback was that the chat surface read as "too much walnut everywhere — make it more elegant, but keep the walnut by the logo header." Three approaches were mocked side-by-side (Paper Halo concentrating walnut at top; Whisper Walnut dialing every alpha down; Vellum + Spine going aggressive parchment with a cool user bubble). Whisper Walnut was chosen — it preserves the current character and gradient shape, just at lower weight. The other two are recorded in mockup form at `public/mocks/walnut-{A,B,C}.html` for future reference. **Dark mode untouched** — the dark-mode tokens already use a different walnut hue + slate balance and don't share the same feedback. **Files.** `src/app/globals.css` (8 lines, light-mode block only). 730 tests pass, build clean.
- Codebase-wide simplification — passes 1 + 2 (2026-05-14, tender-allen worktree): Two cleanup passes plus follow-up batches, focused on dedupe and removing dead state without changing user-facing behavior. **New helpers.** `extractResponseText(response)` in `src/lib/anthropic.ts` (replaces 4 inline `response.content[0].type === "text" ? ...` repetitions); `requireAdmin()` in `src/lib/admin/verify-admin.ts` (admin guard returning client + userId or Response — sibling to the later-shipped `requireUser`); `listAllAuthUsers(admin)` in `src/lib/admin/list-auth-users.ts` (centralizes `listUsers({perPage:1000})` + emailMap construction across 4 admin routes that hand-rolled it); `isValidEmail(email)` in `src/lib/beta-allowlist.ts` (replaces duplicated regex in waitlist + admin/beta-allowlist); `firstNameOrNull(name)` in `src/lib/utils/name.ts` (sibling to `firstNameFrom`; null fallback for linq sites that decide whether to address by name); `useAsyncFetch<T>(url)` in `src/lib/hooks/useAsyncFetch.ts` (data-side dedup for 4 admin health panels); `renderManualEntryFull(entry)` in `src/lib/manual/layers.ts` (canonical "Manual entry in a prompt" rendering). **Centralized constants.** 5 model IDs (`PERSONA_MODEL`, `EXTRACTION_MODEL`, `COMPOSITION_MODEL`, `SUMMARY_MODEL`, `SIMULATION_MODEL`) moved into `src/lib/persona/config.ts`; `CHECKPOINT_ACTIONS` map (system message + natural reply paired) replaces stringly-typed if/else in `mapSystemMessages`; `ConversationMode` + `CONVERSATION_MODES` tuple, `CheckpointAction` type, `PersonaMode` import replace duplicate inline literals across 6+ files; `LAYER_ROMAN` constant moved from EmptyLayer + PopulatedLayer into `layer-definitions.ts`. **Migrations.** 13 admin routes to `requireAdmin()`; 4 admin routes to `listAllAuthUsers()`; 4 admin health panels to `useAsyncFetch`; voice rule "Jove" hardcoded → `PERSONA_NAME` templated in 4 voice files + voice-scaffold (will invalidate Anthropic prompt cache once on next request); stale "Sage" comments swept to "Jove" across 8 files. **Perf wins.** `createAdminClient()` module-scope cached (saves Supabase constructor on every API route + webhook on warm Edge instances); `mentionsPersona` regex hoisted to module load (was recompiling per call on group-message hot path); `Promise.all` in `checkpoint/confirm/route.ts` (msg + conv lookups now parallel, with sanity check that conv.id matches msg.conversation_id) and in `conversations/route.ts` (latest/count/firstText queries); `useMemo` for `refinementCeilingActive` (MobileSession) and `buildLayers` (MobileManual); MobileSettings phone fetch deferred until Settings tab visited (no longer fires on app load for users who never visit Settings); `manual_viewed` analytics fires once per tab visit instead of duplicating after every checkpoint confirm; `conversations/route.ts` count-only query no longer reads message content (drops payload for power users with long text-channel histories). **Dead code removed.** `hasManualContext` flag (redundant with `manualComponents.length > 0`); `modalStateLoading` state (admitted-unused); `LEAD_IN_LINE` dead ternary; `ManualEntryForContext` interface collapsed to `ManualEntry` alias; unused `getLayer()` helper; `{true && (...)}` wrappers in MobileSettings (3 sites); duplicate `ROMAN` constant. **Phone normalization.** Added `@deprecated` JSDoc on `normalizePhone` pointing future code at `normalizeUSToE164` — documents the divergence (always-prefixed vs null-on-invalid) without touching the 11 linq/messaging call sites that depend on the always-prefixed semantic. Full migration deferred (HIGH risk; see `docs/deferred-refactors.md`). **New documentation.** `docs/deferred-refactors.md`: register of items considered but explicitly deferred during the simplification passes, with value/risk/trigger for each. Includes the items the parallel sessions also deferred (small follow-ups to `guided-intake-copy.test.ts`, `buildSystemPrompt` wrapper collapse) so future-you has one place to find the open work. **Merge integration.** Pass 1 + 2 work was authored on a worktree based on a week-old main; merge integrated cleanly with the parallel SSE / BuildPromptOptions / buildTier3 / requireUser / prompt-caching sessions that landed independently. 7 conflicts resolved (mostly accepting main's structural restructures and layering pass-1's dedupe on top); zero behavior conflicts. 730 tests pass, 8 pre-existing TS errors unchanged, build clean. Net feature-branch delta: 596 insertions / 697 deletions (−101 LOC) across 80 files.
- Anthropic prompt caching on chat + extraction system prompts (2026-05-14, vigilant-johnson worktree): The Jove system prompt is now sent to Anthropic as a three-block `system` array with a `cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" }` marker on the largest stable block. **Block layout.** `tier1` = intro + Tier 1 constitutional rules (smallest, never changes). `staticContext` = Tier 2 voice + compressed older Manual entries (stable per session unless persona modes change or a new entry is confirmed; carries the marker). `dynamic` = Tier 3 mechanics + current-session Manual entries + session/extraction/transcript/exploration context (changes every turn, never cached). **New API.** `buildSystemPromptBlocks(opts: OneOnOnePromptOptions)` in `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` returns `{ tier1, staticContext, dynamic }`. The group-chat path keeps using `buildGroupPrompt` directly (no caching — group sessions are too short and varied for the 5-minute window). Legacy `buildSystemPrompt` (now narrowed by `options.kind === "group"`) is unchanged for admin renderer + Linq bridges + simulate-user + all other non-hot-path callers. `prepareManualContextBlocks(entries, currentConversationId)` in `src/lib/persona/manual-context.ts` returns `{ recent, older }`; the legacy `prepareManualContext` wraps it and reproduces byte-identical output. **Wired surfaces.** `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (chat hot path, streaming) constructs `SystemBlock[]` with one cache_control marker; `src/lib/persona/extraction.ts` (per-turn classifier) does the same on the EXTRACTION_SYSTEM constant. Confirm-checkpoint, generate-summary, simulate-user, group-bridge, and persona-bridge are intentionally NOT cached (sparse calls / dev-only / deprecating). **anthropic.ts changes.** `AnthropicRequest.system` accepts `string | SystemBlock[]`; `AnthropicResponse.usage` exposes `cache_creation_input_tokens` + `cache_read_input_tokens` + `input_tokens` + `output_tokens`. New `parseStreamUsage(event)` extracts token counts from streaming `message_start` + `message_delta` SSE events. **SSE plumbing.** `parseAnthropicStream` in `src/lib/anthropic-sse.ts` gained an optional `onUsage` callback — call-persona uses it to capture token counts inside the shared parser loop rather than re-implementing event parsing. No `anthropic-beta` header needed — prompt caching is GA. **Observability.** New `cache_performance` event in `src/lib/observability/log.ts` carries surface (`chat` | `extraction`), model, and all four token fields. Logged once per chat stream after the response completes and once per extraction call. **Live verification (2026-05-14, devtest@test.com, two consecutive chat turns within ~30s).** Chat turn 1: `cache_read=0 cache_create=3493 uncached_input=4459` (miss, prefix cached). Chat turn 2: `cache_read=3493 cache_create=4502 uncached_input=15` (HIT — 3493 tokens served from cache, ~91% of input now cached or near-zero). Extraction turn 1: `cache_read=0 cache_create=3931 uncached_input=2913` (miss). Extraction turn 2: `cache_read=3931 cache_create=0 uncached_input=2971` (clean HIT — no new cache write needed). **Cost math.** At Sonnet's published rates and a typical 10-turn session, the chat path saves ~9 × 3500 × 0.90 = ~28,400 cached tokens × ~$3/M = ~$0.085 per session; extraction saves ~9 × 3931 × 0.90 = ~31,800 cached tokens per session. Roughly 70-80% reduction in chat+extraction input cost across the active portion of a session. **Tests.** +31 tests (730 total post-merge). New: stability test (`buildSystemPromptBlocks` byte-identical across calls with same inputs), block-separation tests (Tier 3 NOT in staticContext, recent entries NOT in staticContext, older entries IN staticContext, identical staticContext across turnCount + checkpointApproaching changes), `prepareManualContextBlocks` split tests, source-contract tests asserting array-form system + exactly one cache_control marker in both call-persona.ts and extraction.ts, `parseStreamUsage` tests, `logEvent` cache_performance smoke. Build clean. Files: `src/lib/anthropic.ts`, `src/lib/anthropic.test.ts` (new), `src/lib/anthropic-sse.ts` (onUsage callback), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts`, `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.test.ts`, `src/lib/persona/manual-context.ts`, `src/lib/persona/manual-context.test.ts`, `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts`, `src/lib/persona/call-persona.test.ts`, `src/lib/persona/extraction.ts`, `src/lib/persona/extraction.test.ts`, `src/lib/observability/log.ts`, `src/lib/observability/log.test.ts`.
- Chat surface composition — Jove/user bubble color swap + Stone input card (2026-05-14, chat-input-stone-card branch): Two coordinated changes to how the chat surface reads. **(1) Bubble color swap.** `Bubble.tsx` exchanges the surface tokens between `JOVE_STYLE` and `USER_STYLE`: Jove now uses `--session-walnut-surface` (the lighter cream / paper tone), user bubbles use `--session-bubble-user` (the warm walnut wash). Reverses the 2026-05-13 "Bubble color flip." Rationale: Jove's voice reads as quiet and spacious — pairs better with the cream / paper surface — while the user's contributions sit on the warmer walnut wash. As a happy side-effect the `--session-bubble-user` token name now correctly matches its consumer color, including the chat input (next change) which keeps that token. Borders swapped to match (`--session-bubble-border` ↔ `--session-bubble-user-border`). Both light and dark mode contrast verified — the sage `JOVE` eyebrow has strong contrast on the cream surface in both themes. **(2) Stone input card.** Replaces the 999px walnut pill + TextBtn "SEND ›" (caps-mono with ink underline) with a soft 21px walnut-cream card and a bare 18px stroke up-arrow glyph at bottom-right. All three button states (mic / stop-recording / send) now share the same 44px transparent hit area (`padding: 13px; margin: -13px`) — iOS HIG preserved while the visible glyph stays at 18px and the row stays tight. The stop-recording state strips its previous 32px dark-circle well to a bare 14px sage rounded square with the existing `voicePulse` animation. Card surface switched from `--session-bubble-user` to `--session-walnut-surface` so the input visually pairs with Jove's bubbles — both read as "paper / writing surface." Border and shadow switched accordingly (`--session-bubble-border`, `--session-bubble-shadow`, lighter than the previous `--session-plate-shadow`). Card grows vertically with the textarea via `align-items: flex-end`, so the send glyph stays anchored bottom-right as the user composes long-form. `min-height: 56px` for a comfortable resting state; `max-height: 240px` caps card growth. Textarea max-height in `useLayoutEffect` bumped from `lineHeight * 3.5` (~91px, 3.5 lines) to `lineHeight * 6` (~156px, 6 lines), past which the textarea scrolls internally so the conversation above stays visible. Padding tightened from `10px 14px` (uniform pill) to `12px 8px 12px 16px`; gap between textarea and button compressed from 10px to 4px. Mobile result on a 375 viewport (343px-wide card): writing area went from 281px to **295px** (+14px); distance from visible glyph to card right edge went from 15px to 9px — comparable to iMessage's tight composition spec. Placeholder font-size 15→16 to match the Spectral italic typing register, repositioned `bottom: 14px` rather than vertically centered so it lands where typed text will sit. **Files.** `src/components/shared/Bubble.tsx` (bubble swap), `src/components/mobile/ChatInput.tsx` (Stone card, button stripping, growth/scroll behaviour, padding tightening). 699 tests pass. Build clean.
- Authentic post-confirm + anti-universal-tone + simulator [END] (2026-05-14, happy-buck worktree): Three discrete fixes from user testing round 3 (the "I Disappear When Nobody Needs Carrying" / "I Spit the Signal Back" case + the simulator acknowledgment-loop). **(1) Post-confirm flow rewritten.** The old "In. A working name: 'X.' Yours to change." stamp + entries-summary line read as admin notifications, not Jove speaking. The title is already in the trigger card, the chat-history label already shows the layer; repeating in Jove's text was redundant. New shape (both first-confirm and subsequent): pinned `Saved.` opener + (first-confirm only) "A Manual takes time to build. Best results come from showing up daily over the next two weeks. You can change the name or sharpen the entry anytime." + a continue-or-pivot offer naming a specific thread from the just-finished conversation. Drops the previous round's forward-question requirement — after a heavy save the user might be tapped out, so the offer gives both paths (continue OR pivot, joined by "or") and lets the user choose. Wiring: dropped the templated Message 1 prepend in `confirm/route.ts`; rewrote both post-confirm `Tier3Block` render functions in the new data-driven pipeline; removed `postConfirmContext` from `BuildPromptOptions`, `Tier3Flags`, and `CallPersonaOptions` (no substitutions needed anymore); Phase B fallback rewritten to match the new shape ("Saved. / [scaffolding for first-confirm] / We could keep going with what we just touched, or pivot to something else if this is enough for now."); typing-indicator condition in `MobileSession` expanded to also fire when the last message is a checkpoint card so the user sees feedback during the post-confirm composition wait. **(2) Body anti-universal-tone rule (composition prompt).** The previous "scope of evidence" rule caught explicit "always / every / all / never" but missed phrasings that read universal without them: "every low-stakes moment," "every friendship," "in those rooms," "whenever this happens," "any time someone X." Now forbidden explicitly with bad → good rewrite examples inline ("In those rooms I'm scanning." → "In moments like that, I notice I'm scanning."). **(3) Headline anti-abstract-verb rule.** The previous round forbade body-as-agent ("Stomach Pushes Me to Fix the Call"); this round adds abstract / metaphorical verbs ("disappear," "vanish," "lose myself," "fade," "go missing," "come undone," "fall apart," "dissolve"). These describe a felt internal state, not an observable behavior. "I Disappear When Nobody Needs Carrying" added to bad examples; "I Steer Toward Problems When Friends Just Want to Chat," "I Get Restless When the Room Has No Job for Me," "I Build Heavy Topics When None Are There" added to good. The headline verb must describe what a friend watching the scene would see the user DO. **(4) Simulator [END] lenient match.** Bonus fix from the same testing — `dev-simulate/route.ts` now uses `userMessage.includes("[END]")` instead of `userMessage.trim() === "[END]"`. Haiku sometimes emits `[END]` at the tail of a longer goodbye ("I will. Thanks. [END]"); the strict equality check missed those and the loop kept running until both sides broke character into an acknowledgment loop ("Standing by." / "👋" / "Ready when you are."). Files: `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts`, `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (+ test, + tier3 snapshot), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (+ test), `src/app/api/checkpoint/confirm/route.ts`, `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx`, `src/app/api/dev-simulate/route.ts`. 699 tests pass (was 696; +3 new contract tests, 2 tier3 snapshots regenerated). Build clean.
- `buildTier3` data-driven block pipeline (2026-05-14, relaxed-bose worktree): Pure refactor — replaced the ~350-line if/else ladder in `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` that mutated a string accumulator with a `readonly Tier3Block[]` array of 13 entries (one per conditional or always-on block). Each entry has `{ id, shouldRender, render }`. `buildTier3` is now 6 LOC: filter + map + join. Block order, content, and join behavior preserved exactly so prompt output is byte-identical. The 13 blocks: `first-message`, `guided-intake`, `upload`, `returning-user`, `returning-user-first-turn-situation` (was nested in returning-user), `checkpoints`, `first-checkpoint`, `post-rejection`, `post-confirm-first-message-2`, `post-confirm-subsequent-single`, `adapting-short-answers` (always-on), `readiness-gate`, `clinical-and-tail` (always-on, includes the FIRST SESSION ternary on `isNewUser`). Each block is now independently inspectable and unit-testable. New `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.tier3.test.ts` adds 15 byte-level snapshot tests covering every block's firing combo (one per block in isolation plus a max-fire combo) — these are the safety net that proved byte-identity through the refactor. All 169 existing `system-prompt.test.ts` assertions pass unchanged. 696 tests pass (was 681; +15 new snapshot tests). Build clean. 8 pre-existing TS errors unchanged.
- SSE parser unification + BuildPromptOptions discriminated union (2026-05-14, happy-nobel worktree): Two pure refactors from a follow-up audit. **(1) SSE parser unification.** New `src/lib/anthropic-sse.ts` carries the hand-rolled `getReader()` + `TextDecoder` + buffer-split + `data:` parse loop that previously lived inline in `call-persona.ts`. Single-callback API (`onTextDelta`) — call-persona is the only consumer of Anthropic's UPSTREAM SSE protocol (`content_block_delta`/`text_delta`). Sibling to `parseSSEStream` in `src/lib/utils/sse-parser.ts`, which parses our DOWNSTREAM protocol (`text_delta`/`message_complete`/`error`). Different event vocabularies so they cannot share a parser. As a follow-on, migrated `src/app/api/dev-simulate/route.ts` to consume `parseSSEStream` (it was hand-rolling the same downstream loop). Net: every SSE consumer in the repo now routes through one of the two shared parsers. Streaming cadence preserved by construction (sync callback per delta = sync inline body per delta). **(2) BuildPromptOptions discriminated union.** `BuildPromptOptions` split into `OneOnOnePromptOptions | GroupPromptOptions` on a `kind` discriminator. The 1:1 logic in `buildSystemPrompt` is now type-narrowed after the `kind === "group"` early return — no more implicit `if (groupContext)` check inside the 1:1 destructure. Group caller in `src/lib/linq/group-bridge.ts` drops the seven 1:1-only placeholder fields it was passing. `buildPromptOptionsFromContext` returns `OneOnOnePromptOptions` so call sites pick up `kind: "oneOnOne"` for free via spread. Dropped the now-redundant `hasManualContext` flag from `groupContext` — the `manualComponents.length > 0` check already gates the MANUAL CONTEXT RULES block. Added a comment near the union flagging the `Partial<Union>` distribution gotcha (`Partial<A | B>` doesn't behave like `Partial<A> | Partial<B>` — both `prompt-sections.ts` and the test helper needed narrowing to a specific variant). One follow-up deferred to `docs/deferred-refactors.md`: `guided-intake-copy.test.ts` calls `buildSystemPrompt` with `ConversationContext`-shaped args (2 pre-existing TS errors); should be reconciled to `OneOnOnePromptOptions` shape. 681 tests pass. Build clean.
- `requireUser()` API auth helper (2026-05-14, hardcore-wilbur worktree): Pure refactor — extracted the repeated `createClient()` + `supabase.auth.getUser()` + 401 boilerplate from 16 invocations across 13 authenticated API routes into a single helper at `src/lib/auth/require-user.ts`. Mirrors the prior `verifyAdmin()` simplification pass. Caller pattern: `const auth = await requireUser(); if (auth instanceof Response) return auth; const { user } = auth;`. Two options handle the variants surfaced in the audit: `errorMessage` (lowercase `"unauthenticated"` for `modal-progress` + `onboarding-status`), and `onUnauthorized` callback (sync, fires before the 401 — used by `checkpoint/confirm` to emit its `confirm_outcome` telemetry log). `recordApiError` pattern stayed at the route boundary unchanged — callers capture `user.id` after the auth check. Net **-82 lines** across the API surface. Migrated: `account/delete`, `chat`, `checkpoint/confirm`, `conversations`, `conversations/complete`, `manual`, `modal-progress`, `onboarding-status`, `session/summary`, `settings/link-phone`, `user/phone` (×3), `user/phone/verify`, `voice/token`. Out of scope: `analytics/identity` (returns 200 + null hashedId on no user, not 401 — different shape), `dev-*` (looser auth), `webhooks/*` (signed payload, not user auth). No test changes needed — tests already mocked `@/lib/supabase/server` and the helper goes through that mock transparently. 681 tests pass. Build clean.
- Forming-state UX + post-confirm fallback + headline trigger requirement (2026-05-14, happy-buck worktree): Four follow-up fixes from a second round of user testing against the "I Swallow the Answer Before I Can Say It" checkpoint case (universalized title from one example, lead-in bubble serialized rather than replacing the card, dead-end after save). **(1) Forming state is now a transient typing-indicator label, not a persisted chat bubble.** New `composing` SSE event type emitted from the server right after deterministic detection passes but BEFORE the slow composition Opus call. The client (`useChat.ts`, `MobileSession.tsx`) renders "Something is forming…" inside the existing typing indicator alongside the ❦ glyph during the ~10-15s wait. Cleared automatically when the trigger card's `message_complete` event arrives — the card REPLACES the forming state instead of appearing alongside a separate bubble. The persisted lead-in assistant message ("A pattern came through in what you said.") that used to be inserted with a backdated timestamp is deleted entirely; existing rows in older conversations still render but no new ones are created. **(2) Headlines must name a specific trigger.** Stronger HEADLINE rule in the composition prompt: "Format: 'I [verb] when [specific trigger]' or 'I [verb] before [specific event]' or 'I [verb] after [specific moment].' The trigger names a concrete situation — a person, a moment, a sensation. Not 'before I can say it' (vague — what stops me?) but 'when guilt hits' or 'when someone waits for me to answer' (specific). The title should answer 'WHEN does this fire?' not just 'WHAT happens?'" Plus: if only one example is in evidence, soften with "can" or "sometimes" to avoid over-claiming. "I Swallow the Answer Before I Can Say It" added to bad examples; "I Can Freeze When Asked What I Want" added to good. **(3) Post-confirm Sonnet failures emit a deterministic fallback Message 2.** Previously when the post-confirm scaffolding LLM call failed, we closed the stream silently (preserving the entry's save success but leaving the user staring at Message 1 with no forward question). New behavior: `buildPostConfirmFallback` returns a templated Message 2 mirroring the prompt structure — pinned copy plus a generic forward question ("What's still open in this for you?"). For `first-message-2`: layer-name opener + the pinned "real Manual takes time" paragraph + the question. For `subsequent-single`: stamp line + entries summary + the question. The fallback is saved to DB so reload reads consistently. Sonnet wins when it works; the template wins when it doesn't. **(4) Saved-to label puts layer name first.** Chat-history `Plate` for confirmed checkpoints now renders `"Saved to <Layer Name> — Layer N"` instead of `"Saved to Layer N — <Layer Name>"`. The meaningful name leads ("SAVED TO SOME OF MY PATTERNS — LAYER 1"), the ordinal supports. Files: `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts` (headline), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (composing event + fallback + lead-in removal), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.test.ts` (fallback contract tests, replacing the old "closes silently" assertions), `src/lib/utils/sse-parser.ts` (composing event handler), `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts` (composingMessage state), `src/components/MainApp.tsx` (prop threading), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx` (typing-indicator label + saved-to label reorder). 681 tests pass (was 678; +3 new fallback contract tests). Build clean.
- Checkpoint voice + UX fixes from 2026-05-14 testing (2026-05-14, happy-buck worktree): Four discrete fixes surfaced by testing the refactored pipeline against a real checkpoint ("Stomach Pushes Me to Fix the Call" — universalized from one example, body-as-agent title, dead-end after save, layer label too sparse). **(1) Headline rules reject body-as-agent.** The previous "good" examples in the composition prompt ("Voice Goes When Pressure Lands," "Body Locks Before Being Asked") were themselves poetic — body parts acting as agents. Replaced with first-person behavior-as-subject examples: "I Switch to Counter-Mode When Talked At," "I Tighten Before Answering," "I Run Interference on Call Tone," "I Freeze Before Someone Can Ask," "I Keep Teaching Him How to Live." "Stomach Pushes Me to Fix the Call" added explicitly to the bad examples. Explicit rule added: subject of the headline is "I" or the behavior itself, NOT a body part as agent. **(2) Composition stays within scope of evidence.** New VOICE RULE: "Stay within the scope of evidence the user gave you. If they described one example (one person, one situation, one moment), anchor the entry IN that example — name the person, name the context. Do not generalize to 'everyone,' 'every time,' 'all conversations.' Do not use 'always,' 'every,' 'all,' 'never' unless the user used those exact words themselves. One example produces a specific claim, not a universal one. Two or three convergent examples can support a wider claim — but only as wide as the examples actually span." Addresses the case where one Ryan call produced "I always get sucked into fixing how someone talks to me" — the universal claim wasn't supported by the evidence. **(3) Post-confirm Message 2 must end with a forward-moving question.** Both `first-message-2` and `subsequent-single` blocks now require: "The final line MUST be a question. It MUST end with a question mark." The previous "open-thread line" framing produced soft declarations ("Worth circling back to," "There's more to explore here") that read as dead-ends after a successful save. Good/bad examples added inline so Opus knows the target — bad: "Worth circling back to." Good: "Where else does this same impulse fire — only with Ryan, or other places too?" The post-confirm pause now propels forward instead of stopping the conversation. **(4) Chat-history label includes layer name.** `MobileSession.tsx` `Plate` for confirmed checkpoints used to render `"Saved to Layer Four"` only. Now appends the layer name: `"SAVED TO LAYER FOUR — HOW I SHOW UP WITH PEOPLE"`. `LAYER_NAMES` was already imported. Files: `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts`, `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (+ two pinned test assertions updated to match new "MUST be a question" wording), `src/components/mobile/MobileSession.tsx`. 678 tests pass. Build clean.
- Checkpoint pipeline refactor — deterministic detection + overlay hardening + post-confirm silent failures (2026-05-14, happy-buck worktree): Three related changes that simplify the checkpoint surface and close several silent-failure modes uncovered in user testing. **(1) Deterministic detection replaces the Haiku classifier.** The system prompt defined "I want to put something in your Manual" as the contract with the user, but a separate Haiku call sat on top of that contract judging whether the message was "really" a checkpoint — requiring a 4-8 word headline, the right structure, an inferable layer. When Haiku disagreed with the contract (e.g. the user-observed "Proof-Giving as Protection" case where the 3-word hyphenated headline tripped the gate), the card silently failed to surface even though Jove had committed to the user with a full proposal. New `src/lib/persona/detect-checkpoint.ts` is a regex on the transition line + minor variants ("I want to put this/that in your Manual"), case-insensitive, end-of-sentence anchored so stray references in unrelated prose don't match. `classifier.ts` and `classifier.test.ts` deleted. One fewer Haiku call per turn. **(2) Composition picks the layer.** Previously `composeManualEntry` took `layer` + `name` as inputs (from the classifier). Now it takes the full `manualComponents` (the user's Manual so far) and Opus picks both — based on the entry content and how it relates to entries already on each layer. Composition output adds a required `layer` field (1-5, validated); invalid layer → null return → checkpoint suppressed. Headline policy (the 4-8 words rule that tripped the old classifier) moved fully into the composition prompt where Opus is rewriting anyway. **(3) Gates simplified.** `applyCheckpointGates` no longer carries layer/name pass-throughs — returns `{ passed, reason }` only. Material-quality + turn-count gates still suppress before composition (cheap pre-emit check). `buildCheckpointMeta` takes `composedEntry` directly. **(4) CheckpointOverlay auto-close fix.** The 1.6s confirmed-cover dismiss timer was using `onClose` as an effect dependency. Because the parent (`MobileSession`) passed `onClose` as an inline arrow function, every parent re-render produced a new closure → effect re-ran → `clearTimeout + setTimeout`. During the post-confirm stream the parent re-renders constantly, so the timer kept resetting and the overlay sat stuck on the "Added to Layer N" cover. Fix: `onCloseRef` ref pattern, effect deps shrunk to `[confirmStatus, open]`. Escape also moved to the ref and now dismisses in every phase as a safety valve (was gated on `phase === "actions"` so a stuck confirmed cover had no escape hatch). **(5) Post-confirm scaffolding failures are silent.** Phase A (the manual_entries RPC write) is atomic + idempotent and always succeeds independently. Phase B (the post-confirm Sonnet call that produces the "scaffolding" follow-up message) was sharing the same SSE stream — when it failed, `emitError` fired "Jove lost the thread" and the client showed a "Connection lost" plate with a RETRY button wired to `retryLastMessage`, which would re-send a stale user message from earlier. Fix: `callPersona`'s top-level catch now branches on `postConfirmMode !== null` — closes the stream cleanly without emitting an error event. The entry stays saved, the prepended Message 1 ("In. A working name: 'X.' Yours to change.") stays in chat, conversation continues on the next turn. **(6) FirstCheckpointModal removed.** Dead code — not imported in production, replaced by inline trigger card + overlay. Removed: component, test, demo screen, two analytics functions (`trackModal3Shown`, `trackModalFlowCompleted`), comment references in `system-prompt.ts`. **Files.** New: `src/lib/persona/detect-checkpoint.ts` + test, `src/components/checkpoint/CheckpointOverlay.test.ts`. Modified: `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (+ test), `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts`, `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts` (+ test), `src/lib/linq/persona-bridge.ts`, `src/components/checkpoint/CheckpointOverlay.tsx`, `src/lib/analytics/events.ts` (+ test), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (+ test stale comment refs), `src/app/demo/glass/page.tsx`. Deleted: `src/lib/persona/classifier.ts` + test, `src/components/modals/FirstCheckpointModal.tsx` + test. Net 353 lines removed across the branch. 678 tests pass. Build clean.

Fixes a bug where the Prompt Architecture link disappeared after clicking Docs — the docs page's hand-rolled nav was missing Health and Prompt Architecture entries entirely. Component accepts `activeId` for highlighting and an optional `badges` record so the main admin page can keep its unread-feedback and api-errors counts. **(2) Desktop dev tools panel.** New `src/components/admin/DevToolsPanel.tsx` extracts the Simulate user + Populate manual UI from `MobileSettings.tsx` into a standalone, compact component rendered in `DesktopVitrine.tsx`'s colophon column alongside the publication text. Admin-only (returns `null` for non-admins) and gated by a new `.mw-dev-tools` CSS class that only displays at ≥1030px (wide desktop), where there's room in the left margin. Lets admins simulate a user (1-3 checkpoints) and populate manual layers directly from the desktop canvas without opening mobile settings inside the phone frame. After populate completes, the page reloads so the phone frame's manual surface picks up the new entries. **(3) Rename.** "System Prompt" → "Jove's prompt architecture" across the page heading, the AdminNavRail label, and the desktop colophon link (`Prompts` → `Jove's prompts`). Files: `src/components/admin/AdminNavRail.tsx` (new), `src/components/admin/DevToolsPanel.tsx` (new), `src/app/admin/page.tsx`, `src/app/admin/docs/page.tsx`, `src/app/admin/prompt-architecture/page.tsx`, `src/components/layout/DesktopVitrine.tsx`, `src/app/globals.css`. 682 tests pass, build clean.

- New vs returning user prompt restructure (2026-05-13, suspicious-mirzakhani worktree): Structural fix to how `buildTier3()` renders user-status blocks across conversation modes. **Problem.** FIRST MESSAGE (new user) and RETURNING USER blocks both rendered alongside guided-intake/upload mode blocks, giving the model conflicting opener instructions. A returning user clicking "Navigate a situation" got "I'm Jove. Tell me what's going on." because the old RETURNING USER three-part template ("Welcome back / Manual ref / What is on your mind today?") assumed Jove speaks first, but the app always has the user speak first via landing cards. **Fix.** (1) `showFirstMessage` gated on `mode === "situation"` — FIRST MESSAGE no longer renders alongside guided-intake/upload openers that have their own instructions. (2) Guided-intake and upload OPENER sections now branch on `isReturningUser`: returning users get "deliver the opener without introducing yourself or greeting them"; new users get "you may briefly introduce yourself before the opener." (3) RETURNING USER block split: general rules (no intro, no recap, follow their lead — all modes) + situation-specific first-turn block (reference Manual, respond to what they said, activated carve-out — situation mode only). (4) FIRST SESSION text cleaned up — removed stale "welcome chip" reference. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts`, `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.test.ts` (4 new tests for mode×user-type matrix, 3 tests updated). 684 tests pass. Build clean.

Merged into the entry's continuous prose — the composition prompt now includes stance naturally in the passage instead of producing a separate JSON field. Removed from: composition prompt output, RPC params (8→7), `CheckpointMeta`, SSE `message_complete` events, `ActiveCheckpoint` type, `ManualEntry` type, `CheckpointOverlay` rendering, `EntryItem` rendering, `manual-context.ts` full-text and mixed-voice logic, `layer-definitions.ts` adapter, `sse-parser.ts` checkpoint type. Migration `20260513200000_remove_so_what.sql` drops the column (never applied to prod) and recreates the 7-param RPC. System prompt and extraction brief hints updated to say "what changes" instead of "the so-what." 653 tests pass. Build clean.
- Checkpoint voice + card + backdrop fixes (2026-05-13, happy-buck worktree): Five fixes from user testing focused on "descriptive, not performative." **(1) Lead-in text.** Changed from "I'm seeing something here" / "Something else is taking shape" to "A pattern came through in what you said." for both first and subsequent checkpoints. **(2) Composition prompt anti-nominalization.** Added explicit rule: "No nominalizations ('the reach,' 'the ask,' 'the pull'). Use verbs, not nouns pretending to be verbs" with before/after example ("I turn away before the reach" → "I turn away before they can ask me"). Added "Write like a field note, not literature" voice rule. Headline bad examples expanded: "Turned Away Before the Ask" flagged as nominalization; good example corrected to "Body Locks Before Being Asked." **(3) Trigger card layer header.** Redesigned from flat card with "SUGGESTED MANUAL ENTRY" eyebrow to two-section card: top section with `--session-walnut-highlight` background showing layer name ("Layer One — Wiring"), bottom section with entry title + "Tap to review" + chevron. `LAYER_NAMES` and `LAYER_ORDINAL` maps used for display. **(4) Modal backdrop opacity.** `--session-backdrop-heavy` increased — dark mode 0.75→0.92, light mode 0.55→0.85 — so conversation text behind the checkpoint overlay no longer bleeds through. 655 tests pass. Build clean.
- VC-readable prompt architecture page (2026-05-13, romantic-colden worktree): Admin-gated page at `/admin/prompt-architecture` that renders the Jove system prompt as an annotated scrolling document, importing directly from the live composition functions — no copy-pasted prompt text. **Route.** `/admin/prompt-architecture` page + `/api/admin/prompt-architecture` API route. Page uses `useIsAdmin()` hook (matching existing admin pattern). API route uses `verifyAdmin()` + middleware gating. **Layout.** Sticky header with title, persona checkboxes (multi-select, General mutually exclusive with neurotypes), conv-mode radio (Standard / Guided Intake / Upload), and phase nav anchors. Four lifecycle phases rendered below: (1) Brand new account, (2) Approaching first checkpoint, (3) Returning user, (4) Returning user approaching checkpoint. Each phase calls `buildSystemPrompt()` with appropriate flags. **Per-section annotations.** Each prompt section shows: tier-coded left border (blue intro / brown T1 / amber T2 / green T3 / purple dynamic), first 3 lines of actual prompt text with fade-out + "Show full text" toggle, token count (~chars/4), "why" condition pill (Always / Persona / State / Conv mode / Dynamic), slot-in alternatives with token counts, and info button opening a modal with full text + source file reference. **Phase footers.** Total tokens, section count, delta vs previous phase, bulleted "what changed" list (added/removed sections). **Section parser.** `src/lib/admin/prompt-sections.ts` parses rendered `buildSystemPrompt()` output into labeled sections using stable header patterns, maps each to source modules, computes token estimates, and generates alternatives. **Replaces** `docs/prompt-architecture.html` (deleted — the three-view prototype that taught us the design). **Files.** `src/lib/admin/prompt-sections.ts` (parser + phase builder), `src/app/api/admin/prompt-architecture/route.ts` (API), `src/app/admin/prompt-architecture/page.tsx` (page). **Nav.** Linked from admin sidebar rail (below Docs, above ← EXIT ADMIN). **Visual redesign v2** (2026-05-13): Two-column slot-in layout. Page now renders full admin nav rail (Users, Beta, Feedback, Health, Docs, Prompt Architecture) matching the main admin page. Four phases collapsed by default as accordions showing summary stats (tokens, sections, delta). Each phase expands to a two-column view: left column "Available Components" lists absent sections (not active in this phase) with tier indicators, token counts, condition pills, and "Active in: ..." labels, plus "Swap Alternatives" for persona/mode-dependent sections; right column "Active Prompt" shows the rendered prompt as clearly separated section cards with tier dots, condition pills, token counts, "Show full text" toggle, and info modal. Persona/mode controls at top drive which components appear in each column. Build clean. **Visual redesign v3 — three-layer cake** (2026-05-13): Replaced the four-phase accordion with a three-layer conceptual model that groups sections by what changes them rather than when they change. Layer 1 "Foundation" (collapsed by default): 14 always-present sections (constitutional rules, banned phrases, pacing, repair — persona/state-independent). Layer 2 "Voice" (open by default): 5 persona-dependent sections (voice intro, voice rules, example register, landing, deepening) with "OTHER VOICES" alternatives bar showing unselected personas' token counts. Layer 3 "Lifecycle" (open by default): 9 conditional blocks (first-message, returning-user, checkpoints, readiness gate, confirmed manual, session context, etc.) each with "Active in:" phase indicator pills showing which of the 4 lifecycle phases include it. Controls: persona multi-select chips + conv-mode chips + total token count. Each section card: tier dot, mono-caps label, token count, info button (modal with source file reference + full text), truncated preview with "Show full text" toggle. Reduces cognitive load from 4×20+ sections to 3 clear categories (~70% foundation, ~15% voice, ~15% lifecycle). Build clean.
- Multi-select persona modes + equal voice stacking (2026-05-13, mystifying-dewdney worktree): Persona mode expanded from single-select to multi-select, and Tier 2 voice composition refactored to stack personas equally rather than the previous primary-plus-additions optimization. **Schema.** `profiles.persona_mode` (text, single value) → `profiles.persona_modes` (text[], multi). Migrations applied in three steps: `20260513100000_expand_persona_mode.sql` widens the CHECK constraint to include audhd + dyslexic + general, `20260513110000_add_audhd_dyslexic_persona_modes.sql` is an intermediate step, `20260513120000_persona_modes_array.sql` converts the column to text[] with `{autistic}` default, drops the old column, and adds a subset CHECK against the four valid values. **UI.** `PersonaModeScreen` converted from radio to checkboxes. State is `PersonaMode[]` rather than `PersonaMode`. Checking "general" unchecks neurotype-specific modes and vice versa; continue is disabled when nothing is selected. DB write goes to `persona_modes` array. The screen renders inside `PostLoginOnboarding` (the post-auth gate), not `OnboardingFlow` (which is now just entry + login). **Equal voice stacking.** Previous design: the first selected persona contributed its full ~1,400-token Voice block; every additional persona contributed only a ~60-token `UNIQUE_ADDITIONS` summary block. Token-efficient but conceptually wrong — it introduced a "primary" hierarchy and lost ~90% of each additional persona's unique behavioral guidance (LANDING_EXAMPLES, WEAK_STRONG_EXAMPLES, voice-rule mutations, deepening additions). New design: new `src/lib/persona/voice-scaffold.ts` holds the ~900 tokens of structural content duplicated across all four voice files (TIER 2 header, BANNED_PHRASES + BANNED_PATTERNS, dash-to-period rule, LANDING intro, DEEPENING intro/outro, PACING rule, WHEN_JOVE_IS_WRONG, WHEN_USER_ASKS_WHAT_SHOULD_I_DO). Each `voice-*.ts` shrinks to ~500 tokens of persona-unique data: `VOICE_INTRO_PARAGRAPHS`, `VOICE_RULES`, `EXAMPLE_REGISTER`, `LANDING_EXAMPLES`, `DEEPENING_ADDITIONS`, `WEAK_STRONG_EXAMPLES`. `renderTier2()` removed from each module. `composeTier2(modes)` in `system-prompt.ts` rewritten to assemble scaffold + every selected module's unique content, deduplicating by full content (label+line for example_register and landing_examples; weak+strong for weak_strong; full string for voice_rules and intro paragraphs) so persona-specific variants with the same label both appear (e.g. autistic's "Naming a pattern" AND audhd's "Naming a pattern" both render in multi-mode). `UNIQUE_ADDITIONS` and `TIER2_RENDERERS` deleted. General is filtered out when combined with neurotype-specific modes (existing behavior preserved). **Behavior guarantees.** Single-mode prompts are byte-identical to pre-refactor (verified by snapshot diff against `renderTier2()` output before deletion). Multi-mode prompts grow ~440 tokens per additional persona vs the old ~60. Worst case three-persona prompt lands around ~6,300 tokens — well within budget. Within-section ordering follows the user's selection order (autistic-first vs dyslexic-first puts that mode's examples first in lists), but content weight is symmetric. **Prompt architecture visualizer.** Originally shipped as `docs/prompt-architecture.html` (single-file prototype with three views). Replaced by the admin-gated `/admin/prompt-architecture` page that imports live composition functions — see separate entry above. **Tests.** Tests refactored for equal stacking: BANNED_PHRASES/BANNED_PATTERNS imports moved from `voice-autistic` to `voice-scaffold` (single source of truth); the `composeTier2 multi-select` describe block rewritten to assert both intros present, both unique behavioral guidance present, shared scaffold (WHEN JOVE IS WRONG, etc.) appears exactly once. 675 tests pass (up from 650). Build clean.
- Checkpoint trigger card + overlay review (2026-05-13, amazing-hertz worktree): Reworked the pending-checkpoint moment from an inline content card with text-link actions into a two-stage reveal — building indicator → compact trigger card → full-screen overlay review. Trigger card shows the eyebrow, the composed entry title, and "Tap to review"; the entry body lives in the overlay, not inline in the chat stream. **New component** `src/components/checkpoint/CheckpointOverlay.tsx` renders the unified module (entry + actions in one card). Entry section shows `composedContent` (the polished version that will actually land in the Manual) with fallback to the assistant message content for older builds. Actions row is "Add to my Manual" primary, "Edit" toggle (contenteditable on headline + body with a hint line), "Jove, let's rework together" (refine), and "Discard". Refinement ceiling preserved as the existing "Put it in as it is / let it go" fork inside the same overlay. **Lead-in now fires for every checkpoint.** `call-persona.ts` previously emitted the transition line ("Something else is forming…") only when `manualComponents.length >= 1`. First-time checkpoints landed cold. The condition was relaxed to always-emit; first-checkpoint variant uses a softer "Hold on — I want to put something in front of you." subsequent variant unchanged. The lead-in is a persisted assistant message inserted 1s before the checkpoint row, so it survives reload and flows into future Jove context. **Edited content reaches the Manual.** `/api/checkpoint/confirm` accepts optional `editedContent` and `editedName`; `confirmCheckpoint()` overrides `composed_content`/`composed_name` when present, recomputes the summary fallback from the edited text, and clears stale composed key_words so the compressed view reflects user-authored words. `useChat.confirmCheckpoint()` plumbs an `edits` object through to the API and to the optimistic local entry insert; `setMessages` also rewrites the in-memory checkpoint message so the chat history's historical Plate shows the final committed text rather than the unedited proposal. **Composed content streams to the client.** `message_complete` SSE event now carries `composed_content` and `composed_name` alongside the checkpoint payload. `ActiveCheckpoint` and the DB-load path in `useChat` both surface them. **Overlay confirm waits on the API.** Replaced the fixed-timer composing → confirmed sequence with a `confirmStatus` prop (`idle`/`pending`/`success`/`error`) driven from parent state — composing shows while the call is in flight, confirmed only on success, and an error line renders above the actions so the user can retry from the same surface. **StrictMode fix.** The building-indicator timer initially gated on `!prevCheckpointRef.current`, which in Next.js dev StrictMode (effects run setup→cleanup→setup) saw the ref already populated on the second setup and skipped scheduling — trigger card never appeared. Switched to keying off `activeCheckpoint?.messageId`; both prod and dev now behave correctly. **FirstCheckpointModal copy** updated from "A pattern is ready for your Manual" / "I have a pattern to put in front of you…" to "A suggested entry is ready" / "I have something to put in front of you. Tap to review it. If it fits, add it to your Manual. If it's off, we can rework it together." **Tests.** `onboarding-copy.test.ts` updated for the new action labels living on `CheckpointOverlay.tsx`; `FirstCheckpointModal.test.ts` updated for the new copy. 650 tests pass. Build clean.
- Checkpoint bug fixes (2026-05-13, happy-buck worktree): Four fixes from user testing. **(1) Greeting for returning users.** Jove was introducing itself by name ("Hello I'm Jove") on returning-user sessions. FIRST MESSAGE block now allows self-introduction on the very first session; RETURNING USER block explicitly prohibits it ("Do not introduce yourself by name — the user already knows who you are"). **(2) Performative titles.** Composition prompt and checkpoint delivery instructions strengthened against poetic/literary titles (e.g. "Gaps Open and the Reach Fires"). Added explicit anti-poetry instruction and bad examples. **(3) Error text contrast.** Checkpoint overlay error message was brown-on-brown (muted salmon on walnut surface). Changed to `--session-ink` for readability. **(4) confirm_checkpoint_write RPC migration.** The `p_so_what` parameter was missing from the production function (old 7-param signature persisted). Re-applied the function portion of `20260513100000_add_so_what_column.sql` — `pronargs` now 8. Confirm + edit flow works. Tests added for so_what, editedContent, and editedName paths. 655 tests pass. Build clean.
- Checkpoint polish pass (2026-05-13, happy-buck worktree): Four fixes from manual testing. **(1) Lead-in voice.** Changed from performative ("Hold on — I want to put something in front of you") to descriptive ("I'm seeing something here" for first checkpoint, "Something else is taking shape" for subsequent). **(2) FirstCheckpointModal removed.** The modal that intercepted the first-ever checkpoint is gone — inline trigger card + overlay flow runs every time. `modal_progress` auto-advances to 3 via fire-and-forget POST. **(3) Discarded checkpoints collapse.** Rejected checkpoints now show only the entry title + "Discarded" status label — no full content. Confirmed and refined checkpoints still show the full text. **(4) Post-discard response.** Changed from "Okay. What made it miss?" to "That entry didn't land. Was it off, or just not ready?" — clearer about what happened. **(5) So-what label removed.** The "WHAT CHANGES" mono-caps eyebrow was over-labeling; replaced with hairline separator + italic styling only in both CheckpointOverlay and EntryItem. Tests updated. 650 tests pass. Build clean.
- Checkpoint redesign — three-phase flow, so-what entries, Opus composition (2026-05-13, happy-buck worktree): Redesigned the checkpoint and manual entry system across extraction, classifier, composition, system prompt, and UI. **New entry structure.** Manual entries now have two parts: the pattern (statement + passage) and an optional so-what (the user's stance — what changes now that they can see the pattern). New `so_what text` column on `manual_entries`; `confirm_checkpoint_write` RPC updated to accept `p_so_what`. **Three-phase conversation flow.** Extraction now tracks `pattern_engaged` (boolean), `user_named_cost`, and `user_named_stance` in extraction state. Jove receives phase-aware hints: pattern not yet named → keep exploring; pattern engaged → work toward the so-what; material ready → checkpoint can fire. Turn-12 override bypasses `pattern_engaged` if extraction missed the engagement signal but material quality is otherwise strong. **Classifier hardened.** Checkpoint detection now requires all of: the pinned transition line ("I want to put something in your Manual"), sustained reflection, headline, and validation question. Reflections without the transition line are classified as naming moves, not checkpoints. **Composition on Opus.** Switched from Sonnet to Opus (`claude-opus-4-6`) with `max_tokens: 2048`. Composition prompt rewritten for first-person voice, two-part structure (pattern + so_what), new exemplars. The so-what returns null when the user hasn't landed on a stance — no canned text fabricated. **System prompt updates.** Added "NAMING THE PATTERN" section with three naming shapes (pointing at repetition, offering plain description, naming contradiction). Two-instance rule and strength exception. Checkpoint delivery updated with so-what step. **Manual context compression.** Recent entries render `so_what` as "What changes: ..." below the pattern. Mixed-voice note appended when both old (you-voice, no so_what) and new (I-voice, has so_what) entries coexist. **UI.** `CheckpointOverlay` renders `composedSoWhat` below the entry body in italic with a hairline separator. `EntryItem` renders `soWhat` on manual entry cards the same way. `ActiveCheckpoint` type, SSE event, and `useChat` all carry `composed_so_what` / `composedSoWhat`. **Text channel.** `persona-bridge.ts` passes `turnCount` to `applyCheckpointGates`. **Tests.** Updated extraction state defaults and checkpoint-gate tests for `pattern_engaged`. 650 tests pass. Build clean. **Migration required.** `supabase/migrations/20260513100000_add_so_what_column.sql` must be applied before deploy — adds `so_what` column and recreates the RPC.
- Bubble color flip (2026-05-13): Reversed the color assignment across the chat surface. Jove bubbles now use the cooler slate/navy surface (`--session-bubble-user`), user bubbles use the warm walnut surface (`--session-walnut-surface`). Background gradient flipped in both dark and light modes — walnut warmth pools at the top of the viewport, navy/slate deepens toward the bottom. Input pill (`ChatInput.tsx`) matches the bottom navy zone with `--session-bubble-user` bg + border. Checkpoint plates unchanged (still walnut-surface — they hold user-confirmed Manual content). Files: `globals.css` (dark + light `--session-bg-chat` gradients), `Bubble.tsx` (swapped `JOVE_STYLE`/`USER_STYLE` backgrounds and borders), `ChatInput.tsx` (pill surface + border tokens). Build clean, 650 tests pass.
- UI polish pass (2026-05-13): Six fixes across drawer, entry screen, session lifecycle, admin, and API error handling. **(1) New-session lag fix.** `startNewSession()` in `useChat.ts` blocked on two awaits — `fetch("/api/conversations/complete")` and `refreshConversations()`. Both changed to fire-and-forget (`.catch(() => {})` without `await`), matching the `startExploration()` pattern. Button response is now instant. **(2) Drawer polish.** "Read my Manual" renamed to "my Manual" and moved first in the pill pair; "+ New session" second. Session list collapsed to 3 visible rows with "Show all (N)" expander — resets on drawer close. Single-line session rows (title + date, removed message counts). Added "Log out" NavRow between Settings and Crisis support; `onLogout` prop threaded through `MainApp` → `SessionDrawer`. **(3) Entry screen single CTA.** Closed-beta landing simplified from dual-CTA ("Begin" + "Already have access? Sign in") to a single "Sign in" pill. `onBegin` made optional, primary button fires `onLogin`. Tests updated. **(4) Admin colophon link.** Desktop vitrine footer shows "ADMIN" link (mono caps, `/admin`) for admin users only. Uses `useIsAdmin()` hook (`user?.app_metadata?.role === "admin"`). **(5) Session summary false-500 fix.** `/api/session/summary` route returned 500 + recorded an API error when `generateSessionSummary` returned null (expected for new conversations or transient Anthropic failures). Changed to return 200 with `{ summary: null }` — the client handles missing summaries gracefully. **(6) displayName bug fix.** `useChat.ts` now exposes `displayName` state alongside `firstName`, fixing a `ReferenceError` when `displayName` was referenced but never declared. 650 tests pass, build clean.
- First name only when addressing the user (2026-05-12, fervent-austin worktree): New `firstNameFrom` utility at `src/lib/utils/name.ts` returns the first whitespace-separated token of a display name, with `"User"` fallback for null/empty. Threaded through the data boundary in `useChat.ts` — the hook now exposes `firstName` instead of `displayName`.
