Single current consumer is `MobileManual.tsx` (PDF export header/title), so the user's PDF title reads "Jeff's Manual" instead of "Jeff Waters' Manual" — but the plumbing is the point: any future surface that addresses the user (greetings, salutations, addressed copy) reads `firstName` and gets the right register by default. Linq's `group-bridge.ts` and `group-detection.ts` keep their nullable local extraction (`displayName?.split(/\s+/)[0] ?? null`) — they treat `null` as "unnamed" rather than defaulting to `"User"`, and changing that semantics would affect message routing. 10 new unit tests cover the utility (single token, multi-word, hyphenated, diacritics, null/empty fallback, whitespace collapse). 636 tests pass, build clean.
- Navigation audit + fixes (2026-05-12, fervent-austin worktree / `claude/light-mode-implementation`): Comprehensive audit found 8 navigation problems across the drawer + TopBar + sub-views. All fixed in one branch. **(1) Glyph contrast in light mode.** `CIRCLE_BTN_STYLE` in `TopBar.tsx` and the drawer close button in `SessionDrawer.tsx` hardcoded `color: rgba(255,255,255,0.85)` — near-invisible on the new linen ground. Same hardcode in `ChatInput.tsx`'s mic/stop SVG stroke. All three now use `var(--session-ink)` (espresso in light, cream in dark). The inner "glass" border switched from `rgba(255,255,255,0.10)` to `var(--session-walnut-border-soft)` for proper definition in both modes. **(2) HIG tap targets.** TopBar circle buttons 30→44, drawer close 28→44. Glyph font-size adjusted (13.5→16 for TopBar, 12→14 for drawer close) so the visible mark stays at the editorial register without ballooning. TopBar vertical padding reduced 14→10 to keep header height balanced with the larger circles. **(3) Back affordance for sub-views.** `TopBar` API extended to accept both `onBack` and `onMenu` simultaneously. When both pass, back chevron occupies the LEFT slot (iOS convention) and menu moves to the RIGHT. When only one passes, behavior unchanged — onboarding screens (LoginScreen/SeedScreen/InfoScreens) keep their solo back chevron; chat keeps its solo menu. `MobileSettings` and `MobileManual` now pass `onBack={onNavigateToSession}` alongside `onMenu`. **(4) Feedback pill hidden on sub-views.** With the menu glyph now in the TopBar's right slot for sub-views, the floating `BetaFeedbackButton` (absolute top:22 right:22) collided. `MobileLayout` renders it only when `activeView === "session"` — feedback stays prominent on chat, hidden on Settings/Manual/Crisis. **(5) "+ New session" actually returns to chat.** `MainApp` wraps `startNewSession` in `handleNewSession` that flips `activeView` to `"session"` before resetting. Previously: tapping from Settings or Manual reset state but stranded the user on the wrong panel — the button looked broken. **(6) Drawer active state.** `NavRow` accepts `isActive`; renders a 2px walnut left rail (matching the active session row pattern) and lifts text from `--session-ink-soft` to `--session-ink` when current. Crisis row uses the oxblood rail. `MainApp` passes `activeView` through to the drawer. **(7) Drawer a11y.** `useEffect` autofocuses the close button on open and listens for Escape on document; either closes. The `aria-modal="true"` claim is now backed by keyboard behavior. **(8) Crisis Support as its own surface.** New `MobileCrisis.tsx` view with the editorial pattern — oxblood eyebrow + Spectral heading ("If you need someone now.") + 2-sentence intro + two link cards (988 + 741741, both `href="tel:"` / `href="sms:"`) + reassurance footer ("Free · Confidential · Available 24/7"). `MobileLayout` extends the `MobileView` union to include `"crisis"` and renders the panel alongside session/manual/settings. The duplicated Crisis section in `MobileSettings.tsx` was removed (Settings stays focused on settings; Crisis gets a real destination). Drawer's Crisis row routes to `onNavigateToCrisis()` instead of `onNavigateToSettings()`. **(9) In-frame wordmark visible at desktop wide.** Removed the `.mw-phone-frame .mw-topbar-wordmark { visibility: hidden }` rule at ≥1030px. The previous assumption (outer 52px masthead replaces in-frame wordmark) left the phone frame feeling unbranded. Both wordmarks now render at different scales: outer at publication 52px on the canvas margin, inner at UI 19px in the TopBar — different roles, not duplicates. **Result.** 1 merge commit, 9 files touched (1 new), 626 tests pass, build clean. Verified in browser at 410×840 and 1440×900 in both light and dark modes: menu glyph readable, back chevron lands you on chat, drawer shows active rail for current sub-view, Crisis surface renders editorially, "+ New session" works from any view. **Post-ship polish.** Three follow-up items: (a) Drawer hierarchy reordered — "Read my Manual" moved from the bottom NavRow to a top-of-drawer pill with equal visual weight to "+ New session". New shared `PrimaryPill` component wraps both: walnut surface + walnut border, optional entry-count badge (Manual shows "N entries"), active-state walnut left rail + card shadow. The two most-tapped entry points (start fresh / open Manual) now sit together at the top; Settings stays in the secondary nav. (b) Swipe-from-left-edge to open the drawer. Document-level passive touch listeners in `MainApp` detect `touchstart` within 24px of the left edge, then a horizontal swipe ≥60px that's clearly horizontal (`dx > 1.5 * dy`). Disarms if vertical scroll wins (`dy > 30` before threshold). Only active when drawer is closed. Verified by dispatching synthetic `TouchEvent` from edge — drawer transitions from `aria-hidden=true` to `false` after the swipe. (c) Exploration chip dismiss `✕` bumped from 22×22 → 44×44 with negative right-margin so layout doesn't shift; color upgraded `--session-ink-mid` → `--session-ink`; glyph 12→14. Closes the last sub-44pt tap target in the nav surface.
- Welcome entry cards + rotating greeting (2026-05-12, angry-shockley worktree): Replaced the three text-based `WELCOME_CHIPS` and scattered "Help me get started" links with a unified entry-point selector. **Welcome header.** Date in uppercase mono-caps (`formatWelcomeDate()`) + 44px serif greeting with accent-colored walnut period + italic lead-in "What's on your mind today?". First-time users see "Hello,\nI'm Jove." (two-line via `white-space: pre-line`). Returning users get a random greeting from `RETURNING_GREETINGS` (6 options, some personalized with `displayName` when `hasRealName` is true — skips email-prefix handles by checking for at least one capital letter and not "User"). Greeting index randomized per session via `useMemo` with `conversationId` dependency. **Three entry cards.** (1) "Navigate a situation" — sends `"I have a situation I want to work through"` to Jove, kickstarting the conversation (restored in 2026-05-13 onboarding cleanup; previously just dismissed cards without sending). (2) "Guided intake" — calls `startGuidedIntake()`, Jove speaks first with the relationship-anchored opener. (3) "Upload" — calls `startUpload()`, Jove speaks first with the paste-content opener. Each card is a flex row: 36px rounded icon square (inline SVG) + serif title + sans subtitle + chevron. Cards use `--session-walnut-surface` bg, `--session-walnut-border` border, 14px radius (updated 2026-05-13 to match walnut glass design). Date eyebrow uses `--session-walnut-meta` (warm tone). 36px gap between subtitle and cards. `chipsVisible` state resets on `conversationId` change. All three empty-state render paths (first-time, returning, fallback) collapsed into one `showEntryCards` block. **Onboarding cleanup (2026-05-13).** Removed 5 ghost props from `MobileSessionProps` (`isNewUser`, `firstSessionCompleted`, `sessionOrigin`, `sessionSummary`, `lastSessionDate`) — declared in interface and passed from `MainApp` but never destructured or read in the component body. Corresponding destructuring and JSX prop-pass removed from `MainApp`. `OnboardingFlow.tsx` cleaned of dead code: unreachable `"onboarding"` and `"seed"` views removed (no handler ever transitioned to them), `handleNavigateToSeed` deleted, unused `InfoScreens`/`SeedScreen` imports removed, `ViewName` simplified to `"entry" | "login"`. `PostLoginOnboarding` (the post-auth gate at `/`) still renders `InfoScreens` → `SeedScreen` — that path is live and unchanged. **Tests.** `onboarding-copy.test.ts` updated: removed negative assertion for kickstart message string, added positive assertion for `sendMessage("I have a situation I want to work through")`. 651 tests pass. Build clean.
- Relationship-anchored guided intake opener + quick-reply chips (2026-05-12, angry-shockley worktree): Two changes shipping together as the guided intake cold-start entry point. **(1) Relationship-anchored opener.** `GUIDED_INTAKE_OPENER` changed from the moment-based opener to "Pick someone of note in your life. A person — or a pet. Could be your partner, parent, a friend, a coworker. Positive or negative, high impact or low. Just someone worth naming." GUIDED INTAKE block in `system-prompt.ts` rewritten: OPENER section (locked text), FALLBACK CHAIN (widen scope → relationship to pattern → gentle end), AFTER NAMING (acknowledge → orienting question → scene invitation), QUICK-REPLY OPTIONS (format spec + behavioral rules), POSTURE/DIRECTED MOVES/PIVOTS/EXIT (carried forward). Detection anchors updated: `RECENCY_DROP_PHRASE` → `WIDEN_SCOPE_PHRASE` ("Who did you last have a conversation with"), `MOMENTS_TO_STATES_PHRASE` → `RELATIONSHIP_TO_PATTERN_PHRASE` ("Skip the person"). `GuidedIntakeOpenerVariant` type updated to `"widen_scope" | "relationship_to_pattern"`. **(2) Quick-reply chips.** Backend: `call-persona.ts` parses `---chips---` delimiter, strips chip lines from `conversationalText`, emits `chips` array on `message_complete` SSE event (capped at 6). Chips are ephemeral — stripped before DB storage, not persisted on assistant messages. `sse-parser.ts` + `types.ts` extended with `chips?: string[]`. Client: `useChat.ts` stores chips on the message, exposes `sendChipResponse(text)` which clears all chips and sends with `isChipResponse: true` flag. `QuickReplyChips.tsx` component renders chips as a vertical stack of serif italic buttons with hairline borders. `MobileSession.tsx` renders chips below the last assistant bubble (hidden during streaming). API: `route.ts` accepts `isChipResponse`, `call-persona.ts` stores `{ chip_response: true }` in message `metadata`, `persona-pipeline.ts` annotates chip-tap messages with `[selected from options]` in history so Jove follows up for texture. **Migration.** `20260513000000_add_message_metadata.sql` adds `metadata jsonb DEFAULT '{}'` to `messages` table. **Tests.** Chip parsing, SSE protocol, variant detection, UI wiring assertions. 640 tests pass. Build clean.
- Upload entry point — backend + pipeline (2026-05-12, romantic-banzai worktree): Third conversation mode (`"upload"`) for users to paste text content (chat logs, email threads, journal entries) for Jove to analyze against their Manual. **URL detection removed.** Inline URL detection (`detectUrls`) and content fetching (`fetchUrlContent`) stripped from the persona pipeline (`call-persona.ts`). The `SHARED CONTENT` and `SHARED CONTENT — FETCH FAILED` dynamic prompt blocks removed from `system-prompt.ts`; `contentContext` param removed from `BuildPromptOptions`. `url-detection.ts` and `fetch-url-content.ts` files retained in the codebase for the future Resonant Content entry point. **Transcript detection kept as passive fallback.** `detectTranscript()` still runs on every non-upload message at zero cost (pure regex). Upload-mode conversations skip it — the Tier 3 block handles content analysis directly. **Upload Tier 3 prompt block.** New conditional block in `buildTier3()` when `mode === "upload"`. Deterministic opener via `UPLOAD_OPENER` constant in `src/lib/persona/upload-copy.ts` ("Paste something here. A text thread, an email chain…"). After the user pastes, Jove identifies format (speaker-alternating, email thread, journal, unknown), acknowledges with a specific reference, asks a framing question, then cross-references against Manual entries. Behavioral guardrails: focus on the user's behavior, no diagnosis of others, no summarizing, no advice. **Plumbing.** Migration `20260512000000_add_upload_mode.sql` adds `'upload'` to `conversations.mode` CHECK constraint. `EntryPoint` and `ConversationMode` analytics types expanded. Chat route accepts `"upload"` mode; message length limit raised to 16,000 chars for upload-mode conversations (vs 4,000 standard). When continuing an existing upload conversation, route reads mode from DB so the higher limit applies to follow-up messages (the paste arrives as the second message, after Jove's opener). `startUpload()` in `useChat.ts` follows the guided-intake pattern: `message: null`, Jove speaks first. `FABRICATED CONTENT` guard simplified — Jove can no longer access URLs, just tells users to paste or describe. **Tests.** 629 pass (from 628 — URL tests replaced with upload mode + fabrication guard tests). Build ✓. **UI partially wired** — Upload card appears on the welcome entry screen (disabled placeholder at 40% opacity). Backend + pipeline ready; card needs to call `startUpload()` once enabled.
- Light-mode redesign (2026-05-12, fervent-austin worktree / `claude/light-mode-implementation` branch, all 8 phases per `docs/implementation/light-mode.md`): Light mode added alongside the already-shipped dark mode. Source of truth: `src/app/demo/linen/page.tsx`, **Version 03 — Embossed linen**. Walnut spine identical across themes (`#5C3A1E`) — only the surrounding tone shifts. **Foundation (Phase 1).** `useTheme` hook (`src/lib/hooks/useTheme.ts`) returns `{ theme: 'system'|'light'|'dark', resolved, setTheme }`, persists to `localStorage.mywalnut.theme`, subscribes to `matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: light)')` for live system-pref changes. `ThemeInit` (`src/components/ThemeInit.tsx`) is a mount-once client component that keeps `<html data-theme>` and `<meta name="theme-color">` in sync. `layout.tsx` adds a FOUC-safe inline script in `<head>` that synchronously reads localStorage → falls back to `matchMedia` → falls back to dark, then sets `data-theme` and the PWA `theme-color` before paint. SSR default remains `data-theme="dark"` with `suppressHydrationWarning`. **Tokens (Phase 2).** `[data-theme="light"]` block in `globals.css` with the full V3 Embossed token set — linen ground `#E5D8BE`, espresso ink `#1F140A`, walnut spine `#5C3A1E`, ink hierarchy (`--session-ink`, `--session-ink-soft #4A3220`, `--session-ink-faded #7A5E40`, `--session-ink-mono #5C3F23`), surface tints (`--session-walnut-faint 0.18`, `--session-walnut-tint 0.08`), bubble/plate paper tones (`--session-jove-bg rgba(232,220,196,0.90)`, `--session-bubble-user rgba(92,58,30,0.16)`, `--session-walnut-surface-soft rgba(236,224,200,0.93)`), shadow stacks (`--session-bubble-shadow`, `--session-plate-shadow`, `--session-card-shadow`), sage `#5E7A4F`, oxblood `#7A2E2E` (cross-theme, unchanged). Plus a `@media (prefers-color-scheme: light) { :root:not([data-theme="dark"]) { color-scheme: light; } }` safety net for no-JS fallback. **Per-surface gradients (Phase 3).** Four light gradient stacks in `globals.css` — `--session-bg-welcome`, `--session-bg-chat`, `--session-bg-checkpoint`, `--session-bg-manual` — byte-identical to the demo's `C` object: cream highlight at top, caramel pool mid, deep walnut at corner. Verified via `getComputedStyle().backgroundImage` against the demo. `DesktopVitrine.tsx` corner vignette switched from a hardcoded `rgba(26,22,20,0.02)` to `var(--session-vignette)` so it flips with theme. **Component audit (Phase 4, the highest-risk sweep).** 15 components scanned for hardcoded RGBs/hexes that would break in light. 18 new tokens added at end of `:root` and matching values in `[data-theme="light"]` to cover what surfaced: `--session-bubble-border`, `--session-bubble-user`, `--session-bubble-user-border`, `--session-slate-soft`, `--session-button-inset`, `--session-button-inset-strong`, `--session-drawer-bg`, `--session-bubble-shadow`, `--session-plate-shadow`, `--session-sheet-shadow`, `--session-walnut-meta-strong`, `--session-walnut-meta-soft`, `--session-walnut-highlight`, `--session-ink-user-text`, `--session-error-border`, `--session-error-border-soft`, `--session-oauth-surface`, `--session-oauth-border`. Components refactored: `Bubble` (jove + user border, shadow, user text color), `Plate` (border, shadow, eyebrow), `TopBar` (button inset), `Modal` (border + shadow + destructive border), `ConnectionErrorPlate`, `BetaFeedbackButton` (popover border + shadow), `ChatInput` (pill border + shadow + mic/stop button bg), `SessionDrawer` (panel bg + close button), `MobileSession` (chip cards + icon bg + guided intake card), `MobileManual` (export sheet), `MobileSettings` (SectionHeader + danger border), `EmptyLayer` + `PopulatedLayer` (header colors), `InfoScreens` (plate), `LoginScreen` + `AuthPromptModal` (Google OAuth wash now its own `--session-oauth-*` token pair since the cream-on-dark rgba didn't map cleanly to existing walnut tokens). **Settings toggle (Phase 5).** New `AppearanceToggle` (`src/components/shared/AppearanceToggle.tsx`) — three-state segmented pill (System / Light / Dark) reading `useTheme()`, active segment uses `--session-walnut-surface` bg + `--session-ink` text + optional card shadow, inactive segments transparent with `--session-ink-mid`. Inserted into `MobileSettings.tsx` between the Account and Crisis Support sections as a `SectionHeader` + `SettingsRow` with a "Theme" label and "System follows OS preference" mono-caps subtitle. **Cross-mode visual audit (Phase 6).** Walked every authenticated surface in both modes at 410×840: welcome chat, drawer (open + closed), Manual list, Manual entry expanded, Settings, ConfirmationModal. Confirmed via `getComputedStyle` probe that no white text (`rgb(255,255,255)`) leaks into the light `<main>` and no near-black backgrounds leak in. Gradient stacks verified against the V3 demo `C` object. Dark mode re-walked to confirm no regressions. **Tests + build (Phase 7).** 626 tests pass. `npm run build` clean. PWA `theme-color` meta flips between `#0A0B10` (dark) and `#E5D8BE` (light). FOUC script verified across reload — chosen theme persists and renders correctly on first paint. **Default behavior.** New users follow OS preference (existing dark users keep their experience because dark is the fallback when prefers-color-scheme is dark or unset). Explicit selection via the toggle overrides system; clearing the setting falls back to system. **What's deferred.** Manual layer cards keep the dark-mode treatment of border-only without `--session-card-shadow` lift even though the demo's PaperRow uses `var(--card-shadow)` — left as-is since both modes render correctly and adding shadow now would change dark mode's shipped look. **Desktop colophon toggle (post-merge).** Added an inline `THEME · SYSTEM · LIGHT · DARK` row to `DesktopVitrine.tsx` colophon, sitting above the "In closed beta · v… · Privacy · Terms" meta row. Editorial register matching the meta line: mono caps, dot separators, active option in `var(--session-ink)` with a 1px underline at 4px offset, inactive options inherit ink-mid. Hidden on mobile (the colophon is already gated by `.mw-desktop-paratext`). Mirrors the mobile Settings → Appearance toggle, both reading the same `useTheme` hook. **Result.** 6 commits on `claude/light-mode-implementation` (theme infrastructure → tokens → per-surface gradients → component-level tokenization → settings toggle → desktop colophon toggle). Dark mode untouched. See [implementation plan](implementation/light-mode.md) for the full files-touched map.
- Dark-mode redesign (2026-05-12, fervent-austin branch, all 9 phases): Full visual redesign of every authenticated and unauthenticated surface against `docs/implementation/dark-mode.md` and `src/app/demo/glass/page.tsx`. **Foundation (Phase 1).** `globals.css` `:root` rewritten with walnut+slate dark-only tokens — `--session-linen: #0A0B10`, `--session-walnut: rgb(170,120,82)`, `--session-walnut-surface: rgba(115,72,42,0.20)`, `--session-walnut-border: rgba(170,120,82,0.18)`. Surface gradient tokens added: `--session-bg-welcome`, `--session-bg-chat`, `--session-bg-checkpoint`, `--session-bg-manual` — each a stack of 3–4 radial gradients placing walnut warmth in different corners. Spectral added via `next/font/google`, exposed as `--font-spectral`. `ThemeInit.tsx`, `useTheme.ts`, the FOUC script, `data-theme` attribute, and all theme-toggle UI deleted. **Shared primitives (Phase 2).** `<Bubble />` for side-anchored chat (Jove left 92%, walnut surface, sage JOVE label / user right 88%, slate surface, asymmetric 5px speaker-side corner). `<Plate />` for walnut-tinted cards with eyebrow + 24px Spectral heading + 17px body. `<TopBar />` for the persistent `mywalnut.` masthead with optional back and menu glyphs. **Chat surface (Phase 3).** `MobileSession.tsx` rebuilt: italic-on-page chat → `<Bubble />`, checkpoint card → `<Plate />`, typing indicator → single pulsing fleuron in a Jove bubble, header → `<TopBar />`. `ChatInput.tsx` restyled as a walnut pill composer (`borderRadius: 22px`, walnut-surface bg, glass blur). TextBtn mono-caps "SEND ›" pattern; mic recasts to 28px ink-ghost glyph. **Manual surface (Phase 4).** `MobileManual.tsx` simplified to flat expanding-cards document with walnut glass section cards and chevron expand/collapse. `EntryItem.tsx`, `PopulatedLayer.tsx`, `EmptyLayer.tsx` restyled. Drop cap and "featured Layer I" removed; all five layers render equally. **Navigation rework (Phase 5).** `MobileNav.tsx` deleted. `MobileLayout.tsx` drops the bottom tab bar; chat extends to safe-area-inset-bottom. `SessionDrawer.tsx` expanded to be the full app menu: new-session pill, sessions list, primary nav rows (Read my Manual with entry count, Settings, Beta feedback), oxblood Crisis support footer. `MainApp.tsx` `activeTab` → `activeView` with drawer-driven view switching; owns `drawerOpen` + `feedbackOpen` state. `BetaFeedbackButton` refactored to a controlled walnut glass popover, no longer rendering its own fixed-position trigger. **Onboarding + auth (Phase 6).** `EntryScreen.tsx` collapsed from 655-line responsive landing to centered hero (72px wordmark, italic Spectral thesis, walnut pill BEGIN, Sign in link). `LoginScreen.tsx` rebuilt with walnut glass plate forms across login/signup/forgot modes — mono-caps labels, walnut-border inputs, TextBtn SIGN IN/CREATE ACCOUNT/SEND RESET LINK CTAs, Google OAuth preserved. `InfoScreens.tsx` collapsed to single disclaimer card on walnut plate. `SeedScreen.tsx` restyled with TopBar, walnut checkbox, TextBtn Begin. `OnboardingFlow.tsx` wires Begin → signup routing and applies welcome gradient background. **Edge cases (Phase 8).** Surface gradients applied via CSS vars (chat → `--session-bg-chat`, manual/settings → `--session-bg-manual`, all onboarding → `--session-bg-welcome`). DesktopVitrine masthead period color fixed from sage to walnut. SW update prompt repositioned (no longer offset for the gone tab bar). Public pages (privacy/terms/waitlist) inherit the dark palette automatically through the shared tokens. **Design audit pass (post-Phase-9).** Two follow-up commits after the initial ship: (a) string-level alignment — TopBar wordmark now CENTERED in a 3-slot flex layout (back chevron / wordmark / menu) with empty 30×30 spacers for missing actions; MobileManual added an `N entries` walnut mono-caps eyebrow above `Your Manual.`; PopulatedLayer + EmptyLayer headers restyled to `Layer I · Name` mono-caps walnut-light with right-aligned numeric count and walnut-border-soft bottom rule (roman numerals derived from layer id); EntryItem headlines got a walnut-period suffix and "Explore further" became a mono-caps walnut TextBtn with chevron + walnut underline; ChatInput mic/stop wrapped in 32px circular `rgba(0,0,0,0.40)` surfaces with 14px / 1.7 stroke icons. (b) computed-style alignment — read `getComputedStyle().backgroundImage` at 410×840 against every demo Frame; gradient strings matched byte-for-byte for Welcome/Chat/Manual; two real deltas surfaced: Bubble Jove + Plate border alpha was 0.18 (from `--session-walnut-border` token) vs the demo's literal 0.20, fixed with inline `rgba(170,120,82,0.20)` (token's 0.18 intentionally preserved for lighter divider roles in SessionDrawer + Manual cards); `--session-bg-checkpoint` was defined but never wired — MobileLayout now accepts `hasActiveCheckpoint?: boolean` and swaps the session panel `backgroundImage` to `var(--session-bg-checkpoint)` (4-stop walnut-top + slate-bottom stack) when MainApp's `activeCheckpoint !== null`. Verified live via DOM probe: `--session-bg-checkpoint` resolves to the demo's exact 4-stop value byte-for-byte. **Open / deferred.** Phase 7 (modal restyle for 5 modals + MobileSettings flat-list refactor) skipped — modals still on pre-redesign styling. Welcome-vs-Chat gradient routing for the chat-empty new-user state — demo uses `bgWelcome` for the "Hello, I'm Jove" hero, production keeps `bgChat`; left as a product decision. Radial-gradient positions tuned for the demo's 410-wide container; real iPhone widths (390 / 393) will see slight position drift — noted as tuning, not bug. **Post-ship UI fixes (post-merge).** Five issues surfaced by the project owner on production: (1) feedback affordance lost in Phase 5 — reverted `BetaFeedbackButton` to self-managing trigger (persistent mono-caps "feedback" pill top-right of phone frame, internal popover state + click-outside close); removed the drawer's "Beta feedback" row and `MainApp.feedbackOpen` state; moved render back inside `MobileLayout`. (2) hamburger menu moved to LEFT slot — `TopBar` now puts `onMenu` (or `onBack` as fallback) in the LEFT, wordmark centered, RIGHT slot empty (frees the corner for the feedback button to float above). (3) `ChatInput` redesigned to match the demo `PillComposer` spec exactly — `borderRadius: 22px → 999` (full pill), `padding: 6px 16px → 10px 14px`, `backdropFilter: blur(20px) → blur(32px) saturate(150%)`, added `boxShadow: 0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.40)`; placeholder repositioned from `left: 0` to `left: 14px` so the italic hint aligns with the textarea's content start (was offset by the pill's padding-left); placeholder fontSize `17 → 15px` to match demo's quieter hint register. (4) `Bubble` bottom padding `20px → 14px` — lighter visual weight below the last line of text. (5) tab-bar blank space removed — `MobileSession` + `MobileManual` scroll containers dropped the leftover `paddingBottom: "calc(52px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))"` (MobileNav clearance that no longer applied); now just the safe-area inset. **Phase 7 + Phase 8-tail follow-ups (post-merge).** Closes the remaining audit gaps with a final pass. **(a) Shared <Modal /> primitive** at `src/components/shared/Modal.tsx` — walnut-glass plate, backdrop, body-scroll lock, focus return, Escape to close, destructive variant. All five modals (`FirstCheckpointModal`, `PatternFormingModal`, `ChatWindowModal`, `AuthPromptModal`, `ConfirmationModal`) restyled to use it: walnut-meta eyebrow, Spectral heading with walnut period, paragraph body Spectral 15.5px, italic cancel + mono-caps TextBtn confirm; `ConfirmationModal` adds an oxblood-accented destructive variant. Test files updated to read the shared `Modal.tsx` for role/aria-modal assertions. **(b) MobileSettings flat-list** — `SectionHeader` is now a typographic h2 (mono-caps walnut-light, bottom rule, oxblood for Crisis), no accordion toggle; `openSections` state and `toggleSection` function deleted. All sections render simultaneously. **(c) `ConnectionErrorPlate`** at `src/components/shared/ConnectionErrorPlate.tsx` — quiet walnut plate over chat with oxblood "Connection lost" eyebrow, Spectral body, Retry TextBtn + italic dismiss. Replaces the inline oxblood top-rule. **(d) Composing state** — when `checkpointActionState === "confirmed"`, the receipt swaps to a pulsing sage fleuron + italic Spectral "Putting it on the page…"; after the entry lands and the historical render kicks in, the receipt shows "Saved to Layer Two" walnut mono-caps (ordinal derived from `LAYER_ORDINAL`). **(e) Empty Manual placeholder** — italic Spectral line "Your Manual fills as you and Jove find patterns together." above the layer headers when no entries exist. **(f) Welcome chips → walnut cards** — the three `WELCOME_CHIPS` now render as glass cards with a leading walnut chevron, italic Spectral chip text, blur + shadow; the "Help me get started" link became a styled Guided intake card with title + sub. **(g) Explore-further chip** — walnut pill at top of chat surfaces the entry the user tapped Explore Further on (mono-caps `LAYER N · LayerName` eyebrow + Spectral entry name + dismiss ✕). `MainApp` tracks `currentExploration` in state, snapshots from the `handleExploreWithPersona` callback, and clears when the conversation switches. **Desktop layout audit + fixes (post-merge).** Five issues from a 1280×900 viewport audit of onboarding/login/Settings: (a) `EntryScreen` rendered edge-to-edge at desktop widths (no max-width container, BEGIN pill full-bleed, brittle `top: 38%` pin). Rewritten with a responsive `.mw-entry-column` (max-width 560→600→640px), `flex justify-content: center` on the wrapper for true vertical centering, hero wordmark scaled `72→88→96→120px` across mobile/tablet/desktop/wide, BEGIN pill capped at `maxWidth: 320px`, thesis `max-width: 44ch`. (b) Duplicate wordmark on every wrapped surface — outer DesktopVitrine masthead AND in-frame TopBar wordmark both showed at ≥1030px. TopBar wordmark span gets `className="mw-topbar-wordmark"`; globals.css adds `@media (min-width: 1030px) { .mw-phone-frame .mw-topbar-wordmark { visibility: hidden; } }` (visibility, not display, so flex layout slot stays — back/menu/feedback positions stable). Below 1030px the outer masthead is hidden and the in-frame wordmark remains. (c) `LoginScreen` content scroll container gets `justifyContent: center` — forms balance in the phone-frame's 932px desktop height instead of pinning to the top. (d) `SeedScreen` drops the bottom-pinning `flex: 1` spacer for a flex-centered content area (the demo's "pin to bottom" pattern fell to the floor at desktop frame heights). (e) `SeedScreen` disabled Begin border-bottom changes from `--session-ink-whisper` (invisible on the walnut gradient) to `--session-walnut-border` (warm + visible). **Result.** 12 commits across the branch (Phases 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 5, 8+9, two audit fixes, post-ship fixes, Phase 7 follow-ups, then desktop layout fixes); 626 tests pass; build clean; no console errors. Demo at `/demo/glass` remains as a frozen visual reference. See [implementation plan](implementation/dark-mode.md) for the full files-touched map.
- Guided intake instrumentation (2026-05-07, pedantic-hofstadter branch, session 4): PostHog event coverage so the team can measure guided intake performance from the dashboard alone — no DB queries, no transcript reads. **Four questions answered.** (1) Fraction of conversations started in guided-intake: `conversation_started.entry_point` (union expanded to `"situation" | "guided-intake"`). (2) Turn count to first checkpoint: new `user_turn_count` property on `checkpoint_proposed` counts user messages at fire time (cleaner than deriving from `message_number`, which counts assistant turns). (3) Fraction of guided-intake checkpoints accepted/rejected/uncommitted: `mode` property added to `checkpoint_proposed`, `checkpoint_confirmed`, `checkpoint_rejected`, `checkpoint_refined`, `checkpoint_deferred`, `conversation_ended` — uncommitted is derivable from PostHog primitives. (4) Which opener-flow path fired: new `guided_intake_opener_fired` event with `variant ∈ {default, recency_drop, moments_to_states, gentle_end}`, detected client-side by string-matching the assistant turn against the canonical phrases in `system-prompt.ts`. **Plumbing.** Server emits `mode` in every `message_complete` SSE event (`call-persona.ts`); client mirrors mode in a ref and attaches it at fire time. No new server-side PostHog dependency — all events fire from the browser, matching the existing 11-event Stage 1 pattern. PII guard tests updated. **Tests.** 41 new (589 → 630): variant detector unit tests (12, including smart-quote and case-insensitive normalization), wiring assertions on events.ts/useChat.ts/call-persona.ts/sse-parser.ts (16), `user_turn_count` semantics (4 — counts only role==='user'), updated PII guard cases. **Deferred.** No dedicated `no_commit` event — `conversation_ended` fires only on explicit `startNewSession()`, not tab-close; tab-close detection would require a separate session-end signal that doesn't exist today. Q3's "uncommitted" is still answerable by joining `checkpoint_proposed` with the absence of a subsequent `checkpoint_confirmed` before the next `conversation_started`. Build ✓, 630 tests ✓.
- Style-guide alignment of chat surface (2026-05-07, naughty-jones branch, third pass): Five fixes after a three-agent audit against the project's authoritative style guide (now committed to `docs/reference/style-guide/` as 17 sg-*.jsx files extracted from the bundled SG artifact). **(1) JOVE label.** Now uppercase via `textTransform`, letterSpacing 2.2, full `--session-persona` color — matching `sg-chat.jsx` §05.1 micro-caps spec. Was lowercase, 1.5px tracking, `--persona-soft` (half-strength). The redundant `.toUpperCase()` calls at MobileSession.tsx:778 and 883 were dropped since CSS now handles the casing. **(2) Desktop masthead.** `DesktopVitrine.tsx` masthead changed from "my walnut" (with space, no period) to "mywalnut." (no space, sage period) — now matches the mobile chat header wordmark and `sg-tokens.jsx::Wordmark`. **(3) Composer chrome.** ChatInput restructured: `borderTop: 1px solid var(--session-hair)` above the row (per SG §05.5), replacing the previous bottom inset shadow. Send button replaced with the SG TextBtn pattern — "SEND ›" mono-caps text-link with hairline rule beneath, appearing only when text is present. The 32px sage-filled circle with white arrow — bubble chrome the SG explicitly forbids — is gone. The mic icon stays for voice (a mywalnut-specific feature the SG doesn't sanction directly) but recasts to a 28px ink-ghost glyph at the SG expand-glyph register, not a 44px sage circle. **(4) Typing indicator.** Three pulsing dots replaced with a single sage fleuron (`❦`) pulsing — per `sg-chat.jsx` §05.1: a typographic ornament marks "Jove is composing," not speech-bubble dots. **(5) MobileNav active state.** Replaced ink underline with a 3px sage dot below mono-caps. Sage now appears on the bottom bar as the canonical "you are here" cue per `sg-nav.jsx` + `sg-donts.jsx` §94 ("sage is the one thing per screen"). Inactive labels stay at `--session-ink-mid`; active labels lift to `--session-ink` plus the dot. **Plus:** style-guide reference files committed at `docs/reference/style-guide/` (sg-tokens, sg-chat, sg-nav, sg-buttons, sg-foundations, sg-shell, sg-dark, sg-a11y, sg-component-states, sg-donts, sg-inputs, sg-lists, sg-long-content, sg-overlays, sg-plates, sg-states, sg-app) so future agents can audit against the canonical spec. **Two deliberate divergences left in place** (per user direction in the same session): user text opacity at 0.68 light / 0.70 dark (SG: ink85 = 0.82 — user wanted "a touch lighter"); user message `marginLeft: var(--sp-xl)` 56px (SG keeps user in same column as Jove, distinguished only by rail+italic — user wanted clearer "user side"). 564 tests pass. Build ✓. See [merge commit 1dd2550](https://github.com/h2onation/mantle/commit/1dd2550).
- Guided intake mode (2026-05-07, pedantic-hofstadter branch, 3 sessions): Full pipeline for a new "guided-intake" conversation mode where Jove sends the opening message instead of waiting for the user. **(1) Prompt module.** New GUIDED INTAKE block in `system-prompt.ts` (Tier 3, conditional on `mode === "guided-intake"`). Contains an opener prompt, a deepening rhythm, five layer-probing questions, and a readiness gate. The opener text lives in a shared constant (`GUIDED_INTAKE_OPENER` in `src/lib/persona/guided-intake-copy.ts`) imported by the prompt builder. **(2) Mode column.** Migration `20260507000000_conversation_mode.sql` adds a `mode TEXT DEFAULT 'situation'` column to `conversations` with a CHECK constraint (`situation`, `guided-intake`). API route validates mode on create. Persona pipeline reads mode from the conversation row and passes it through to the prompt builder. **(3) Frontend affordance.** "Help me get started" text link appears in all three empty-conversation states in `MobileSession.tsx` (first-time user, returning user, fallback). Click triggers `startGuidedIntake()` in `useChat.ts`, which POSTs to `/api/chat` with `{ message: null, mode: "guided-intake" }` — reuses the existing `triggerPersonaOpener` pattern to have Jove speak first. Guards: isLoading/isStreaming, messages.length > 0. **(4) Tests.** 29 new tests across 4 files: system prompt rendering (6), API mode validation (4), pipeline mode integration (3), opener constant + UI wiring (12). All pass. **Deployment note:** the `conversations.mode` column migration was applied to the remote Supabase DB on 2026-05-07 (verified via `supabase migration list` — present on both Local and Remote). Build ✓, 589 tests ✓.
- Theme polish — dark-mode review fixes (2026-05-07, naughty-jones branch, second pass): Seven fixes after a dark-mode design review identified rough edges in the initial Linen v2 ship. **(1) Vignette direction.** New `--session-vignette` token (`rgba(22,20,18,0.06)` light / `rgba(0,0,0,0.35)` dark) replaces the previous use of `--session-hair-soft` for corner darkening in MobileLayout and OnboardingFlow. The hair-soft token is light in dark mode (it's used for borders), so corner gradients were producing a halo (lifting edges) instead of a vignette (darkening edges). Now corners darken correctly in both modes. **(2) Bottom nav lifted.** MobileNav background changed from flat `--session-linen` to `--session-cream` (one step warmer/lighter), so the nav reads as a quiet platform instead of a flat slab. Especially needed in dark mode where the chat above has the new vignette darkening, leaving an un-vignetted nav looking "true black" by comparison. **(3) Input focus state.** ChatInput dropped the top inset hairline; the focus state is carried by a single bottom underline. The earlier two-line "groove" treatment perceptually fused into a 4-side outline in dark mode where hairlines lift more. **(4) User message indent.** User messages now sit at `marginLeft: var(--sp-xl)` (56px) with `paddingLeft: var(--sp-sm)` instead of `paddingLeft: var(--sp-md)` (22px). Their rail starts past Jove's column, creating clear "user side" affordance — marginalia-on-the-right rather than two voices in the same gutter. **(5) Jove voice softened in dark.** `--session-ink-persona` now resolves to `rgba(242,234,214,0.72)` in dark mode (was full `#F2EAD6` matching `--session-ink`). Jove no longer shouts in pure cream against a dark ground. Light mode unchanged. The Jove rail strengthened from `--persona-soft` (sage at 0.32) to full `--session-persona` for clearer differentiation against the muted user rail. **(6) Desktop theme toggle.** DesktopVitrine colophon now exposes `Theme · sys · light · dark` inline with the existing version/Privacy/Terms meta row, with the active option underlined. Same `useTheme` hook as Settings → Appearance on mobile. Wide and narrow desktop both show it. **(7) Settings APPEARANCE expanded by default.** Was hidden behind a `▾` accordion next to ACCOUNT; now expanded on first load so the SYSTEM/LIGHT/DARK toggle is visible without a click. Plus: `--session-glow-scroll` lowered from 0.10 to 0.04 in dark mode so the top-of-chat fade reads as a gradient rather than a sage stripe. Verified in browser at mobile + 1280×800 desktop in both color schemes; theme round-trip works (system → light → dark → system) with no flash, no hydration errors, no console errors. 564 tests pass. Build ✓. See [merge commit 68c30fe](https://github.com/h2onation/mantle/commit/68c30fe).
- Desktop canvas polish — texture + frame shadow on vitrine (2026-05-07, cool-goldstine branch): Two CSS-only changes to `DesktopVitrine.tsx` so the desktop canvas reads as continuous material rather than a phone floating on a flat surface. **(1) Canvas texture.** The outer parchment canvas now carries the same paper-surface treatment as the phone interior panels — fractal noise SVG at 1.2% opacity (half the 2.5% used inside the phone) plus a radial vignette darkening 2% at the far corners (half the 4% inside). Reduces the visual gap between the frame interior and the surrounding surface. **(2) Frame shadow.** Phone frame border dimmed from 8% to `var(--session-hair-soft)` (uses the new Linen v2 token) and a soft diffused `box-shadow: 0 0 80px rgba(26,22,20,0.06)` added. Shadow reads as depth (page lifts from folio); hard border alone read as device edge. Both changes are invisible on mobile — the `clamp()` border trick resolves to 0px at ≤430px, and the canvas is behind the full-viewport phone frame. Build ✓, 564 tests ✓.
- Theme system — Linen Design System v2 + system-driven dark mode (2026-05-07, naughty-jones branch): Two interlocking changes shipped together. **(1) Linen v2 token rewrite.** Warm bone canvas (`--session-linen: #FAF7F0`) replaces the prior gray-white. Sage shifts from `#556B48` to `#5C6B4E` and gains soft/muted/border/tint variants. Oxblood error (`#7A3B2E`) replaces the prior brick red, and a new amber warning role (`#9A6B2A`) is added for quiet heads-ups. New literary spacing scale (`--sp-hair` through `--sp-xxl`, named not numeric), almost-flat radii (`--radius-none/xs/sm/pill`), and a paper-lift elevation pattern (used sparingly). MobileSession's chip rail is restyled as italic serif text-with-rule links instead of pilled cards; the masthead wordmark becomes "mywalnut." with a sage period; checkpoint card actions restructure into a title-case mono-caps primary button ("Put it in my Manual" / "Put it in as it is") plus lowercase italic serif secondary actions ("close but not quite", "this is not me", "let it go"). All hardcoded hex/rgba values across components were replaced with CSS custom properties so the system adapts cleanly. **(2) Dark mode.** System-driven by default with explicit override. An inline FOUC-prevention `<script>` in `<head>` reads `mw-theme` from localStorage before paint, sets the `data-theme` attribute on `<html>`, and updates the `theme-color` meta. Three-state toggle (system / light / dark) lives in Settings → Appearance, with a mono-caps text-with-rule pattern matching the rest of the design system. New `useTheme` hook (`src/lib/hooks/useTheme.ts`) uses `useSyncExternalStore` to track preference and resolved values, listens to `matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)')` for system changes, and dispatches DOM updates on every preference change. New `ThemeInit` component mounts at the layout root and is a no-render wrapper around the hook. `suppressHydrationWarning` on `<html>` prevents the React mismatch from the FOUC script setting attributes before hydration. Dark tokens are LIFTED for legibility on dark ground rather than simply inverted: linen → `#15110C`, ink → `#F2EAD6`, sage → `#9CB18A` (lifted from `#5C6B4E`), oxblood → `#D08278` (lifted from `#7A3B2E`). `--lift` shadow uses `rgba(0,0,0,0.20/0.25)` in dark vs subtle warm shadows in light. **Verification.** Tested in browser preview at mobile and desktop viewports, both color schemes, with theme toggle round-trip (system → light → dark → system). No flash on load, no hydration errors, no console errors. Build ✓, 564 tests ✓. **Test file revisions.** `src/components/onboarding/onboarding-copy.test.ts` updated to expect the new lowercase secondary button labels (`close but not quite`, `this is not me`, `let it go`). Older title-case assertions were treated as outdated since those buttons are now intentionally rendered in lowercase italic serif (literal lowercase, not via text-transform). Primary button assertions (title-case mono-caps "Put it in my Manual" / "Put it in as it is") preserved. See [merge commit 2a8b595](https://github.com/h2onation/mantle/commit/2a8b595).
- Landing — rail-and-numeral architecture, full rewrite from scratch (2026-04-22, page-design branch): Fourth landing pass today, treating the prior file as empty rather than iterating on it. Design direction: the page reads as a small composed document — quiet, premium, confident — but without costume (no Issue-One paratext, no chapter titles, no colophon, no pull-quote, no fleuron). **The one formal gesture** is a thin sage vertical hairline spanning the whole page (canvas `::before` linear-gradient, 1px, fades in at 72px / out at bottom-160px), with hanging markers in the left rail: italic Instrument Serif roman numerals (I–V) for the five Manual layers, JetBrains Mono two-digit numerals (01 / 02 / 03) for the method steps. The rail gives the composition architecture; the markers tie the spine to content without announcement. **Structure, in reading order.** (1) **Masthead** — wordmark "my walnut" + sage period top-left, mono-caps "LOG IN" top-right as a text button (not a link) that fires `onLogin`. Flex justify-between spans the full canvas width, so the wordmark anchors the left edge while the content column below indents past the rail. (2) **Hero** — sage mono eyebrow "mywalnut · a private manual", then headline "A private manual of how you work." at 38 / 68 / 92 / 104px (mobile → ≥1440) in Newsreader 400 with `-0.028em` to `-0.036em` tracking, ink color, sage terminal period. Three-sentence subhead in Newsreader 400 / ink-mid ("my walnut is an AI that helps you write one — through conversation. Nothing enters unless you confirm it. Built for neurodivergent adults."). Outlined CTA `<a href="/waitlist">Join the waitlist →</a>` in ink — ghost button that inverts to ink-filled on hover with a 5px arrow nudge. (3) **Sample Manual entry** — sage mono label "AN ENTRY", ink-faded italic layer marker "from Layer Two — How I process things", italic first-person statement set in Instrument Serif ("When plans shift without warning, my voice is the first thing that goes quiet.") at 25 / 32 / 38px, prose elaboration in Newsreader ("Not because I have nothing to say — but because speech is where my regulation leaves."). (4) **Five layers** — mono-caps kicker "Your Manual, in five layers", then I–V verbatim layer names. The II layer ("How I process things") has a `text-decoration: underline` with sage-border color and 5px offset — a quiet visual tie to the sample entry above. (5) **Method** — three steps with 01 / 02 / 03 mono numerals hanging in the rail, ink-solid lead phrase, ink-mid elaboration. (6) **Final CTA + login + footer** — same ghost CTA, "Already have access? Log in." with inline button, mono-caps `PRIVACY · TERMS`. **Typography.** Newsreader throughout for body, Instrument Serif italic reserved for the sample entry's quoted statement and the roman numerals, JetBrains Mono for eyebrow/kicker/numeral/footer labels only. **Color.** Linen background, ink for content, `--session-persona` (sage) for period marks, eyebrow, roman numerals, mono kicker on the sample label, and the hairline rail. No filled bright buttons, no paper-noise texture, no stock photography. **Motion.** Page-load stagger on hero elements (`mwEntryRise`, 720ms fade-up with 80/200/320/440ms delays on eyebrow/headline/subhead/CTA). No scroll triggers, no rotating specimen. `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` zeros animation. **Responsive.** Four breakpoints with explicit CSS custom properties (`--mw-gutter`, `--mw-rail`, `--mw-column`, `--mw-section-pad`): mobile 20/40/— content-width-driven, tablet 48/96/600, desktop 64/120/720, wide 80/144/760. Canvas is the column + rail, centered — the rail indent creates a deliberate right-shift of content that reads as editorial composition. **Deleted from earlier pass.** `MYWALNUT · A PRIVATE MANUAL` as a standalone paratext masthead, the rotating specimen, the sand-ripples image (already gone), the single-rule sample sidebar. **Tests.** `src/components/onboarding/onboarding-copy.test.ts` revised: positive assertions cover the new sample-label framing, the I–V roman numerals + 01/02/03 method numerals at JSX-line granularity, the masthead Log in affordance alongside the final one, and the four-breakpoint media queries. Negative assertions preserved from prior pass. 48 EntryScreen assertions, 564 total tests pass. Build ✓.
- Landing — clear/premium pass, no costume, sample Manual entry as visual anchor (2026-04-22, design-3.0 branch merged again): Third landing pass in a single day. The earlier editorial-pastiche direction (Issue-One paratext, "On bringing / On listening / On composing / On sharing" chapter titles, centered pull-quote, sand-ripples photograph, rotating thesis specimen, colophon signature line) was all borrowed costume — conventions lifted from literary publications that earned them through different means. Composition looked editorial but didn't do editorial work. Scrapped entirely. Current page carries itself with type, space, and **one product-specific anchor**. Seven moments: (1) **Wordmark** top-left, 19–24px, "my walnut" with sage period. No rule, no paratext. (2) **Hero** — one headline "A private manual of how you work." at 40 / 64 / 88 / 96px (mobile → ≥1440), one three-sentence subhead ("my walnut is an AI that helps you write one — through conversation. Nothing enters unless you confirm it. Built for neurodivergent adults."), one outlined CTA button ("Join the waitlist →") that inverts to ink-filled on hover with a 4px arrow nudge. No filled sage plate (tech-startup grammar), no text-only italic (too precious) — a confident middle path. Hero is centered. (3) **Sample Manual entry** — the product, as type. Left sage rule, mono-caps kicker "HOW I PROCESS THINGS", italic first-person statement in quotes ("When plans shift without warning, my voice is the first thing that goes quiet."), prose elaboration ("Not because I have nothing to say — but because speech is where my regulation leaves."). This single artifact communicates what a Manual IS in one glance. Replaces the sand-ripples photograph, the rotating thesis specimen, and the HeroManualVignette checkpoint-narrative animation all at once. (4) **How it works** — three plain numbered method steps with small sage italic numerals (1/2/3), lead phrase in ink-solid ("Talk to Jove about things on your mind." etc.), elaboration in ink-mid. No roman numerals, no gerund chapter titles. (5) **Five Manual layers** — literal list of `Some of my patterns / How I process things / What helps / How I show up with people / Where I'm strong`, under a mono-caps kicker "Your Manual, in five layers". Shows the product's actual surface area to a first-time visitor. (6) **Final CTA** — same outlined button + "Already have access? Log in." italic text link. (7) **Legal** — mono-caps `Privacy · Terms`. **Typography:** Newsreader throughout (Instrument Serif unchanged elsewhere in the app), JetBrains Mono reserved for the two kicker labels and the legal row. Sage (`--session-persona`) reserved for period marks, the rule on the sample entry, and the method numerals — otherwise the page is pure ink-on-linen. **Motion:** headline/subhead/CTA fade up on load (120/260/400ms staggered); no rotation, no scroll triggers, no hover tricks beyond the arrow nudge. `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` zeros every duration. **No paper-noise SVG background** (earlier passes stacked a 3.5% fractal noise texture + a radial vignette; both gone). Flat linen background — the type does the work. **Deleted:** `public/images/hero-sand.jpg` and `.webp` (sand-ripples atmospheric band is gone from both the markup and the repo), `HeroManualVignette.tsx` had already been deleted in the previous pass. **Tests:** `src/components/onboarding/onboarding-copy.test.ts` inverted all the editorial-pastiche assertions — now asserts the sample Manual entry ships, the three method steps use plain prose, the five layers list renders, and negative-asserts ROTATING_EXAMPLES, "Issue One", "On bringing", "Nothing enters the manual" (old pull-quote), "Set in Newsreader" (old colophon), HeroManualVignette imports, and hero-sand image references. 46 assertions, 562 total tests pass. Build ✓. See [merge commit 0c76b99](https://github.com/h2onation/mantle/commit/0c76b99); earlier landing passes today are superseded in source but remain in the commit history ([52f8120](https://github.com/h2onation/mantle/commit/52f8120) editorial-pastiche, [14ba466](https://github.com/h2onation/mantle/commit/14ba466) vignette).
- Landing — editorial periodical direction, rotating thesis specimen, no tech-demo grammar (2026-04-22, design-3.0 branch merged twice): Successor to the earlier same-day "HeroManualVignette" ship, which was reverted once on-screen. The animated checkpoint demo was the right instinct (show the mechanic) but the wrong venue (the hero is for thesis, not demo) — editorial landing pages state, they don't demo. The whole page was restaged as a thin publication; the rotating thesis sentences were restored unframed. **Seven sections, top-to-bottom.** (1) **Masthead** — wordmark "my walnut." with a sage-colored period, alongside two-line mono-caps paratext ("ISSUE ONE · SPRING 2026 / A MANUAL FOR NEURODIVERGENT ADULTS"). On desktop they sit split left/right with a hairline rule drawing across the width on mount; on mobile they stack centered. The old "Built for neurodivergent adults." subhead is absorbed into the paratext so the hero doesn't carry redundant framing. (2) **Hero** — display headline "A more complete manual / of how your mind / works." broken across three lines with manual `<br>`s, pushed to 108px on desktop and 136px at ≥1440; the sage period at the end is the seal. Below, the ten original thesis sentences (`ROTATING_EXAMPLES` in `src/components/onboarding/EntryScreen.tsx`) rotate one at a time in italic Newsreader with a sage bullet prefix. 6.5s dwell (up from 4.5s) and a 520ms crossfade — each sentence reads like a photograph. Unframed: no card, no "IN THE MANUAL" kicker. (3) **Sand-ripples atmospheric band** — unchanged from the earlier ship. Full-bleed editorial photograph at `/public/images/hero-sand.jpg` (572KB, 1600px max; `.webp` source also committed), edges soft-faded into the linen via `mask-image: linear-gradient`, grain overlay at 14% opacity / `mix-blend-mode: multiply`. Heights: 200px mobile, 260px tablet, 360px desktop. (4) **Method** — four beats restaged as chapters. Roman numerals (`i.`, `ii.`, `iii.`, `iv.`) sit in the left margin on desktop (120px column with `position: absolute`), stacked inline on mobile/tablet. Each chapter opens with an italic title — *On bringing*, *On listening*, *On composing*, *On sharing* — above the prose body. The old "An oversimplification of how it works:" heading is gone; chapters don't need a signpost. Beats separated by a single hairline. (5) **Pull-quote chapter break** — "Nothing enters the manual / unless you confirm it." at 54px italic on desktop (64px at ≥1440), centered, floating in ~176px of whitespace. The product's promise elevated to a statement. (6) **CTA** — text-only. An italic intro line ("A manual waits to be written."), then a 48–56px italic "Join the waitlist →" whose arrow shifts right 8px on hover. The filled sage-green `<a>` plate is gone — it was the one piece of tech-startup grammar on the page and imported the wrong register. The text-link treatment matches editorial landing pages in the segment (Aesop, Le Labo, Commune, Rizzoli). (7) **Footer** — fleuron (❦) above an italic colophon line ("Set in Newsreader. Printed on linen. Assembled in conversation.") that signs the document, above the mono-caps Privacy · Terms legal row. **Motion.** One orchestrated page-load reveal (masthead fade, rule draw, headline fade-up, specimen fade-in) spanning ~1s total. The rotating specimen is the only continuing animation. `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` zeros all durations and forces the rule to its drawn state. **Deleted.** `HeroManualVignette.tsx` was removed entirely (it was introduced three commits earlier this same date and reverted); all its markup, keyframes, and the `useMemo` workaround for dev-mode style-block re-emission are gone. `(Coming soon)` qualifiers on method items 3 and 4 stay dropped — the page is present-tense throughout, not a roadmap. **Tests.** `src/components/onboarding/onboarding-copy.test.ts` inverted the HeroManualVignette assertions (now asserts the ten thesis sentences ship and that `HeroManualVignette` is not imported), added assertions for masthead paratext, chapter titles, pull-quote copy, editorial CTA copy, and the colophon line. Full suite 562 passing (from 560 — two net-new assertions). Build ✓, 562 tests ✓. See [merge commit 52f8120](https://github.com/h2onation/mantle/commit/52f8120) for the full diff of this successor ship; [merge commit 14ba466](https://github.com/h2onation/mantle/commit/14ba466) is the earlier vignette ship that was superseded.
- Track A — checkpoint and onboarding revamp (2026-04-22, design-3.0 branch merged): Eight-gate implementation replacing the inline progress-signals model with an explicit modal flow, revising landing + onboarding copy, and restructuring the checkpoint confirm loop. **Modal state machine:** single integer `profiles.modal_progress` (0-3) with idempotent server-side advance via `POST /api/modal-progress`; advance only on user dismissal, fires sequentially across sessions, terminal at 3. `GET /api/modal-progress` returns `{modal_progress, signup_at_ms, is_anonymous}` so anonymous-auth users are suppressed from the flow entirely. Three new modals in `src/components/modals/`: `ChatWindowModal` fires on first chat-window entry when `modal_progress < 1`; `PatternFormingModal` fires when `modal_progress === 1 AND any layer signal >= emerging AND concrete_examples >= 1 AND emerging_pattern_snippet !== null`, with the snippet snapshot into local state on first open so the displayed text stays stable through user reads; `FirstCheckpointModal` fires before the first checkpoint card render when `modal_progress === 2 && activeCheckpoint !== null`, with the card render suppressed while the modal is open. All three: Escape/Tab handling, focus trap, focus restore, body scroll lock with compose-safe restoration. Five new PostHog trackers: `modal_1_shown` / `modal_2_shown` / `modal_3_shown` on render, `modal_flow_completed` on POST-success from Modal 3 (not optimistic — skews funnel metrics otherwise), `first_checkpoint_completed` on first lifetime confirmation. PostHog's implicit distinct_id and `$session_id` are trusted (no duplication in props); only `time_since_signup_ms` is explicit. **EntryScreen revamp:** new copy, new responsive treatment — mobile 28px-padded single column, desktop 880px centered reading column (escapes `DesktopVitrine` on desktop so the landing reads as a real web page instead of a 430px phone-frame column), wordmark scales 13/18/22px across breakpoints, legal footer gains hairline + 480px centered max-width + 12px type on desktop. Rotating examples unchanged. Primary CTA inverted to "Join the waitlist" plate, secondary "Already have access? Log in." italic link. InfoScreens dropped 4→2 paragraphs (daily-for-two-weeks reminder); SeedScreen 3→2. `{PERSONA_NAME}` interpolation preserved across copy. **Checkpoint card + action restructure:** display labels "Put it in my Manual" / "Close but not quite" / "This is not me" (underlying `confirmed/refined/rejected` action values unchanged). New `deferred` action type for the refinement ceiling's "Let it go" — same DB behavior as rejected (`status="rejected"`) but distinct system message `[User let the checkpoint go]` so Jove bypasses the POST-REJECTION fixed line (the user already explained twice what was off). `refinement_count` field on `checkpoint_meta` (JSONB, no migration); `computeInheritedRefinementCount` chain rule: unbroken `refined` status carries count forward, any terminal status resets to 0. Ceiling card UI (inline conditional render, not a new component) fires at `refinement_count >= 2` with two-button fork ("Put it in as it is" → confirmed, "Let it go" → deferred). `trackCheckpointDeferred` analytics separate from `trackCheckpointRejected` for ceiling-rate monitoring. **Post-confirm flow + inline transition:** extended SSE protocol to support multiple `message_complete` events per streaming response; client discriminates bubble vs card via `checkpoint` null/non-null (no new field). `cleanContent` now mandatory on the final `message_complete` because the client resets its text buffer per event. New `prependedMessages` primitive on `callPersona` options (type intentionally has no `checkpoint` field — compile-time guard preventing shortcut checkpoint delivery). First-lifetime confirmation: Message 1 "In. A working name: '[HEADLINE].' Yours to change." server-templated (no LLM call — deterministic, eliminates deviation risk on the stamp), Message 2 LLM-generated via new `postConfirmMode="first-message-2"` system-prompt block. Subsequent confirmation: single LLM message via `postConfirmMode="subsequent-single"` with server-built entries summary (Oxford-comma pluralization in `buildEntriesSummary`). Subsequent checkpoints get an inline transition message ("Something else is forming. Let me put this one in front of you.") inserted as a separate assistant row with `created_at = jovResponse.created_at - 1s` so reload queries return it before the card. Existing `POST-CHECKPOINT` prompt block deleted — replaced by the two mode-specific blocks. `useChat` refactored for multi-event streams (append per event, reset buffer per event, finalize per event). **Post-rejection fixed line** (added Phase 7-Low): `POST-REJECTION` block instructs Jove to respond with exactly "Okay. What made it miss?" on the turn immediately after a rejection, then return to natural exploration. **Returning-user opener tightened:** three-part template — "Welcome back." / reference to recent entry or open thread / "What is on your mind today?" — activated-user carve-out drops both the greeting and the Manual reference. **Gate 8 deletions:** welcome-prose block removed from `MobileSession` (Modal 1 replaces); `PROGRESS SIGNALS` block removed from `system-prompt.ts` (modals replace EARLY FRAME / DEPTH BUILDING / CHECKPOINT APPROACHING); `PROGRESS SIGNAL DELIVERY` section removed from extraction prompt; `early_frame_delivered` / `depth_signal_delivered` / `approaching_signal_delivered` fields removed from `ExtractionState` and all reducer paths. Collateral: the long-standing pre-existing typecheck error in `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.test.ts` (fixture missing `observation_miss_count`) resolved during Gate 8 — `npx tsc --noEmit` is clean for the first time since early April. **Deploy order (STRICT, ONE-DIRECTIONAL — SERVER FIRST THEN CLIENT):** the new server can serve both old and new clients, but the new client cannot tolerate old server. Old server emits one `message_complete` per stream; new client resets its buffer on each `message_complete` and expects `cleanContent` on each — against an old server, non-first messages would render empty. Server must deploy first and old clients must roll forward before 7e/7f paths exercise. The `?:` optional fields on the SSE payload (`emergingPatternSnippet`, `hasLayerEmergingOrBeyond`, `concreteExamples`, `checkpoint.refinement_count`) are backward-compatible in one direction only: old clients ignore unknown fields against new server (OK); new clients degrade on missing fields against old server (not OK if multi-event paths fire). **Known gap (out of scope):** text-channel parity for the refinement ceiling and `deferred` action. Text users repeatedly typing NOT QUITE will keep incrementing `refinement_count` server-side, but no text-equivalent of the ceiling UI or defer keyword exists — no text ceiling fires. Acceptable for beta; text channel is not shipping this phase. **Reserved observability flag:** `modalStateLoading` in `MainApp.tsx` distinguishes "modal-progress GET in-flight" from "GET completed with no data"; read side is intentionally unused, setter fires in the fetch's `finally`, documented inline with an `eslint-disable` directive and a rationale comment. Available for future retry/observability logic; if no consumer arrives in a few weeks, next engineer to read the comment can delete safely. **Manual-eval checklist** planned as `docs/track-a-manual-eval.md` — deferred; not authored in this ship. Should be produced before the next meaningful Jove-prompt change so snippet quality / post-confirm compliance can be validated against a reusable conversation set. Build ✓, 560 tests ✓ (from 472 — 88 new across onboarding-copy + system-prompt blocks + extraction parser + events + modals + persona-pipeline helpers + sse-parser), typecheck ✓. See Gate 8 commit for the full diff (31 files changed, 3101 insertions, 574 deletions).
- Extraction pipeline hardening — state wipe, observability, waitUntil (2026-04-21): Three commits closing diagnostic work on intermittent extraction failures observed in Vercel logs on 2026-04-17 (three `DOMException [AbortError]` + one `Anthropic API returned unexpected response shape` over a four-hour window, all under the `[extraction] Failed:` prefix). Extraction is fire-and-forget from both web (`/api/chat`, edge runtime) and text (SMS webhooks, nodejs runtime); it writes `conversations.extraction_state` to feed Jove's next-turn research brief and does not produce Manual entries (those come from `confirmCheckpoint`). **(1) State-wipe fix** (`src/lib/persona/extraction.ts`): catch block used to return `{...state, next_prompt: "", sage_brief: ""}` on failure, which `fireBackgroundExtraction` then wrote to the DB — one failed extraction silently blanked the prior turn's working brief on top of the original error, degrading Jove's next turn. Now re-throws; `fireBackgroundExtraction`'s existing .catch already skips the DB write so the prior good state stays authoritative. The `[extraction] Failed:` log is preserved so existing ops queries match. **(2) Structured observability** (`src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts`, `src/lib/anthropic.ts`): two new structured log lines per extraction attempt — `[persona-pipeline] extraction_attempt {conversation_id, message_count, is_first_checkpoint}` as the failure-rate denominator and `[persona-pipeline] extraction_failed {conversation_id, error_class, error_message, error_name}`. New `classifyExtractionError()` helper buckets into `abort` / `response_shape` / `parse` / `http` / `other` so log queries can group failures by cause instead of grepping free-form error messages. On the "unexpected response shape" path, `anthropicFetch` now emits `[anthropic] unexpected_response_shape` with sanitized payload metadata (keys, type, stop_reason, content_length, content_types, and any Anthropic-framework error_type/error_message fields) before throwing — never logs `content[i].text` per the CLAUDE.md Security Rule against logging user content. Enables measuring the failure rate and seeing a real Error B payload next occurrence. **(3) waitUntil** (`src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts`): wraps the extraction promise in `waitUntil` from `@vercel/functions` so the Vercel platform keeps the function alive until extraction settles. This addresses the dominant failure (Error A, AbortError) — when the parent request's response closes before the Sonnet call returns, Vercel was terminating the in-flight fetch and throwing DOMException. Next.js 14 Route Handlers don't expose a native waitUntil (Next 15's `after()` does); `@vercel/functions` is the canonical shim and works on both edge and nodejs runtimes. Off-Vercel (local dev, tests) it degrades to a plain promise, so no test changes needed. `@vercel/functions@3.4.3` added as a direct dep (was already transitive via posthog-node). **Not done, gated on observation**: tool-use migration for extraction / classifier / confirm-checkpoint — deferred until the new payload log captures another Error B occurrence so the rewrite isn't done blind. Post-merge watch: `error_class: "abort"` counts should drop sharply; `error_class: "response_shape"` firings will pair with the `[anthropic] unexpected_response_shape` line by timestamp and carry the sanitized payload needed to decide on tool use. Build ✓, 472 tests ✓.
- Jove response-structure validator + voice rule 15 + dash/loaded-question exemplars (2026-04-16): Follow-ups from `/evaluate` on a first-session transcript that caught the two recurring drift patterns the prompt wasn't stopping — multiple question marks per turn and em dashes joining clauses — plus a third pattern where Jove was smuggling its hypothesis into questions and getting the user to confirm its own frame. **Validator:** new `validateResponseStructure(content, messageId)` in `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts` (co-located with `validateComposedEntry`). Two checks: `question_count` (counts `?` characters — 0 or 1 ok for the post-confirmation transition, 2+ is a violation) and `dash_usage` (counts `—` plus spaced en-dash/hyphen sequences matching ` – | - `). Logs structured `console.warn` lines as `[persona-pipeline] response_validation { type, check, count, message_id }`. **Non-blocking for beta** — logs only, does not interrupt conversation flow. Promotes to rewrite/strip only after log volume justifies it. Wired into both persona paths: `call-persona.ts` at step 11a and `persona-bridge.ts` after DB insert. Runs on raw `fullText` not `conversationalText`, because `CRISIS_RESOURCES` contains an em dash that would produce false positives on `dash_usage`. **Voice rule 15:** "Never load a question with the answer you expect. If your hypothesis is inside the question, the user is confirming your frame, not finding their own. Rewrite as an open invitation." Added to `voice-autistic.ts` `VOICE_RULES` (14 → 15); doc comment updated to drop the hardcoded count. `system-prompt.test.ts` assertions use `VOICE_RULES.length` dynamically so no test change required. Count comments bumped in `CLAUDE.md` (`VOICE_RULES (14)` → `(15)`), `docs/rules.md` ("The 14 voice rules" → "The 15 voice rules"), and the `system-prompt.ts` Tier 2 block comment ("14 voice rules" → "voice rules"). **Dash exemplar:** added inline with the Tier 2 VOICE dash rule in `system-prompt.ts` — two bad/good pairs (`"She went quiet — what did you do?"` / `"She went quiet. What did you do?"` and `"Not the wrong thing — the true thing."` / `"Not the wrong thing. The true thing."`). Rule existed but kept getting ignored; pairing bad/good examples next to it makes the period-replacement obvious to the model. **Loaded-question exemplar:** added to DEEPENING weak→strong list: `"Do you feel like everyone else got the manual and you didn't?"` → `"What happens when you realize you didn't know the code?"`. The bad form packs Jove's read into the question (eval's T5 violation); the good form opens the same territory without predetermining the answer. Build ✓, 453 tests ✓.
- Messaging audit — content redaction, metadata-only (2026-04-16): Hotfix on top of ADR-035. Pre-fix schema stored full message text in `messaging_events.content` — user-authored inbound, Jove-authored outbound, and **OTP codes on OTP-send rows**. Caught at Checkpoint E of the Sendblue migration before any real user text flowed through the system. Violated the CLAUDE.md Security Rule against logging user content and auth tokens. **Fix (ADR-037):** `src/lib/messaging/send.ts` now exports `MessageContentKind = "otp" | "user" | "jove" | "system"` and a `redactForAudit()` helper. Every outbound insert runs content through the helper before writing to `messaging_events`. Replacement strings: `[OTP_SEND]`, `[USER_MSG len=N]`, `[JOVE_REPLY len=N]`, `[SYSTEM_MSG len=N]`. Default when unspecified is `"system"` — safe fallback. Inbound Sendblue webhook applies the same transform inline (`[USER_MSG len=N]`) and replaces `raw_payload` with a fields-only projection so the full message body never lands there either. Sendblue outbound insert now omits `raw_payload` entirely because Sendblue's response echoes full content in it. **Call sites tagged (16 total):** all 14 in `src/lib/linq/message-router.ts` (keyword/media/rate-limit/fallback/unknown-number/confirm-ack/refine-ack/reject-ack → `"system"`; Jove response, checkpoint text, post-confirm, post-refine, post-reject follow-ups → `"jove"`), plus `src/app/api/user/phone/route.ts` OTP send → `"otp"` and `src/app/api/user/phone/verify/route.ts` greeting → `"system"`. **Backfill:** new migration `20260417000008_redact_messaging_events_content.sql` nulls `content` on any legacy rows whose value doesn't already start with `[`. Idempotent. Applied to prod. **Tests:** 442 pass — the route test mocks `sendMessage` by shape, so the new `contentKind` param passed through doesn't affect existing assertions. Build ✓, lint ✓. See ADR-037.
- Beta Health panel — PR 3 of 3: feedback panel (2026-04-16): Third and final panel for the Beta Health tab. **UI only — no new routes.** Reuses the existing `/api/admin/beta-feedback` endpoint (GET returns `{ items, unread_count }`, PATCH `{ id }` marks one read) already consumed by `BetaFeedbackTab`. **Panel** — new `src/components/admin/FeedbackPanel.tsx` mirrors `ApiErrorsPanel` layout conventions (mono uppercase section header, hairline-bordered list, "✓ Clean" collapse when empty). Narrower scope than `BetaFeedbackTab`: unread rows only, no pagination, no full history. Each row renders user_email, timestamp, optional page_context, the feedback_text in full, and a monospace "Mark read" button. When `unread_count === 0`, collapses to the same "✓ Clean — No unread feedback" banner ApiErrorsPanel uses. For full history browsing, admin still goes to the Feedback section. **Data flow** — `admin/page.tsx` calls `data.loadBetaFeedback()` when `section === "health"` so the panel loads with fresh data. `useAdminData` is the single source of truth — marking read in the Health panel updates `betaFeedbackUnreadCount` immediately and the Feedback-tab nav badge tracks it. No new badge on the Health nav entry for feedback (per scope — Health's red pill is for API errors; the Feedback tab's existing badge remains the primary nag for unread). Mounted below `ActiveUsersPanel` in the Health section. Verified end-to-end: submitted a test feedback via `BetaFeedbackButton` → appeared in panel with ⚠ 3 unread + all rows → clicked Mark read → row disappeared, count dropped to 2 → marked the remaining two → panel collapsed to ✓ Clean banner, Feedback nav badge hidden. Beta Health panel triptych now complete (errors, active users, feedback). Build ✓, 453 tests ✓.
- fix(api): force dynamic rendering on 17 routes using cookies (2026-04-16): Real issue surfaced by the Beta Health Errors panel within hours of PR 1 shipping — Next.js was logging "Dynamic server usage" errors during static-generation analysis on every route that used `@/lib/supabase/server createClient()` (or `verifyAdmin()`, which wraps it). Both paths call `next/headers cookies()` which is strictly dynamic. Routes worked at runtime but the build-time warnings were being caught by the outer try/catch wraps PR 1 added and flooding `api_errors` with ~10 noise rows per build. Fix is a single line per file: `export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";` right after imports. Tells Next.js not to attempt static rendering at all. 17 routes touched (admin: beta-allowlist, beta-feedback, conversations, feedback, manual, messages, users, waitlist; user: /api/user/phone, /api/user/phone/verify; public: /api/manual, /api/conversations, /api/analytics/identity, /api/onboarding-status, /api/auth/signup, /api/auth/logout, /api/settings/link-phone). Not touched: edge-runtime routes (already opt out of static rendering), admin routes that already had the export. Also issued SQL to purge stale rows: `delete from public.api_errors where error_message like 'Dynamic server usage%';`. Nice "tool works" moment — the observability shipped in PR 1 caught an issue within hours that would have otherwise sat as noise in the build logs. Build ✓, 453 tests ✓.
- Beta Health panel — PR 2 of 3: active beta users (2026-04-16): Second of three panels for the admin Health tab. Shows who's on the beta allowlist, who's signed in ever, and who was active in the last 24h / 7d — the "who should I check on" signal at 7am after beta launches. **Endpoint** — new `src/app/api/admin/active-users/route.ts` runs parallel queries: `beta_allowlist (email, created_at)` and `supabase.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 })` (matches `/api/admin/beta-feedback` — default 50/page would silently truncate). Builds an email → authUser map (lowercased both sides for safety), emits per-user rows (email, user_id, allowlisted_at, signed_in_ever, last_sign_in_at) plus a 4-stat summary (total_allowlisted, ever_signed_in, active_last_24h, active_last_7d). verifyAdmin() JWT guard. No new migration. **Panel** — new `src/components/admin/ActiveUsersPanel.tsx` mirrors `ApiErrorsPanel` layout conventions (mono uppercase section header, card treatment, hairline-bordered list). No window selector — the data is a single snapshot. No nav badge — the list of beta users is reference info, not something to nag about. Summary banner renders as a 4-column stat grid with mono uppercase labels and sans-500 values. User rows: email + optional `· INVITED, NO SIGN-IN` chip when `last_sign_in_at` is null + relative "allowlisted Nd ago" + relative "Nd ago / today / yesterday / never" last-sign-in. Exact ISO timestamps available on hover via `title` attr. Mounted below `ApiErrorsPanel` in the Health section of `admin/page.tsx`. Verified locally on prod allowlist: 5 allowlisted, 3 signed-in-ever, 1 active 24h/7d, "today" rendered correctly for recent activity. Build ✓, 453 tests ✓.
- Beta Health panel — PR 1 of 3: errors from last 24h (2026-04-16): First of three panels planned for the admin Health tab ahead of beta launch. Catches uncaught API errors that users don't report — 500s, thrown exceptions, and Supabase query failures on wrapped routes — so a 7am "what broke overnight" check is one glance at /admin?section=health. **Table** — new migration `20260417000007_api_errors.sql` creates `public.api_errors` with columns (route, method, status_code, error_message, error_stack, user_id_hash text (16-char hex from hashUserId, no FK), request_id, created_at), two indexes (created_at DESC, (route, created_at DESC)), RLS enabled with no user policies — service_role writes and reads via admin API only. Two SECURITY DEFINER RPCs mirror `admin_confirm_stats` / `admin_confirm_recent_failures`: `admin_api_error_stats(p_window_seconds)` returns grouped counts by route plus a total; `admin_api_error_recent(p_limit)` returns the last-N feed. Both granted only to service_role. Migration numbered 7 because the in-progress messaging branch claimed 5 and 6. **Logger** — new `src/lib/observability/record-api-error.ts` mirrors `recordConfirmFailure`: takes the admin client as a param, awaits the insert (small latency vs. losing observability rows under Edge Runtime unhandled-promise warnings) but never throws. Hashes user_id server-side via existing `hashUserId()` so the table never stores raw ids. Captures error_stack when error is an Error instance. **5 routes wrapped** — `/api/chat` (edge), `/api/conversations`, `/api/manual`, `/api/analytics/identity`, `/api/session/summary` (edge).
