Pattern: `let capturedUserId` declared before try; catch reports the user + route + method + error + stack. Internal 500 paths (conversations.insert failure in `/api/chat`, convError in `/api/conversations`, null summary in `/api/session/summary`) log explicitly so Supabase query failures are covered too. `/api/checkpoint/confirm` intentionally NOT wrapped — it has domain-specific `confirm_failures` logging already. Routes that intentionally return 4xx (auth, validation) also not wrapped — those aren't silent failures. **Admin endpoint** — new `src/app/api/admin/errors/route.ts` mirrors `/api/admin/confirm-stats`. `verifyAdmin()` JWT guard, parallel RPC calls, windowSeconds param clamped to [60, 7d], default 24h. **Panel UI** — new `src/components/admin/ApiErrorsPanel.tsx` mirrors `ConfirmHealthPanel` layout inside `/admin?section=health` below `SchemaHealthTab` + `ConfirmHealthPanel`. Window selector (1h/24h/7d), ✓ clean banner or ⚠ alert with by-route breakdown, recent feed (last 50) with `METHOD /route · HTTP STATUS` header and error message ellipsis. **Health nav badge** — `useAdminData` gains `apiErrorsCount` state + `loadApiErrorsSummary` action; `/admin/page.tsx` calls it on every section change (cheap RPC round-trip) so the Health tab's red-pill badge refreshes as the admin navigates. Same treatment as the feedback unread count. Verification deferred post-migration-apply: force a test throw in `/api/chat`, hit as signed-in user, confirm row in `api_errors` + badge + panel render, then remove. Build ✓, 453 tests ✓.
- Design 2.0 — Step 5 (final): wordmark tracking 4→1.5px + landing hero 34→42px (2026-04-16): Closes Design 2.0 with a smaller slice than originally scoped. **Wordmark tracking unified across 8 surfaces** (MobileSession, MobileManual, EntryScreen, LoginScreen, InfoScreens, SeedScreen, waitlist, reset-password): `letterSpacing` 4px → 1.5px. At 20px that drops from 20% to 7.5% of font size; at 13px from 30% to 11%. Both now inside editorial-appropriate tracking ranges. 4px was reading as decorative flourish; 1.5px reads as considered label. Canvas masthead at 52px with `-0.5px` tracking unchanged from Step 4 — large type and small type want different tracking, and the principle "one name, one treatment, different scales" is loose on tracking by design. **Landing hero** (`EntryScreen` "Map your operating system.") bumped 34px → 42px inside the phone at all viewport widths. Approaches Instrument Serif's display-character threshold (~50px) while staying within the 430px phone width (wraps cleanly to "Map your operating / system."). **Canvas right-margin display hero (Option B) considered and rejected**, prototyped twice: (1) hero + subhead + whisper on canvas / phone shows only CTAs — phone went hollow with ~500px of empty linen between wordmark and CTAs, read as frame-around-buttons rather than product preview; (2) hero + subhead on canvas / whisper stays in phone — composition's cohesion fluctuated with whisper length (rotating 10 sentences, half single-line reads as stranded, half two-line holds together), oscillating pass/fail every 4.5s. Honest gate call per explicit instruction: fall back to Option A without further iteration. Canvas right margin on landing remains empty, matching authenticated's intentional negative space. The authenticated-vs-unauthenticated-desktop asymmetry principle remains available for a future pass but was NOT documented as an ADR — there's no asymmetry to document yet; the composition's phone-as-product-preview side would need something denser than CTAs alone (a mock chat surface, or a more substantial editorial artifact) before Option B could pass. Build ✓, 453 tests ✓.
- Design 2.0 — Step 4 complete: shared DesktopVitrine, Jove rail, paper surface, 17px Jove body, side-margin layout (2026-04-16): Four stacked commits closing Design 2.0 Step 4. **DesktopVitrine extraction + paratext consistency fix** — new `src/components/layout/DesktopVitrine.tsx` owns the parchment canvas + masthead + phone frame + colophon scaffolding; both `MobileLayout` (authenticated) and `OnboardingFlow` (landing) wrap their children in it, so landing and authenticated surfaces share one framing by construction (structurally impossible to drift). Masthead dropped 64px → 52px with tight -0.5px tracking (was pulling eye before the phone at 64). Colophon left-aligned in a column whose left edge aligns with the phone (centered 320px wrapping was reading "wedding invitation"; left-aligned matches editorial colophon convention — NYRB, NYR, Ghost). 120px centered hairline between statement and meta removed (orphaned ornament under left-aligned text; typeface change Instrument Serif → JetBrains Mono is itself the register rule). **Jove rail treatment** — `MobileSession.tsx` persona message body and typing indicator drop the tinted-bubble + borderRadius in favor of a 2px `--session-persona-soft` left rail with 14px text indent and 4px vertical padding. Typing and streaming share 4px padding (an annotation mark doesn't resize to fit what it's annotating). JOVE label paddingLeft 4 → 0 so label aligns with rail's left edge, marginBottom 4 → 6px so label sits tethered to rail without touching it. Welcome panel is structurally different (nothing to annotate yet; chips are utilitarian UI) and correctly drops the rail while keeping the JOVE label as voice-introduction — rail is annotation grammar, welcome is introduction grammar. Checkpoint cards unchanged (moment, not utterance — they retain their heavier gradient + shadow + radius treatment). Prototyped and verified at one-sentence, medium, twenty-sentence, and welcome lengths. **Paper surface** — tab panel `backgroundImage` composites the existing 2.5% noise SVG with a new `radial-gradient(ellipse at center, transparent 50%, rgba(26,22,20,0.04) 100%)` corner vignette (4% ink-darken at the far corners, threshold-of-perception). Same vignette applied to `OnboardingFlow`'s inner wrapper so landing and chat share one paper feel. `ChatInput` flat 1px `borderBottom` replaced with a paper-groove composite: `inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.5)` constant top highlight + `inset 0 -1px 0 <state-color>` bottom shadow (`--session-ink-hairline` idle, `--session-persona-border` focus, `--session-persona-soft` recording). Input reads as a recessed zone pressed into the linen rather than a line drawn on top. The global focus ring on `<textarea>` coexists without fighting because it's on a different element and a different visual register (inset groove = ambient "this is the input zone"; focus ring = active "your keyboard is here"). **Typography** — Jove body 16px → 17px (matches `--size-prose`) in chat messages, welcome prose, and the returning-user "What's on your mind?" prompt. User messages stay at 15.5px, preserving the 1.5px differential between drafted input (17) and committed history (15.5). Checkpoint card body stays at 16px Instrument Serif — its hierarchy is managed separately. **Side-margin desktop layout** — at viewports ≥1030px, masthead relocates from top-center to top-LEFT corner (x=48, y=48) and colophon from bottom-center to bottom-LEFT (x=48, y=bottom-48, column ≈ 220-320px adaptive via `min(320px, calc((100vw - 430px) / 2 - 80px))`). Phone drops the 400px vertical reservation and takes full natural `min(932px, 100dvh)` maxHeight — paratext exits the vertical sightline, phone sits at full height, the horizontally-empty left margin does paratext work, and the right margin stays as intentional negative space. Below 1030px, fallback to the prior vertical stack with centered masthead + colophon + 400px phone reservation (existing narrow-desktop users unaffected). Responsive behavior lives in new `globals.css` classes (`.mw-masthead`, `.mw-colophon`, `.mw-colophon-inner`, `.mw-phone-frame`) with media queries at 431px (mobile/desktop) and 1030px (narrow/wide desktop). Verified at 375×812 (mobile unchanged), 1029×900 (fallback), 1030×900 (threshold transition), 1280×900, 1440×900. Build ✓, 453 tests ✓.
- Messaging migration — Sendblue for 1:1, Linq for groups (2026-04-16): Replaces Linq for 1:1 SMS/iMessage. Dual-provider permanently: 1:1 text flows through Sendblue at `+16292925296`, group facilitator stays on Linq because Sendblue does not expose `participant.added` / `participant.removed` / `chat.created` events or a `getChatInfo` equivalent. **Architecture:** new `src/lib/messaging/` module contains `provider.ts` (env flag), `phone.ts` (strict E.164 validation), `sendblue.ts` (API client — `sb-api-key-id` + `sb-api-secret-key` headers, POST `/api/send-message`, throws on non-2xx with parsed `error_message`), `linq.ts` (wrapper that adapts Linq's chat_id API to the unified shape, encapsulates phone → `phone_numbers.linq_chat_id` lookup and first-message `createChat` fallback with automatic chat-id persistence — non-throwing, returns `{ok: false, ...}` on failure), and `send.ts` (unified `sendMessage({to, content, ownerUserId?, linqGroupChatId?})` — non-throwing contract preserved so existing callers that checked `{ok: false}` keep working). Routing: `linqGroupChatId` present forces Linq; otherwise `MESSAGING_PROVIDER` (defaults to `sendblue`). **Audit:** new `messaging_events` table (20260417000005) writes one row per outbound send and one per inbound webhook. Partial unique index on `(provider, provider_message_id) where provider_message_id is not null` dedupes Sendblue retries (3× with 45s timeout). RLS enabled, no public policies — service-role only, same pattern as `safety_events`. **Inbound:** new `/api/webhooks/sendblue/route.ts` logs the header key set on first deploy so the secret header name can be confirmed, then returns 200 on every non-5xx path (idempotent insert catches 23505 and treats as expected). Signature verification deferred until first real webhook lands. **Call sites migrated (1:1 only):** `src/lib/linq/message-router.ts` (keyword responses, unknown-number, rate-limit, media-only, Jove response, checkpoint follow-ups, fallback — `handleCheckpointResponse` gains a `senderPhone` param; `sendTypingIndicator` + `markAsRead` stay Linq-direct since Sendblue does not expose them), `src/app/api/user/phone/route.ts` (OTP send), `src/app/api/user/phone/verify/route.ts` (greeting send, manual `linq_chat_id` store-back removed — wrapper handles it). **Untouched (groups stay on Linq):** `src/lib/linq/group-bridge.ts`, `src/lib/linq/group-detection.ts`, `src/lib/linq/group-state.ts`, `src/app/api/linq/webhook/route.ts` (now carries a deprecation banner for the 1:1 path only). **E.164 enforcement:** migration 20260417000006 adds CHECK constraint `phone_numbers_phone_e164` (idempotent via pg_constraint guard); phone audit on prod showed 2/2 rows already strict E.164 so the drafted normalize script was unnecessary and deleted. **Env:** `MESSAGING_PROVIDER=sendblue`, `SENDBLUE_API_KEY_ID`, `SENDBLUE_API_SECRET_KEY`, `SENDBLUE_FROM_NUMBER=+16292925296`, `SENDBLUE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_MESSAGING_FROM_NUMBER=+16292925296` added across Prod/Preview/Dev. `LINQ_*` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_LINQ_PHONE_NUMBER` retained for rollback. **Rollback:** flip `MESSAGING_PROVIDER=linq` (outbound only); inbound stays dual-wired. See ADR-035 (dual-provider architecture) and ADR-036 (no dev Supabase — migration applied to prod via dashboard paste; infrastructure debt to resolve before scale). Side fix: `.env.local` had `DEEPGRAM_API_KEY` and `DEV_USER_EMAIL` concatenated onto one line — split. Build ✓, 442 tests ✓.
- PostHog follow-ups — PII guard test, init hardening, startExploration 401 leak fix (2026-04-16): Three small follow-ups on top of PR 2, each shipped straight to main. **Regression guard** — new `src/lib/analytics/events.test.ts` (11 cases, one per tracker) mocks `posthog.capture`, calls each `trackX` with a valid payload, and asserts (a) correct event name, (b) no forbidden key from `{content, message, text, body, email, phone, user_id, display_name, reason, excerpt}`, (c) expected keys present. Strict equality on forbidden keys so existing `message_number` / `message_count` pass; if someone later adds a `content` or `excerpt` field to an event payload, CI fails before it reaches PostHog. Test count 442 → 453. **Init hardening** — `initPostHog()` now wraps `posthog.init()` in try/catch (if the library ever throws synchronously we warn once and leave `initialized=false`; events and pageviews silently don't fire, which is the correct failure mode for analytics) and adds `advanced_disable_decide: true` to skip the `/decide` endpoint, which powers feature flags we don't use and was otherwise logging a blocked-request error when `*.posthog.com` was blocked by an ad-blocker, network rule, or extension. Config-fetch failures on `/array/{key}/config.js` were already handled internally by posthog-js (captures batch to localStorage and retry). **startExploration 401 leak** — latent state leak at `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts:830` in the exploration flow's 401 branch: `router.push('/login')` was called without first clearing `isLoading`. Moot in practice (redirect unmounts the component) but the function-entry guard `if (isLoading || isStreaming) return false` would block re-entry if the leak ever mattered. Discovered during an audit of a "send button stuck disabled" report that Jeff flagged during mywalnut.app testing; the audit didn't find code evidence for the main symptom but surfaced this one real latent bug. One-line fix: `setIsLoading(false)` before the redirect. Build ✓, 453 tests ✓.
- PostHog analytics — Stage 1 events + permanent recording-off (2026-04-16, PR 2 of 2): Wires the event taxonomy on top of PR 1's plumbing and resolves the "replay recording" question with a permanent off. Mywalnut is a deep personal-disclosure product; recording user screens crosses a trust line that aggregate events never do. **Client config:** `disable_session_recording: true` is permanent (no longer the temporary fixup from PR 1), and the `session_recording` masking block was removed since it's unreachable with recording off. Belt-and-suspenders: project-level recording also disabled in PostHog Settings → Recordings so an accidental client config change still captures nothing. **Event taxonomy:** new `src/lib/analytics/events.ts` exports typed tracker functions for the Stage 1 set — `conversation_started`, `message_sent`, `conversation_ended`, `checkpoint_proposed/confirmed/rejected/refined`, `manual_viewed`, `manual_exported`, `entry_edited`, `session_started`. Every tracker's prop shape is tight: no content, content_length, rejection reason text, or other user-authored fields. `EntryPoint` union starts at `"situation"` (resonant-content + personal-upload literals added when those flows ship). **Instrumentation:** `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts` fires conversation_started when `conversationId` transitions null→value in finalizeMessage (and again in startExploration), message_sent for user+assistant after successful persistence (ordered chronologically, message_number snapshotted from messages.length at send time), checkpoint_proposed when setActiveCheckpoint runs (captures proposedAt ref), and checkpoint_confirmed/rejected/refined on button action carrying time_to_decision_ms. conversation_ended fires in startNewSession with end_type="natural" and duration_seconds from a conversationStartedAt ref. `src/components/MainApp.tsx` fires manual_viewed in a tab-watching useEffect with days_since_last_view derived from `mw_last_manual_view` localStorage (key updated after capture). `src/components/mobile/MobileManual.tsx`'s doExportAndShare fires manual_exported (format:"pdf", entry_count) after the PDF generates + shares successfully. `src/components/PostHogProvider.tsx` fires session_started after identify resolves, using `mw_last_session` localStorage for days_since_last_session + is_first_session; a ref guard blocks React Strict Mode double-invoke in dev. **Privacy policy:** new "Analytics" section in `src/app/privacy/page.tsx` naming PostHog, hashed user IDs, the explicit "we do not record your screen" line, and the Do Not Track opt-out; last-updated stamp bumped to 2026-04-16. `entry_edited` tracker exists but isn't called anywhere — there is no entry-edit UI yet. Verified locally: distinct_id stays the 16-char hex hash across session, session_started fires once (ref-guarded), manual_viewed fires on tab click, no `/s/` snapshots and no `posthog-recorder.js` load. Build ✓, 442 tests ✓.
- PostHog analytics foundation — plumbing only (2026-04-16, PR 1 of 2): PostHog browser SDK wired for the beta. **PR 1 scope is plumbing, not events** — identifies users, captures pageviews, no Stage 1 events yet. `posthog-js` + `posthog-node` installed. `src/lib/analytics/posthog-client.ts` inits with `autocapture: false` (so user-authored text from messages / manual entries cannot leak into event properties), `capture_pageview: false` (we capture manually with pathname only — no query strings, which could carry PII), `respect_dnt: true`, `persistence: "localStorage+cookie"`, and `disable_session_recording: true` as a temporary flag until PR 2 wires `data-ph-mask` on the chat + manual surfaces. `src/app/api/analytics/identity/route.ts` returns the current user's hashed id via the existing `hashUserId()` helper in `src/lib/observability/log.ts` and the `OBSERVABILITY_USER_ID_SALT` env var — reusing Track 4's hash keeps PostHog distinct_ids correlatable with `confirm_failures` rows without sending raw user_ids to a third party. Hash is 16 hex chars (~64 bits entropy), not 64. `src/components/PostHogProvider.tsx` inits once, calls `identify(hashedId)` on mount, and captures `$pageview` events with the pathname on every route change. The pageview tracker is wrapped in a Suspense boundary (via a nested `PageViewTracker` component) so `useSearchParams` at the root layout does not opt the whole app out of static rendering. Wired into `src/app/layout.tsx` — provider wraps `{children}` in the body. Two env vars added to Vercel: `NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST`. `OBSERVABILITY_USER_ID_SALT` remains unset in prod as of ship — the helper falls back to the shared dev salt `mywalnut-dev-salt-v1`; set a distinct prod salt to prevent prod/dev hash collisions. Verified locally in preview that PostHog init + identify + pageview capture all return 200, and after the `disable_session_recording` fixup the recorder script stops loading and no `/s/` snapshots post. Build ✓, 442 tests ✓.
- Design 2.0 — desktop paratext (masthead + colophon) (2026-04-16, step 3/4): Path A for the desktop canvas — commits the empty parchment field around the phone frame to an editorial composition. The phone is the reading surface, the canvas around it is paratext. `MobileLayout.tsx` gains `<header>` and `<footer>` siblings on the outer parchment canvas. Masthead: `my walnut` at 64px Instrument Serif, letter-spacing `-0.5px`, weight 400, color `--session-ink-soft`, absolute-positioned `top: 48px`. Same name and typographic treatment as the in-phone 20px wordmark, rendered larger — one name, one treatment, different scales. Lowercase held at scale with tight negative tracking so it reads intentional, not default; color `--ink-soft` keeps it a label, not a shout. Colophon: two Instrument Serif 18px statement sentences in `--ink-soft` ("A private manual, written by you and assembled in conversation. / Nothing enters it unless you confirm."), a 120px centered hairline, then a JetBrains Mono 12px mixed-case `--ink-mid` meta row ("In closed beta. v{APP_VERSION} · Privacy · Terms") using `var(--size-meta)` and flex-gap for separator spacing. Mixed-case is deliberate: app structural labels (nav tabs, JOVE label, Settings section headers) use uppercase mono as UI register; the colophon is editorial register, closer to a book colophon line. No hairlines around the phone frame — whitespace defines zones, not rules; the one rule inside the colophon marks where editorial statement meets structural metadata. Phone's `maxHeight` on the canvas recalibrated from a flat `932px` to `min(932px, 100dvh - clamp(0px, (100vw - 430px) * 999, 400px))` — reserves 400px of vertical space for paratext at desktop widths while leaving mobile's full-viewport behavior untouched (clamp resolves to 0 at ≤430px). Two new CSS classes in `globals.css`: `.mw-desktop-paratext` (display: none on mobile, block at ≥431px) and its inverse `.mw-mobile-only` (hides at ≥431px, used on the EntryScreen's in-phone Privacy/Terms footer so the canvas colophon's legal links aren't duplicated and the phone doesn't read as a self-contained document competing with the publication it's inside). `APP_VERSION` imported from `src/lib/version.ts` for the meta row. The "In closed beta." string has an EDITORIAL COPY comment directly above it flagging it as intentional publication metadata (e.g. future "First edition, 2026") rather than a forgotten flag — deliberate string edit at beta exit, not an automated transition. Verified at 1280×900, 1440×900, and 375×812 viewports. On mobile the paratext hides cleanly and the phone fills the viewport exactly as before. Build ✓, 442 tests ✓.
- Observability + integration tests — closes hardening plan (2026-04-17, Tracks 4 & 5): Two tracks shipped together to close the checkpoint hardening plan. **Track 4 (observability):** new migration `20260417000004_confirm_failures.sql` creates `confirm_failures` table (user_id, message_id, conversation_id, error_kind, error_detail, status_code, duration_ms, created_at) with RLS enabled and three indexes (created_at DESC, user_id, error_kind+created_at). Two SECURITY DEFINER RPCs: `admin_confirm_stats(p_window_seconds)` returns failure counts grouped by kind within the window; `admin_confirm_recent_failures(p_limit)` returns recent feed. New `src/lib/observability/log.ts` with `logEvent()` JSON line emitter (dev mode pretty-prints, prod is single-line JSON for Vercel logs) and `hashUserId()` using Web Crypto SHA-256 with fixed salt from `OBSERVABILITY_USER_ID_SALT` env var (falls back to dev salt). Fire-and-forget `recordConfirmFailure()` in `src/lib/observability/record-failure.ts` awaits the DB write but never throws. `/api/checkpoint/confirm/route.ts` fully instrumented: `confirm_attempt` at start, `confirm_rpc_ok`/`confirm_rpc_fail`, `confirm_stream_started`, `confirm_outcome` on every terminal path (success, idempotent, 4xx, 5xx, rate-limit, unauthorized). Every 4xx/5xx also writes a `confirm_failures` row. New `/api/admin/confirm-stats` API (nodejs runtime, admin-gated) and `ConfirmHealthPanel.tsx` component rendered below `SchemaHealthTab` in `/admin?section=health`: window selector (1h/24h/7d), ✓/⚠ banner with failure count + kind breakdown, last-20-failures feed. 5 new `hashUserId` tests (determinism, output shape, no-leak). **Track 5 (integration tests):** new `vitest.e2e.config.ts` (separate config, fileParallelism:false, 30s test timeout, excluded from unit suite) and `npm run test:e2e` script. `src/db-contract.e2e.test.ts` is the drift canary — attempts real inserts into every table the code writes to (manual_entries full + minimal shapes, messages assistant + system, conversations update, profiles persona_mode, safety_events crisis path, confirm_failures, beta_feedback). Would have caught yesterday's `type` column drift. `src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.e2e.test.ts` exercises the actual `confirm_checkpoint_write` RPC — happy path, idempotent repeat, concurrent invocations (exactly one row), checkpoint_not_found, checkpoint_not_pending. New `.github/workflows/supabase-e2e.yml` spins up local Supabase (Docker on Ubuntu runner) via `supabase start`, applies migrations, extracts creds, runs `npm run test:e2e` on every PR and main push. Build ✓, 442 unit tests ✓ (was 437, +5). E2E suite runs in CI only — local E2E requires Docker Desktop which the owner hasn't installed.
- Client resilience for checkpoint confirm (2026-04-17, Track 3 of hardening plan): Checkpoint confirm now auto-retries transient failures and surfaces specific, actionable error messages instead of the generic "Something went wrong." `useChat.ts:confirmCheckpoint` wraps the fetch in a retry loop: max 3 attempts, backoffs 500ms / 2s between attempts, 30s per-attempt timeout via `AbortController`. Retries on network rejection or 5xx. Does NOT retry on 4xx (404 gone, 400 rejected-already, 403 forbidden) or 429 (rate-limited) — those are surfaced with specific messages. 401 short-circuits to `/login`. Server-side idempotency from Track 2 means retries never produce duplicates. Mid-stream failures AFTER a successful write no longer surface as errors — the atomic RPC guarantees the DB is consistent, so `streamFromResponse` exceptions just log and proceed to `loadManual()`. New `confirmErrorMessage(status, networkFailed)` helper exported from `useChat.ts` covers the full taxonomy (network, 400, 404, 429, 5xx, generic fallback). 22 new tests in `src/lib/hooks/confirm-error-message.test.ts` including a message-quality pass that checks every status returns a non-empty sanitized sentence (no "undefined" leakage, ends with punctuation). SW audit (`docs/reference/sw-audit.md`) confirmed `/api/*` is network-only in `public/sw.js:46`, no SW intervention possible on API calls — yesterday's `(failed) net::ERR_` row on `sw.js:69` was the navigation offline fallback, unrelated to checkpoint confirm. Build ✓, 437 tests ✓.
- Idempotent + transactional checkpoint confirm (2026-04-17, Track 2 of hardening plan): `/api/checkpoint/confirm` is now safe to call any number of times with the same messageId — no duplicate writes, no "already resolved" 500s. New migration `20260417000003_confirm_idempotency.sql` adds: (a) partial unique index `manual_entries_user_source_msg_uniq` on `(user_id, source_message_id) WHERE source_message_id IS NOT NULL` — prevents duplicate rows for the same checkpoint, doesn't constrain populate-shaped null-source rows; (b) `confirm_checkpoint_write()` SECURITY DEFINER function that runs the three writes (insert manual_entries, update message status, insert system message) atomically with `FOR UPDATE` locking on the message row. Returns `(entry_id, was_already_confirmed)`. Raises `P0002 checkpoint_not_found` or `P0001 checkpoint_not_pending` for specific error states. `confirm-checkpoint.ts` rewritten to call the RPC — TS still handles composed-field fallbacks (composed_content → message.content stripped of crisis resources, composed_name → meta.name → "Untitled", derived summary from first sentence) but delegates the write. Route handler maps RPC errors to precise HTTP statuses (404 for not-found, 400 for rejected/refined, 500 for other), and returns a short JSON ack (not SSE stream) when `wasAlreadyConfirmed=true` so the follow-up doesn't duplicate. Client `useChat.ts:confirmCheckpoint` detects content-type and skips stream parsing on JSON ack. 10 new tests (5 in confirm-checkpoint.test.ts covering composed field handling + RPC error mapping + idempotent return, 5 in route.test.ts covering error-to-status mapping + JSON-vs-SSE response shape). Build ✓, 415 tests ✓.
- Schema Health admin panel + CI for migrations (2026-04-17, closes Track 1): New `/admin?section=health` panel surfaces migration state — applied in prod (queried via new `admin_list_migrations()` SECURITY DEFINER function in migration `20260417000002_admin_list_migrations.sql` that wraps `supabase_migrations.schema_migrations`), files on disk (via `fs.readdir` on `supabase/migrations/`), and the diff between them. Banner reports "✓ In sync" or "⚠ Drift detected" with specific missing-file / missing-applied lists. Table view shows every version with ✓/— for on-disk and in-prod columns. New `/api/admin/migration-status` route (nodejs runtime, admin-gated, dynamic). `SchemaHealthTab.tsx` component. Also shipped `.github/workflows/supabase-migrations.yml`: on PR touching `supabase/migrations/**` it prints the migration diff in the check summary; on push to main it runs `supabase db push` to apply. Concurrency group serializes rapid merges. Two repo secrets required: `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN` and `SUPABASE_DB_PASSWORD` (both set). This makes today's first CLI-driven migration (`20260417000002`) the reference for the flow going forward.
- Migration tooling + baseline squash (2026-04-17, Track 1 of hardening plan): Supabase CLI linked to prod project (`nkmperzwcmttdkxwhbiv`). Pre-flight audit ran on prod: RLS confirmed on all 13 public tables, every code-expected column present, zero orphan `manual_entries`. Generated `20260417000000_squash_baseline.sql` via `pg_dump` against prod — 1165 lines capturing all tables, indexes, functions, policies, grants, triggers. Deleted 12 pre-squash migration files and 5 loose SQL files (`supabase/schema.sql`, `add-extraction-snapshot.sql`, `add-feedback-table.sql`, `add-group-conversation-link.sql`, `admin-migration.sql`). Marked squash as applied in prod via `supabase migration repair`. Follow-up migration `20260417000001_fix_cleanup_function.sql` fixes a drift found in the squash — `cleanup_stale_anonymous_users()` was still referencing the pre-rename `manual_components` table; function body now points to `manual_entries`. Removed the defensive `extraction_snapshot` fallback in `call-persona.ts:343` (column is guaranteed present). Updated `docs/system.md` Migration Protocols section to mandate CLI-driven migrations (dashboard SQL editor now read-only). `scripts/test-phase1-gate.sh` reference to deleted `schema.sql` updated. Remaining Track 1 follow-ups: CI `supabase db push` on merge, admin Schema Health panel. Build ✓, 405 tests ✓.
- Design 2.0 — Tailwind token projection + mobile nav/input cramping fix (2026-04-16): Two-step Design 2.0 slice shipped together. **Step 1 (tokens):** projects existing `--session-*` CSS variables from `globals.css` into `tailwind.config.ts` under `theme.extend.colors`, `fontFamily`, `fontSize`. Utility classes like `bg-linen`, `text-ink-soft`, `font-persona` now available. No new tokens introduced — every value points at an existing var. `font-sans`/`font-serif`/`font-mono` override Tailwind defaults to resolve to DM Sans / Instrument Serif / JetBrains Mono; `font-persona` exposes Source Serif 4 (Jove's voice). `text-ink` resolves to `--session-ink` (darkest) because grep confirmed it's the body default across ~57 `color:` uses versus ≤4 for softer variants. No components migrated in this pass — utility surface is available for new work and opportunistic edits; ADR-009 (inline styles) policy unchanged. Spacing, borderRadius, boxShadow, and animation deliberately NOT projected — each requires a design decision due in later Design 2.0 steps. Two naming quirks accepted as tech debt (`border-persona-border`, `text-error-text`) — logged for a later token-hygiene pass. **Step 2 (cramping fix):** recovers ~14–18px of chat feed height on mobile without architectural change. `MobileNav.tsx`: face `--font-serif` → `--font-mono`, size 12px → 11px, tracking 1.5px → 1.8px, padding 14/20 → 10/14 above safe-area, new `border-top: 1px solid var(--session-ink-hairline)` as a structural floor. Active tab color `--session-ink-soft` → `--session-ink` (darkest); inactive stays `--ink-mid` for accessibility — avoided `--ink-ghost` because 4:1 at 11px fails for dyslexic users in low-light conditions. `ChatInput.tsx`: outer padding `8 20 4` → `12 20 8` so input reads as a dedicated writing surface on linen, not utility chrome. Three-tab `main` paddingBottom reservation 68px → 52px across Session/Manual/Settings for consistent frame. Three tabs, absolute positioning, and phone-frame all retained — Manual is the product's output, not incidental navigation, so the decision to demote mid-beta was rejected. Known sub-44px tap-target debt on MobileNav scheduled for a dedicated accessibility pass (ADR-033). See decisions.md ADR-032, ADR-033, ADR-034. Build ✓, 405 tests ✓.
- Dev test tools — stop auto-confirming, stop wiping real data (2026-04-16): Two related footguns fixed. `dev-simulate` used to auto-call `confirmCheckpoint()` inside its loop, flipping the checkpoint's `status` server-side to `"confirmed"` before the user ever touched the UI — so manual clicks on the same card then hit the `status !== "pending"` guard in `confirm-checkpoint.ts` and returned 500 "Checkpoint already resolved." Now dev-simulate generates a single checkpoint and exits with it left in `"pending"`, so the real `/api/checkpoint/confirm` path drives the rest (and can actually be tested). Separately, both `dev-simulate` and `dev-populate` wiped all of a user's `manual_entries` (and dev-simulate also wiped `manual_changelog`, all conversations, and all messages) before running, destroying real test data on every click. dev-simulate no longer wipes anything — it creates a fresh conversation alongside existing data; use `/api/dev-reset` for clean-slate. dev-populate now narrows its delete to populate-shaped rows only (`name IS NULL`), preserving real confirm-generated entries which always have a composed name. `checkpointTarget` param still accepted by dev-simulate for backwards compat but ignored (loop exits at first checkpoint regardless). Removed now-unused `confirmCheckpoint` + `parseCheckpointIntent` imports. Build ✓, 405 tests ✓.
- Checkpoint confirm bug fix — DB schema sync (2026-04-16): User-visible fix for "Something went wrong saving that. Try again." on every checkpoint confirm. Root cause was three separate drift points between `supabase/migrations/` and the production DB — none of which had been run: (a) the `sage_*` → `persona_*` rename (`20260414_rename_sage_to_persona.sql`) — code selected `persona_mode` but DB still had `sage_mode`; (b) the `safety_events` table itself was never created from the baseline schema, so crisis writes had been silently failing against a ghost table; (c) the `type` column on `manual_entries` was never dropped (`20260407_drop_pattern_type.sql` targeted the old `manual_components` name and was orphaned by the later table rename), so every `confirm-checkpoint.ts` insert hit a NOT NULL constraint on a column the code no longer provides. Ran the rename in the dashboard. Two new idempotent retry migrations: `20260416_create_safety_events.sql` creates the missing table with the already-renamed column (`persona_included_988`), FKs to `conversations` + `profiles`, RLS enabled without policies (service-role writes only), helper indexes on `user_id` and `created_at`; `20260416_drop_manual_entries_type.sql` retries the column drop against the current `manual_entries` name and also cleans `manual_changelog.type`. Verified end-to-end: devtest user confirms a checkpoint → row with non-null `name` + `source_message_id` + Jove-composed content lands in DB → Manual tab renders it after the follow-up stream completes. Follow-up noted: `dev-populate/route.ts` wipes ALL of a user's manual_entries before reseeding (line 64), which masked the confirm bug during testing and is a footgun if ever run against a non-devtest admin — track for later narrowing to populate-shaped rows only.
- A11y + design hardening (2026-04-15): BetaFeedbackButton redesigned — icon replaced with "feedback" pill label; popover gains heading, subtitle, 6-row textarea, dedicated error/retry state, 2s auto-close on success. Persona token darkened (`--session-persona` #6B7A4F → #556B48, 5.53:1 on cream, passes WCAG AA); primary CTA buttons remain sage green. Global `:focus-visible` added to `globals.css` — cream+sage box-shadow ring on all focusable elements, beats inline `outline: none` via `!important`. `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` guard neutralises all transitions/animations. Full ARIA pass across 19 files: `MobileNav` is `role="tablist"` with `aria-selected`/`aria-controls`/roving `tabIndex`; `MobileLayout` panels are `role="tabpanel"` with `aria-labelledby`; chat scroll container is `role="log"` `aria-live="polite"`; 7 modals/drawers gain `role="dialog"` `aria-modal`; icon-only buttons (hamburger, drawer close, send/mic) get `aria-label`; `SectionHeader`/`EntryItem` toggles get `aria-expanded`; `autoComplete`+`inputMode` on all email/password/phone/OTP inputs; `<main>` landmarks and `<h1>` headings on all onboarding + mobile screens. Body-copy contrast: 11 `ink-ghost` usages migrated to `ink-mid` across session, drawer, manual, settings, admin for WCAG AA. Design audit docs (`design-audit.md`, `design-audit-v2.md`) added at repo root for reference — not user-facing.
- Checkpoint experience hardening — Prompt C (2026-04-15): Three progress signals Jove delivers through the arc — EARLY FRAME (turns 3-4, new users only), DEPTH BUILDING (turns 5-7), CHECKPOINT APPROACHING (gate close). Each is tracked via a latching flag on `ExtractionState` (`early_frame_delivered`, `depth_signal_delivered`, `approaching_signal_delivered`); the extractor both triggers signals in `sage_brief` and detects delivery from the assistant's last message. Approaching signal is the only one that resets on thread shift. First-checkpoint educational wrapper moved out of the checkpoint and onto the approaching signal 1-2 turns *before* — so the user is primed before they see the reflection. Transition copy: "Something's taken shape from what you've told me" is gone; the only line is "I want to put something in your Manual." POST-CHECKPOINT rewritten as three explicit steps: confirm + name the layer structure, name the open thread, plant the return hook. Composition (`confirm-checkpoint.ts`) tightened: 80-150 word cap (was 80-300), NOT A RECAP rule, EXPLICIT BIND required, second exemplar. `validateComposedEntry` upper bound 300 → 150 to match. Tier 2 section renamed `PROGRESS SIGNALS` → `PACING` so the new Tier 3 block can own the name.
- Three-tier Jove prompt + no post-checkpoint fork + observation miss tracking (2026-04-15): Restructured `system-prompt.ts` into Tier 1 (constitutional) / Tier 2 (voice and behavior) / Tier 3 (conversation mechanics). `voice-autistic.ts` is the sole source for voice rules, banned phrases, and examples. Removed the post-checkpoint "Work with it / Keep building" fork — Jove now acknowledges briefly and returns to the conversation from whatever the user surfaces next; no scripted menu. Added `observation_miss_count` to `ExtractionState`: extractor increments on misses, resets on confirmations; the brief injects a grounding nudge at ≥2 and a full reset at ≥3. Rewrote `system-prompt.test.ts` for the tier structure and added miss-count injection tests in `extraction.test.ts`.
- Manual context compression + extraction window 6→12 (2026-04-15): New module `src/lib/persona/manual-context.ts` exposes `compressManualEntry()` + `prepareManualContext()`. Recent entries (current conversation + backfill up to 4 total) render in full; older entries render as one line: `[Layer N — LayerName] "Headline" — summary. Key words: w1, w2, w3.` — keeps the context budget bounded as returning users accumulate entries. Group-chat prompt path opts out (`currentConversationId=null`). Added `summary` (text) and `key_words` (text[]) columns to `manual_entries` via migration `20260415_add_manual_entry_compression_fields.sql`. `confirm-checkpoint.ts` composition prompt emits both fields in its JSON response; runtime fallback derives summary from the first sentence of content when absent (backfill-free). `loadConversationContext` now joins `source_message_id` → `conversation_id` so current-session entries can be distinguished. Extraction window: `EXTRACTION_MESSAGE_WINDOW = 12` constant; extractor slice widened from 6 to 12 messages so mechanisms developing across several turns get captured.
- Manual terminology unification (2026-04-15): Cosmetic follow-up to the `manual_entries` table rename. Brings code, prompts, docs, and admin UI in line with the canonical vocabulary (Manual / Layer / Entry / Checkpoint). TS type `ManualComponent` → `ManualEntry` across 11 files. UI `Thread`/`threads` → `Entry`/`entries` in `layer-definitions.ts`, `PopulatedLayer.tsx`, `MobileManual.tsx`, `AdminManualView.tsx`, `generate-manual-pdf.ts`. State and prop renames: `confirmedComponents` → `confirmedEntries` (`useChat`/`MainApp`/`MobileSession`), `components` prop → `entries` (`MobileManual`, `AdminManualView`, callers in `MainApp` and `UserProfilePane`). File rename: `PatternItem.tsx` → `EntryItem.tsx` (export + interface `ThreadCardProps` → `EntryCardProps`). Prompt strings: "confirmed components" → "confirmed entries" in `system-prompt.ts`; "behavioral model(s)" → "Manual(s)" in `classifier.ts`, `confirm-checkpoint.ts`, `extraction.ts`, `simulate-user.ts` (kept the legal AI-disclosure phrase in `rules.md` intact). Doc fixes in `system.md`. Admin user-list display: "manual component(s)" → "manual entr(y/ies)". DB column names and API response field names (e.g. `component_count`) intentionally left alone — public contract.
- `/feedback` slash command removed (2026-04-15): The in-chat slash command (intercepted in `useChat.ts` before reaching Jove) is gone. Users now submit feedback exclusively through the persistent `BetaFeedbackButton` (writes to `beta_feedback`). Removed: `/api/feedback` POST route, `FeedbackModal` in `MobileSession.tsx`, `feedbackHint` + `showFeedbackModal` state and prop plumbing through `useChat` → `MainApp` → `MobileSession`, the instructional paragraph in `MobileSettings.tsx`. The legacy `feedback` table is retained as read-only history; admin can still view existing entries via `/api/admin/feedback`.
- Manual table rename `manual_components` → `manual_entries` (2026-04-15): Aligns the DB and code with the canonical vocabulary (Manual / Layer / Entry / Checkpoint). Table renamed by hand in the Supabase dashboard; migration `20260415_rename_manual_components_to_entries.sql` codifies the change with idempotent guards. Two RLS policies renamed (`Users can {create,update} own manual components` → `... entries`); third policy already used "manual" alone and was left. Trigger name `update_manual_components_updated_at` left in place — internal label, repoints to the renamed table automatically. All 14 `.from("manual_components")` call sites updated to `manual_entries`. Pure rename — no schema or logic changes.
- Typography minimums + WCAG AA contrast floor (2026-04-15): Darkened `--session-ink-ghost` (#B8B2AC → #756E6A, ~5.5:1 on linen) and `--session-ink-faded` (#8A8480 → #5E5855, ~7:1) so all secondary/tertiary text passes WCAG AA. Added size tokens `--size-meta` (12px), `--size-body` (15px), `--size-prose` (17px), `--size-heading` (22px). `docs/rules.md` typography table raised — mono 12–13px (was 7–9px), sans 14–15px (was 13px), serif 17–22px. 12px is now the stated minimum text size anywhere in the product. `--session-ink-whisper` marked decorative-only. Inline-styles-only rule nudged toward size tokens.
- Sub-12px fontSize migration (2026-04-15): 88 inline `fontSize` values across 16 files (admin pages, admin panels, MobileSession, MobileSettings, MobileNav, SessionDrawer, SettingsRow, BetaFeedbackButton, etc.) bumped from 7–11px to `var(--size-meta)` (12px). Brings all text up to the new minimum stated in `docs/rules.md`. One unicode arrow glyph (`▸`/`▾` in MobileSettings) intentionally preserved at original size — not text.
- Admin docs page (2026-04-15): `/admin/docs` renders the five canonical docs (intent, system, rules, state, decisions) from disk via `/api/admin/docs` and `/api/admin/docs/[name]` (admin-guarded, node runtime, `fs.readFile` + `fs.stat`). Left sidebar ordered intent → system → rules → state → decisions with last-modified date; main panel markdown-renders with `react-markdown` + `remark-gfm`; staleness banner on state.md when mtime > 7 days. Extra "docs mapping" entry embeds `public/admin/docs-mapping.html` via iframe. Admin rail gains a "Docs" entry linking from `/admin`. Read-only — no editing, search, or versioning.
- intent.md canonical rewrite (2026-04-15): Replaced with April 2026 product strategy (hypothesis, how it works, Manual structure, beta scope w/ 2026-04-28 ship, WS1–WS7 workstreams). CLAUDE.md Documentation section now has per-doc one-line descriptions; added hard rules for browser test login (`devtest@test.com` / `testtest`) and the never-run-`npm run build`-while-preview-dev-server-is-running cache corruption.
- Wordmark "MYWALNUT" → "my walnut" (2026-04-15): Lowercase, letter-spacing 15px → 4px, removed `textTransform: uppercase` across MobileSession, MobileManual, reset-password, waitlist, EntryScreen, InfoScreens, LoginScreen, SeedScreen.
- Admin dashboard → `/admin` route (2026-04-14): Promoted admin out of the mobile settings overlay into a dedicated desktop-first route. Persistent left rail (Users / Beta / Feedback) with URL-driven section via `?section=`. Users section is master-detail: 320px user list + inline `UserProfilePane` with sessions/manual/feedback tabs — no more page-jumping when selecting a user. Beta section merges waitlist + allowlist under sub-tabs. Feedback section combines beta feedback (with unread badge on rail) and in-app feedback. Responsive: rail collapses to a horizontal tab strip under 720px. Internals: new `useAdminData` hook centralizes state + fetchers; extracted `UserProfilePane`, `ExtractionPanel`, `AdminManualView`; `src/components/mobile/admin/` → `src/components/admin/`; deleted the 1432-line `src/components/mobile/AdminView.tsx` overlay. `MobileSettings` now links to `/admin` instead of embedding the view.
- Persona rename Sage → Jove (2026-04-14): `PERSONA_NAME` / `PERSONA_NAME_FORMAL` flipped in `src/lib/persona/config.ts`. Abstraction held — system prompt, summary transcript labels, error strings, checkpoint prompts, simulation prompts, SMS templating, and group-gate direct-address regex all pick up the new name automatically. Hand-touched untemplated literals: `public/persona-contact.vcf` FN field, `public/offline.html` brand copy, `public/narrative/*.html` (4 investor/demo pages — visible text flipped, CSS class tokens like `.bubble-sage` / `--sage` preserved). Value-bound test assertions in `system-prompt.test.ts` and `generate-summary.test.ts` updated. `group-gate.ts` keeps `|sage` alternation as a transition fallback so old iMessage groups using "sage" as direct address still route correctly. `APP_VERSION` 2.10.0 → 2.11.0, `PERSONA_VERSION` 2.4.0 → 2.5.0 (Anthropic prompt cache invalidates on first call after deploy). Deferred: `sage_brief` JSONB key in extraction state — needs its own dual-read migration.
- Narrative pages brand fix (2026-04-14): `public/narrative/mantle_intro.html` → `mywalnut_intro.html`, access code `understandingwmantle26` → `understandingwmywalnut26`, 15 Mantle brand references flipped across `index.html` and the intro page. Old preview links and old access code will 404 / reject (small audience, acceptable break).

**Pre-2026-04-15 history** (compressed; full prose preserved in git log):

- **Brand identity (2026-04-09 → 2026-04-15):** Mantle → mywalnut migration across 52 files + DB column `mantle_user_id` → `owner_user_id` + localStorage `mantle_*` → `mw_*` (2026-04-09). Persona Sage → Jove flip in `PERSONA_NAME` (2026-04-14). Internal `src/lib/sage/` → `src/lib/persona/` directory + `sage_*` DB columns renamed via migration `20260414_rename_sage_to_persona.sql` (2026-04-14).
- **ND pivot (2026-04-06, 4 PRs):** Layer rename to autism-specific (SSOT at `src/lib/manual/layers.ts`). Jove voice rewrite (`voice-autistic.ts`: somatic-first, sensory-verbatim, clinical framework ban). Checkpoint composition requires somatic anchor. Onboarding/legal/marketing copy rewritten for ND audience. `profiles.sage_mode` column (later renamed to `persona_mode`) for future voice modes.
- **Jove prompt hardening + tuning (2026-03-17 → 2026-04-07):** Sensitive logic moved server-side (`validateMaterialQuality` pre-emit, `validateComposedEntry` post). ~75 lines of enforcement scaffolding pruned from the prompt. Seven prior rounds of tuning landed the abstract-stacking rule, receive-land-ask rhythm, checkpoint delivery sequence, post-checkpoint advisory mode, one-question-per-turn enforcement, therapy-ism bans.
- **Security hardening (2026-04-07):** Upstash rate limiting on chat/summary/checkpoint/OTP. Anon Gate B at 2 manual_entries. OTP phone verification (SHA-256, 6-digit, 10-min TTL). PII logging cleanup.
- **Manual pipeline shape (2026-04-07):** Pattern feature removed — single entry type per layer, no per-layer cap, no recurrence gate. `manual_components.type` column dropped (table later renamed `manual_entries` on 2026-04-15).
- **Legal + closed-beta gates (2026-04-06 → 2026-04-14):** Privacy/Terms updated (provider-agnostic SMS, "not used to train AI"). Entry screen + post-login onboarding gate (2026-04-08, migration `20260408_add_onboarding_completed_at.sql`). Allowlist-gated signup, waitlist page, admin allowlist tab. Tables: `beta_allowlist`, `waitlist`, `beta_feedback`.
- **Channel expansion (2026-03-31 → 2026-04-03):** Text Jove via Linq (1:1 since deprecated; ported to Sendblue per ADR-035). Session drawer text channel. Narrative pages (investor/demo, password-gated). Group chat Jove with facilitator mode + scoring-based gate (still live for groups).
- **Pre-rebrand UI baseline (2026-03-31 → 2026-04-09):** Typography (Source Serif 4 / DM Sans), session opening states, sign-in nudge, manual share/export, original flat Manual page. All of these have since been redesigned in the May 2026 typography + Manual + chat passes — see chronological entries above. Listed here for chronological completeness only.

## Not Yet Functional
*Last verified: 2026-05-07*

- **Multi-user group conversations**: Groups with 2+ mywalnut users get neutral intro but can't create conversation records (user_id NOT NULL constraint). Facilitator mode only works for single-mywalnut-user groups.

## Known Issues
*Last verified: 2026-06-02*

- **Auth token expiry**: No explicit token refresh on the client. Relies on middleware `getUser()` per page request. Long SPA sessions could expire. API routes return 401 → redirect to /login.
- **Ghost conversation rows**: Largely closed 2026-05-25 by the `X-Conversation-Id` response header + server-side user-message dedup (see ship log). The retry-storm path (first-send-fails → client retries with `conversationId: null` → server creates new conversation row) is fixed. The original race-condition path (user sends second message before the first's `conversationId` has propagated to state) is still theoretically possible but stays mitigated by the ref guard. Existing ghost rows from the 2026-05-25 incident remain in the DB (harmless, 0-message rows); cleanup SQL deferred.
- **OAuth redirect config**: Supabase dashboard Redirect URLs allowlist must contain callback URL for every environment. Defensive `?code=` forward in middleware recovers from allowlist drift.
- **Upstash provisioned 2026-06-02**: `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL` + `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_TOKEN` added to `.env.local` and Vercel. Ping confirmed live (`{"result":"PONG"}`). Rate limiting is now enforced in production — the hard-fail at module load is the correct posture and will surface immediately if credentials ever lapse.

- **Migrations applied 2026-06-01** (CI `supabase db push` green): the waitlist anon-INSERT lockdown (`20260601000000`) and the `phone_numbers` `UNIQUE(user_id)` constraint (`20260601000001`) are both live. The phone constraint already existed in the live DB as drift (a plain `ADD CONSTRAINT` hit `42P07`), so the migration was made idempotent (`a67b232`) and the migration history now matches the DB.

## In-Flight Work
*Last verified: 2026-06-01*

- **Two-layer engine plan** ([docs/reference/two-layer-engine-evaluation.md](reference/two-layer-engine-evaluation.md), proposed ADR-044 at [docs/reference/two-layer-engine-adr-draft.md](reference/two-layer-engine-adr-draft.md)). Six-phase plan to replace the single-overloaded Jove call with monitor + tracker + selector + stripped generator. **Phase 0 shipped 2026-05-21** — shadow monitor on every web turn, writing structured alliance reads to `monitor_reads`. Currently log-only; running with **Opus (`claude-opus-4-7`)** as a ceiling test of whether the signal is detectable at all. Validation now has TWO instruments: (a) live shadow traffic accumulating in `monitor_reads`, and (b) the `/replay-monitor` skill for eyeballing the harness against handcrafted or scrubbed transcripts on demand. **Phase 0 closed 2026-05-27 — verdict: promote** (see [docs/architecture/phase-0-closeout.md](architecture/phase-0-closeout.md)). Six replay runs (five real transcripts + one discounted synthetic) validated the monitor's `direction` (slope) read as load-bearing — it caught the documented sinking-user failure on real data — and `rupture` as validated with a one-turn recovery lag (a calibration finding for selector design, not a blocker). `scope` (the decision-seeking pull) is observed-but-not-validated; a focused scope-drift replay run is the gate condition before the scope-exit row gates anything. **Lock 1 shipped 2026-05-27** (the composer charged-material input gate; ADR-043) — the first safety unit off the post-Phase-0 build order, running in parallel with the selector work, NOT the scope-exit gate itself. **Its upstream half shipped 2026-05-28** — `deriveCheckpointApproaching` (the decision that loads CHECKPOINTS instructions) now requires charged material on the signal-ready layer and no active crisis before priming a checkpoint, so the merged-gate principle is enforced at both thresholds (firing + instruction-loading); see ADR-043's second implementation note. **Still shadow-only / not started:** nothing yet reads `monitor_reads` back into the pipeline; the deterministic pre-prompt selector (Decision 4) and the save-off scope-exit gate (the two-layer plan's Phase 1) are not built. Remaining build order: Contract opener content, Reflect-as-default selector, then safety-spine integration. The full component map and build order live in [docs/architecture/master.md](architecture/master.md).
- Dark-mode redesign is fully shipped — all 9 phases plus follow-ups. See the Deployed Features entry.
- Light-mode redesign is fully shipped — all 8 phases. See the Deployed Features entry.
- **Checkpoint hardening plan** ([docs/reference/checkpoint-hardening-plan.md](reference/checkpoint-hardening-plan.md)) — substantially shipped as of 2026-05-15. Track 1 (Supabase CLI migrations + drift detection): shipped, post-squash baseline at `20260417000000_squash_baseline.sql` plus per-feature migrations. Track 2 (idempotent confirm RPC + unique constraint + transactional writes): shipped via `confirm_checkpoint_write` + `manual_entries_user_source_msg_uniq` partial unique index. Track 3 (client-side resilience): shipped — 3-attempt retry with backoff, error taxonomy, stream-interruption recovery via `loadManual()`. Track 4 (structured logs + `confirm_failures` table + admin health panel): shipped, panel lives at `/admin?section=health` → ConfirmHealthPanel. Track 5 (E2E integration test + schema drift canary): shipped, `.github/workflows/supabase-e2e.yml` runs against a Dockerized Supabase on every PR. Track 6 (Upstash + enforced rate limits): code shipped, rate-limit module hard-fails in production if env vars missing; Upstash provisioned 2026-06-02 — rate limiting now fully enforced in prod. 2026-05-15 follow-up closed the three remaining gaps surfaced by the beta audit — reject/refine/defer idempotency, rate-limit prod hard-fail, stream outcome log timing. See Deployed Features for the audit + fixes entry.
- **Retire `beta_allowlist` table** — the 2026-06-02 beta-access unification (commit `4844375`) moved the auth gate onto `waitlist.status='invited'` and backfilled the table, but left `beta_allowlist` physically in place as a rollback net. Follow-up: once prod login is confirmed working on the new gate, land a migration dropping `public.beta_allowlist`. No code reads it anymore. (Optional pre-drop check the reviewer flagged: dry-run the unify migration against a prod snapshot to confirm no legacy allowlist row predates the lowercase/trim CHECK.)
- PWA Phase 3 pending: standalone polish, auth flow testing, splash screens — needs device QA
- Beta recruitment: target 10 late-diagnosed autistic adults, ages 25-45
- Linq deprecation: dual-provider architecture shipped 2026-04-16 — 1:1 text on Sendblue, group facilitator on Linq. Future work: port group facilitator once participant-diff logic is rebuilt around per-message `participants[]` arrays. See ADR-035.
